### [1] [A Hybrid Physics-Informed Neural Network Framework for Subcritical and Supercritical Dynamics in Multi-Species Chemotaxis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24949)
*Hamid El Bahja,Jan C. Riedel,Peter Jung*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对两物种趋化系统，在亚临界区使用标准连续时间PINN，在超临界区引入结合后向欧拉时间步进、对数变换和残差自适应配点策略的离散时间PINN，有效解决了从光滑解到有限时间爆破解的数值模拟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准连续时间PINN在超临界区无法鲁棒地解析高度局部化结构和陡峭梯度（与爆裂相关），需要更稳健的数值方法。

Method: 亚临界区采用标准连续时间PINN与交替训练；超临界区引入离散时间PINN（后向欧拉时间步进），结合对数变换稳定大解值，以及残差自适应配点策略在活跃区域加密训练点。两种方法混合使用，根据动力学状态切换。

Result: 连续时间PINN准确解析亚临界动力学；离散时间PINN可靠捕获超临界区中的奇异聚集和有限时间爆裂。

Conclusion: 提出的混合PINN框架能根据趋化系统的动力学状态自适应选择简单经济的连续时间公式或鲁棒的离散时间公式，有效处理从光滑解到爆裂的全范围行为。

Abstract: We study a two-species chemotaxis system that exhibits two qualitatively different regimes: subcritical dynamics, in which solutions remain smooth, and supercritical dynamics, in which strong aggregation may lead to finite-time blow-up. In the subcritical regime, we use a standard continuous-time physics-informed neural network (PINN) with alternating training for the coupled fields and show that it provides accurate and efficient approximations. In the supercritical regime, however, this formulation is not sufficiently robust to resolve the highly localized structures and steep gradients associated with blow-up. To address this limitation, we introduce a discrete-time PINN based on backward Euler time stepping, combined with a logarithmic transformation to stabilize large solution values and a residual-adaptive collocation strategy that concentrates training points near regions of high activity. This hybrid framework allows us to use a simple and computationally inexpensive PINN when the solution is smooth, while switching to a more robust discrete formulation when singular dynamics emerge. Numerical experiments confirm that the continuous-time formulation accurately resolves subcritical dynamics, while the discrete-time formulation reliably captures singular aggregation and finite-time blow-up in the supercritical regime.

</details>


### [2] [On residual bounds, backward shadowing stability of the Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition solution to the eigenvalue problem for the Koopman operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25086)
*Zlatko Drmač*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于影子理论的向后稳定性评估方法，用于Koopman算子框架下离散动力系统的数据驱动计算分析，将计算特征对的残差聚合为算子的向后扰动，并证明计算近似对应于系统的伪轨迹，最终被真实轨迹所影子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据驱动的动力系统分析中（特别是使用Koopman算子和EDMD时），计算得到的近似特征对存在数值误差，且数据常受噪声污染，需要一种系统性的方法来评估这些计算结果的可靠性。

Method: 将计算特征对的残差聚合为Koopman算子的向后扰动，使计算特征对成为扰动算子的精确特征对；再将算子扰动反推至原映射和初始条件，证明计算近似对应系统的伪轨迹；最后用影子理论证明该伪轨迹被系统真实轨迹所影子。

Result: 证明了计算得到的Koopman算子近似特征对可以解释为原系统某条真实轨迹（从不同初始条件出发）的数值近似，为数据驱动场景下的误差分析提供了严格的向后稳定性框架。

Conclusion: 该工作将数值影子理论引入Koopman算子和EDMD的数据驱动分析中，为受噪声污染数据的动力系统计算分析提供了新的可靠性评估视角，并深化了对数值影子的理解。

Abstract: This paper introduces shadowing theory based backward stability assessment of data driven computational analysis of discrete dynamical systems in the framework of the Koopman composition operator and the Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition. Data driven spectral analysis of the dynamics using the approximate (computed) eigenpairs of the Koopman operator is cast in terms of the backward error analysis. The individual residuals of the computed eigenpairs are aggregated in a backward perturbation of the Koopman operator, so that the computed eigenpairs are exact for a perturbed operator that is not a composition operator. Then, the impact of the perturbation in the operator is carried over to the map and the initial condition of the original system, showing that the computed approximations correspond exactly to a pseudo--trajectory of the system. In the final step, the pseudo--trajectory is shadowed by an exact trajectory of the system. This interpretation of errors is in particular suitable in data driven scenarios where the data is contaminated by noise. New insights into the numerical shadowing are provided.

</details>


### [3] [Construction of entropy satisfying Active Flux-type methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25111)
*Remi Abgrall,Yongle Liu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文分析了Active Flux格式用于满足一个熵不等式的双曲系统的熵稳定性。该格式演化两组自由度：单元边界的点值和单元内的平均值。研究表明只需确保平均值的熵不等式，点值自由度不起作用。作者构建了一个保界、无振荡且熵减的单体格式，并在KPP测试案例上验证了熵修正的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析Active Flux格式在双曲系统（满足单一熵不等式）中的熵稳定性性质，并构建同时满足保界、无振荡和熵减特性的数值方案。

Method: 基于Tadmor半离散框架实现熵条件，构建兼顾保界（参考BP_Pampa_VEM）和无振荡（参考PampaDG）性质的单体格式。仅在三角形网格上开发、实现和测试，但方法可扩展至多边形网格。

Result: 熵修正是有效的：即使不激活保界和无振荡条件，也能获得正确解（仅含预期中的虚假波动）。在KPP测试案例上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: Active Flux格式中只需保证平均值满足熵不等式即可实现熵稳定性，点值自由度不产生影响。所提方法可推广至多边形网格。

Abstract: This paper is devoted to the analysis of the entropy stability properties of Active Flux\yolo{-type} scheme for a hyperbolic system equipped with one entropy inequality. This type of scheme evolves two sets of degrees of freedom: point values that are chosen on the boundary of the elements that cover the computational domain, and the average of the solution in these elements. We show that the only thing to do is to get an entropy inequality for the average values, the point values degrees of freedom do not play any role. We construct a monolithic scheme which is bound preserving of \cite{BP_Pampa_VEM}, non oscillatory following \cite{PampaDG}, and entropy diminishing. The entropy condition is implemented in Tadmor's framework\cite{TadmorEntropy}, i.e. for the semi-discrete scheme only. The scheme is tested on the Kurganov-Popov-Petrova test case \cite{KPP} which is known to \yolo{be} sensitive to the satisfaction of an entropy inequality. We show that our entropy correction is effective: if we do not activate the bound-preserving nor the non oscillatory condition, we get the correct solution with some spurious wiggles, as expected. Though the development, implementation and tests are done with the triangle version of the scheme, the same method can be used for polygonal meshes, following \cite{BP_Pampa_VEM}.

</details>


### [4] [An adaptive phase field framework for large-scale interface evolution problems using a strong-form gradient smoothing approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25142)
*Zirui Mao,Alice Xu,Shenyang Hu,Panos Stinis,Yuyan Shao,Ang Li,Yulan Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合梯度平滑方法（GSM）与分层自适应移动结构化网格的强形式相场求解器，通过将计算分辨率自动集中在界面狭小区域，实现了近线性计算复杂度O(N)，显著优于均匀网格的二次复杂度，适用于具有薄界面或小界面面积分数的大规模多尺度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场模型在模拟界面主导现象时计算成本高昂，尤其是解析整个域内薄扩散界面需要大量计算资源，限制了其在大规模问题中的应用。

Method: 将梯度平滑方法（GSM）与分层自适应移动结构化网格相结合，在界面区域自动局部加密，而在体相区域保持粗网格；引入分层细化设计保持界面附近局部均匀分辨率，使GSM在非均匀网格下仍保持二阶精度。

Result: 基于Allen-Cahn和Cahn-Hilliard方程的数值算例表明，该自适应GSM求解器在界面演化精度上满足要求，计算复杂度为近线性O(N)，相较于均匀网格方法的二次复杂度O(N²)以及现有弱/强形式求解器具有显著效率优势。

Conclusion: 该自适应GSM强形式求解器在大规模薄界面或小界面面积分数问题中展现出优越的计算效率和可扩展性，为相场模型的高效求解提供了新途径。

Abstract: Multiscale problems with evolving interfaces are ubiquitous in science and engineering. Phase-field models are a powerful tool for simulating interface-dominated phenomena in computational mechanics and materials modeling, but their application to large-scale problems is often constrained by the high computational cost of resolving thin diffuse interfaces over the entire domain. This paper presents an efficient strong-form phase-field solver that couples the Gradient Smoothing Method (GSM) with a hierarchical adaptive and moving structured mesh, enabling automatic localization of resolution within a narrow interfacial region while retaining coarse discretization in bulk domains. A layered refinement design is introduced to preserve locally uniform resolution across the interface, allowing the GSM discretization to maintain overall second-order accuracy despite strong mesh non-uniformity away from the interface. Although GSM incurs a higher per-degree-of-freedom cost than standard finite-difference schemes, the adaptive framework substantially reduces the total number of degrees of freedom, resulting in near-linear computational scaling compared with the quadratic scaling of uniform-grid approaches. Numerical examples based on the Allen-Cahn and Cahn-Hilliard equations demonstrate that the proposed adaptive GSM solver delivers desired accuracy for interface evolution while attaining more favorable computational complexity, O(N), than existing weak-form and strong-form solvers, becoming significantly more efficient for large-scale problems with thin interfaces or a small interfacial area fraction relative to the whole domain.

</details>


### [5] [A Riemannian View on Active Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25163)
*Zachary Grey*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将主动子空间（Active Subspaces）从欧氏空间推广到黎曼流形上的方法，通过平行传输（Parallel Transport）实现。研究对比了内蕴方法与基于嵌入的外蕴方法在测地球内的局部特性，证明了二者在中心切空间上的特征值二阶一致、特征空间在谱间隙下同阶一致。以超球面为例，展示了该方法在预形状空间统计形状分析中的应用，并在2-球面上通过数值实验验证了脊恢复的曲率受限二次收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主动子空间方法局限于欧氏空间，无法直接应用于流形上定义的标量函数。许多科学计算与数据分析问题（如形状分析）涉及黎曼流形上的量，因此需要将主动子空间推广到流形设置。

Method: 1) 利用平行传输将主动子空间从欧氏空间推广到黎曼流形；2) 在测地球内局部研究内蕴方法与外蕴（嵌入梯度平均）方法的差异；3) 从理论上证明二者在中心切空间上的特征值和特征空间的高阶一致性；4) 对超球面这一特定流形进行重点分析，并在2-球面上进行数值脊恢复实验。

Result: 在中心切空间上：(1) 内蕴与外蕴方法的特征值在测地球半径的二阶意义下一致；(2) 主导特征空间在谱间隙条件下同阶一致。数值实验表明，在2-球面上脊恢复达到曲率受限的二次收敛率。

Conclusion: 主动子空间可成功推广到黎曼流形，内蕴与外蕴方法在局部中心切空间上高度一致，但扩展至更广区域时需改用变化切空间上的重新分解或利用平行传输保持单一中心标架。该方法在超球面预形状空间上具有实用价值。

Abstract: Active subspaces provide an explainable, eigenvalue-ordered principle for studying how scalar-valued quantities of interest change the most, on average, over a reduced basis of Euclidean domains. Composition with parallel transport generalizes this principle from Euclidean space to quantities of interest defined over Riemannian manifolds, and the resulting intrinsic formulation is contrasted with the extrinsic, embedding-based gradient average of manifold learning. Either strategy is studied in an intrinsically local sense, restricted to mean-centered geodesic-balls, and within that scope the two are not identical: on the central tangent space, eigenvalues agree to second order in the geodesic radius of the sampled domain, while dominant eigenspaces agree at the same order relative to the spectral gap. Extending activity beyond that central space then calls for either recomputed decompositions over changing tangent spaces or, intrinsically, parallel transport of a single central frame. Hyperspheres are emphasized throughout as a particular manifold of interest, motivated by applications over preshape spaces for statistical shape analysis. Numerical examples over the 2-sphere illustrate the formalism, including the derived ridge recovery at a curvature-limited quadratic rate.

</details>


### [6] [A Convex Splitting Spectral Method for the Phase Field Crystal Equation: Energy Stability, Computational Stability Maps, and Three-Dimensional GPU Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25177)
*Saulo Orizaga,Peimeng Yin,Deep Choudhuri*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于凸分裂框架的高效傅里叶谱方法用于相场晶体（PFC）方程。该一阶格式无条件能量稳定且质量守恒到机器精度。通过40,000次GPU加速模拟揭示经典条件a≥2过于保守，稳定区域可扩展至a<2。在256^3网格上成功执行10^6时间步长至T_f=10,000，严格保持能量耗散与质量守恒。最高512^3分辨率仿真在单块消费级GPU上完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PFC方程数值方法要么计算成本高，要么稳定性条件过于严格（经典条件a≥2），限制了其在大规模长时间模拟中的应用。需要开发一种既高效又具有宽松稳定性的数值方案。

Method: 基于Eyre凸分裂框架的傅里叶谱方法；截断势能论证建立能量稳定性定理；主模态能量平衡导出半解析中性稳定曲线；40,000次GPU加速模拟绘制计算稳定性图谱。

Result: 经典条件a≥2被证明过于保守；较小a值在稳定区内产生更低的L^2误差；在a<2条件下成功完成256^3网格至T_f=10,000（10^6时间步）的长时间三维仿真；单GPU实现512^3分辨率模拟。

Conclusion: 所提方法扩展了PFC方程数值模拟的可行参数范围，在放松稳定性条件下仍保持高保真度，且无需高性能计算基础设施即可在消费级GPU上实现大规模模拟。

Abstract: We present an efficient Fourier spectral method based on the convex splitting framework of Eyre~\cite{eyre1998unconditionally} for the phase field crystal (PFC) equation. The proposed first-order scheme is unconditionally energy stable and conserves mass to machine precision. An energy stability theorem is established using a truncated potential argument, and a semi-analytical neutral stability curve is derived from a dominant-mode energy balance, providing a closed-form characterization of the practical stability boundary. The classical sufficient condition $a \geq 2$ is shown to be conservative: a computational stability map obtained from 40,000 GPU-accelerated PFC simulations reveals that energy-stable solutions persist for values of $a$ significantly below this threshold. Crucially, accuracy analysis demonstrates that smaller values of $a$ within the stable region consistently yield lower $L^2$ errors. This high-fidelity regime is rigorously verified through an extended asymptotic stress test consisting of a long-time three-dimensional simulation on a $256^3$ grid up to $T_f = 10,000$, successfully executing $10^6$ continuous temporal increments deep within the relaxed stability regime, well below the classical convex splitting limit ($a < 2$), while preserving strict monotonic energy dissipation and machine-precision mass conservation. Finally, two-dimensional and three-dimensional simulations at resolutions up to $512^3$ are performed on a single consumer GPU, demonstrating the scalability of the proposed framework for resolving complex phase-field dynamics without requiring HPC infrastructure. The code is made publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [7] [Parallelisation of Discrete Exterior Calculus via Representation Theory on Curved and Three-Dimensional Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25192)
*Leon D. da Silva,Marcelo P. Santos,José D. da Silva,Gilson Ferreira*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了离散外微积分(DEC)算子在对称网格上的通用块对角化框架，证明离散外导数$d$和Hodge星算子$\star$在等距有限群作用下具有等变性，从而任何由$d$和$\star$组装的算子（如Hodge Laplacian、Maxwell型算子等）在单一对称适应基下自动呈现块对角结构。数值实验在测地球面($I_h$对称)、六边形环面($D_{6h}$对称)和体心立方(BCC)三维网格上分别实现了最高$182\times$的FLOP并行加速和约$9.07\times$的序列加速，且等变性在机器精度内得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DEC方法在对称网格上未能充分利用对称性来加速计算，而谱方法又受限于平坦Platonic域。本文旨在建立适用于弯曲流形和三维网格的通用对称约化框架，实现结构保持型DEC计算的高效并行求解。

Method: 利用有限群作用在余链空间上诱导的置换表示，严格证明了$d$和$\star$在等距有限群作用下是等变的。由此推导出由$d$和$\star$组装的任意算子在单一对称适应基下具有块对角结构，该基仅需对每个网格计算一次。

Result: 在$I_h$对称测地球面和$D_{6h}$对称六边形环面上分别实现$62\times$和$182\times$的FLOP并行加速；在BCC三维环面网格上验证了$d$、$\star$和Hodge Laplacian在机器精度内的等变性，序列加速接近理论渐近值$9.07\times$，Schur多重度约化可进一步加深约$|G|$倍。

Conclusion: 该框架将对称约化从平坦谱域推广到弯曲流形和三维网格，仅需构建一次对称适应基即可显著降低结构保持型DEC计算的线性求解成本，适用于计算电磁学和几何流体模拟等应用领域。

Abstract: We establish a universal block-diagonalization framework for Discrete Exterior Calculus (DEC) operators on symmetric meshes, enabling embarrassingly parallel solvers with provable FLOP reductions. We prove that the two fundamental DEC operators, the discrete exterior derivative $d$ and the Hodge star $\star$, are equivariant under isometric finite group actions on simplicial complexes. The proof exploits the permutation representation induced on cochain spaces by the group action. As a consequence, any operator assembled from $d$ and $\star$ (including the Hodge Laplacian, the codifferential, Maxwell-type operators, and elasticity operators) inherits a block-diagonal structure in a single symmetry-adapted basis, which is computed only once per mesh. Unlike spectral methods restricted to flat Platonic domains, the framework applies natively to curved manifolds and is applicable in principle to computational electromagnetism and geometric fluid simulation on symmetric domains. Numerical experiments on a geodesic sphere ($I_h$ symmetry) and a hexagonal torus ($D_{6h}$ symmetry) yield FLOP-based parallel speedups, relative to a dense direct factorization, of up to $62\times$ and $182\times$, respectively. A further experiment on a body-centred-cubic (BCC) tessellation of the flat 3-torus $T^3$ with $T_d$ symmetry confirms equivariance of the exterior derivative, Hodge star, and Hodge Laplacian at machine precision for form degrees $k=0,1,2$ across three mesh resolutions. The FLOP-based sequential speedup approaches its theoretical asymptote of $\approx 9.07\times$, which a standard Schur-multiplicity reduction deepens by a further factor of order $|G|$. These results show that a single symmetry-adapted basis reduces the linear-solve cost of structure-preserving DEC computations on curved and three-dimensional meshes.

</details>


### [8] [Sequential Preconditioned Conjugate Gradient Method for Linear Statistical Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25272)
*Guan-Yu Chen,Dong-Yue Xie,Xi Yang,Zun-Hao Zheng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种名为SPCG的随机迭代方法解决大规模线性模型中的普通最小二乘估计问题，通过构建一系列草图尺寸递增的最小二乘子问题并用PCG内求解，以预热启动和最终精炼阶段降低计算成本，理论证明其达到OLS预测精度，实验表明在迭代次数和CPU时间上优于全数据PCG和IDS方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模线性统计模型中，普通最小二乘估计的计算成本极高，现有方法效率不足，需要一种既能保证预测精度又能显著降低计算开销的迭代求解方法。

Method: SPCG方法：构建一系列草图尺寸递增的随机素描最小二乘子问题，采用预条件共轭梯度法(PCG)作为内层求解器，每个子问题以上一个子问题的解进行热启动，最后对全尺度问题进行精炼阶段优化。

Result: 理论证明了SPCG能够达到OLS预测精度，并给出了各子问题的迭代界和复杂度估计。数值实验显示SPCG在达到目标预测精度时，迭代次数和CPU时间均少于全数据PCG和迭代双重素描(IDS)方法。

Conclusion: SPCG通过多尺度子问题递进求解策略，在保证OLS预测精度的前提下显著降低了计算成本，是一种高效的大规模最小二乘估计方法。

Abstract: We propose a randomized iterative method for the ordinary least-squares estimation problem in large-scale linear statistical models, namely the Sequential Preconditioned Conjugate Gradient Method (SPCG). SPCG constructs a sequence of sketched least-squares subproblems with increasing sketch sizes, applies PCG as the inner solver, and warm-starts each subproblem from the previous solution. A final refinement stage is then performed on the full-scale problem. Since most iterations are carried out on smaller subproblems, the overall computational cost is significantly reduced. We establish the convergence theory, prove that SPCG attains OLS prediction accuracy, and derive per-subproblem iteration bounds and complexity estimates. Numerical experiments show that SPCG reaches the target prediction accuracy with fewer iterations and less CPU time than full-data PCG and Iterative Double Sketching (IDS).

</details>

Abstract: The Julia programming language aims to provide a modern approach to develop high-performance computing (HPC) applications. It tries to achieve this by combining a high-level, dynamic interface with just-in-time compilation to native machine code, thereby facilitating high developer productivity and native code performance at the same time. While this approach has already been shown to work well for serial applications, it is not clear if it readily translates to traditional, massively parallel HPC work loads. In this paper, we fill this gap by analyzing the parallel performance of the numerical computational fluid dynamics simulation code Trixi$.$jl, written in Julia, and compare it to the Fortran code FLUXO. We show some of the challenges of using Julia at scale and discuss possible solutions, specifically with respect to code loading and compilation at startup. Finally, we demonstrate the parallel scaling of our Julia code on up to 61440 CPU cores.

</details>

Motivation: 现有数值求解器框架大多针对单一或多物理问题独立开发，耦合求解时通常采用扩展现有求解器、开发全新求解器或通过耦合库/框架的接口连接两个求解器，但尚缺乏一个允许轻松直接开发耦合求解器的框架。

Abstract: Many standalone frameworks for numerical solvers have been developed to tackle the simulation of specific single- or multi-physics problems. For solving coupled problems, common approaches are to extend existing solvers, to develop an entirely new solver or to couple two existing solvers by using the interface provided by a coupling library or framework. However, to the best of our knowledge, there is not yet a framework that allows the easy and direct development of coupled solvers. In this work, we prototype a portable reproducible cross-language framework for the development of coupled parallel adaptive solvers using Trixi$.$jl and deal$.$II for the numerical simulation of coupled multi-physics problems. Currently, this is tightly entangled with an example coupled solver. We show its usability by developing a partitioned strongly-coupled multi-physics solver for the dynamics of Newtonian self-gravitational gases. For the coupled solver, we validate the expected order of convergence, physical sensibility of the results and mesh adaptivity. Finally, we investigate its parallel scaling. A publicly accessible reproducibility repository for the numerical results and code is available.

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文针对平面点集$(1+\varepsilon)$-spanner的边交叉问题，提出了一个边数为$O(n/\varepsilon)$、每条边交叉次数为$\tilde{O}(1/\varepsilon^3)$、总交叉次数为$\tilde{O}(n/\varepsilon^4)$、交叉边长度比为$O(1/\varepsilon^2)$的构造方法，显著改进了之前的上界，并且是首个对任意常数$\varepsilon>0$保证每条边$O(1)$次交叉的spanner构造；同时给出了相应的下界结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在平面点集的$(1+\varepsilon)$-spanner构造中，边交叉次数是衡量spanner质量的重要指标之一。此前已知的上界不够理想，且缺乏对每条边交叉次数的常数界保证。本文旨在构造一个在保持稀疏性（边数$O(n/\varepsilon)$）的同时，显著降低边交叉次数及其比值的spanner，并探索该问题的下界。

Method: 提出了一种新的spanner构造算法，通过精心设计的几何结构控制边的交叉次数，使得每条边的交叉次数仅为$\tilde{O}(1/\varepsilon^3)$，总交叉次数为$\tilde{O}(n/\varepsilon^4)$，且任意两条交叉边的长度比不超过$O(1/\varepsilon^2)$。

Result: 上界：构造出$(1+\varepsilon)$-spanner，边数$O(n/\varepsilon)$，每条边$\tilde{O}(1/\varepsilon^3)$次交叉，总交叉$\tilde{O}(n/\varepsilon^4)$，交叉边长度比$O(1/\varepsilon^2)$。下界：存在$n$点集使任何$(1+\varepsilon)$-spanner有$\Omega(n/\varepsilon^3)$次总交叉；存在$n$点集使某条边有$\Omega(1/\varepsilon^{5/2})$次交叉；存在4点集使交叉边长度比达到$\Omega(1/\varepsilon)$。

Conclusion: 本文的构造首次实现了对任意常数$\varepsilon>0$每条边$O(1)$次交叉的$(1+\varepsilon)$-spanner，大幅改进了交叉次数的上界；同时下界结果表明该构造在交叉次数和长度比方面几乎是紧的。

Abstract: For $n$ points in the plane and an $\varepsilon>0$, we construct a $(1+\varepsilon)$-spanner with $O(n/\varepsilon)$ edges in which every edge has $\tilde{O}(1/\varepsilon^3)$ crossings, hence the total number of crossings is $\tilde{O}(n/\varepsilon^4)$, furthermore the ratio between the lengths of any two crossing edges is $O(1/\varepsilon^2)$. Our spanner construction substantially improves on the previous upper bound for the number of crossings in a $(1+\varepsilon)$-spanner, and it is the first spanner construction that ensures $O(1)$ crossings per edge for any constant $\varepsilon>0$. In contrast, we construct: $n$ points in the plane for which every $(1+\varepsilon)$-spanner has $Ω(n/\varepsilon^3)$ crossings, $n$ points for which every $(1+\varepsilon)$-spanner has an edge with $Ω(1/\varepsilon^{5/2})$ crossings, and 4 points for which every $(1+\varepsilon)$-spanner contains two crossing edges where one is $Ω(1/\varepsilon)$ times longer than the other.

</details>

*Jérôme Michaud,Sergey Korotov*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于将三角形最大角n等分的网格细化算法，并与经典的最长边n等分法进行对比。研究表明，最长边n等分在n≥4时会导致最小角趋于0、最大角趋于π，从而破坏角度条件；而最大角n等分法（n≥2）始终满足最小角和最大角条件，能生成非退化的三角剖分，且递归算法产生的三角形直径随层级增加趋于0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最长边n等分法在n≥4时会产生退化三角形（最小角→0，最大角→π），需要探索新的细化规则以避免这一缺陷，同时保持网格质量。

Method: 提出最大角n等分细化算法：每次将当前三角形中最大角等分为n份，递归生成子三角形。从几何角度分析该算法生成的三角剖分性质，并与最长边n等分法进行理论对比。

Result: 1) 最大角n等分法对所有n≥2均满足最小角条件：若初始三角形最小角为γ₀，则所有后代三角形的最小角≥ min{γ₀, π/(3n)}，最大角≤ π-2m_n < π。2) 该算法产生的三角形直径随递归层级k→∞而趋于0，构成真正的三角剖分族。3) 证明了退化并非n等分本身所致，而是最长边分割策略的固有缺陷。

Conclusion: 最大角n等分法是比最长边n等分法更优的网格细化策略，它克服了后者在高n值时的退化问题，适用于需要高质量、非退化三角剖分的数值计算场景。

Abstract: We define a mesh refinement algorithm based on the rule of dividing the largest angles of triangular elements of planar partitions in focus into $n$ equal parts, and analyse the (geometric) properties of triangulations generated by this technique. This largest-angle $n$-section rule is compared with the classical longest-edge $n$-section rule, where it is the longest edges which are split into $n$ equal parts. The longest-edge bisection and trisection are known to produce nondegenerate triangulations (possibly with hanging nodes), but the longest-edge $n$-sections with $n\geq 4$ always produce (infinite) sequences of triangles with minimum angles tending to zero (moreover, their relevant maximum angles tend to $π$), thus breaking the minimum and maximum angle conditions. We show that this degeneration effect is not a consequence of $n$-section itself. For every $n\geq 2$, the largest-angle $n$-sections produce partitions satisfying the minimum angle condition (and, therefore, the maximum angle condition). More precisely, if the initial triangle has its smallest angle $γ_0>0$, then all descendant triangles have angles bounded below by $m_n=\min\left\{γ_0,\fracπ{3n}\right\},$ and, correspondingly, bounded above by $π-2m_n<π$. We also show that the recursive largest-angle $n$-section algorithm always produces a family of triangular partitions, i.e. the maximum diameter of level-$k$ descendants tends to zero as $k \to \infty$.

</details>

Abstract: While drawing planar graphs with few slopes and few bends is a well-studied problem, corresponding extensions to beyond-planar graphs still remain mostly unexplored. Motivated by this observation, in this work, we provide bounds on the slope number of biconnected $1$-planar graphs when two bends are allowed along each edge. Our contribution is an incremental drawing algorithm that produces $2$-bend $1$-planar drawings of biconnected $1$-plane graphs with maximum degree $Δ$ using any prescribed set of $Δ$ pairwise distinct slopes.

</details>


### [16] [Extending Biconnected Straight-Line Planar Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25756)
*Giordano Andreola,Susanna Caroppo,Giordano Da Lozzo,Marco D'Elia,Giuseppe Di Battista,Fabrizio Frati,Fabrizio Grosso,Maurizio Patrignani*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究了部分图可扩展性（PDE）问题，即给定平面图G、子图H及其直线平面绘制Γ_H，询问能否将Γ_H扩展为G的直线平面绘制。研究发现：即使H是双连通图且G是次三次图，PDE仍保持NP难；但当G有固定嵌入且H双连通时，提出了O(p² n)时间算法；此外，基于实数存在理论，证明了该问题在顶点覆盖数为参数时是FPT的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDE问题在平面图绘制领域具有基础重要性，Patrignani已证明其在H高度不连通时是NP难的。本文旨在探索当初始部分绘制H具有更高连通性（双连通）时问题的计算复杂度边界，并寻找可高效求解的特例。

Method: 1) 通过归约证明即使H双连通、G次三次且H外部分仅为长度2路径，PDE仍NP难；2) 针对G固定嵌入且H双连通、剩余部分为p条长度2路径的情形，设计O(p² n)时间算法；3) 利用实数存在理论（ETR）证明在顶点覆盖数为参数时问题为FPT。

Result: 1) H双连通时PDE仍NP难，且G可限制为次三次图；2) 固定嵌入下存在多项式时间算法（O(p² n)），与可变嵌入的NP难形成鲜明对比；3) 参数化版本（顶点覆盖数）在固定和可变嵌入下均为FPT。

Conclusion: PDE问题的复杂度高度依赖于嵌入方式和图的连通性：可变嵌入下即使H双连通仍NP难，而固定嵌入下则可多项式求解。开放问题为当G固定嵌入且H连通（或双连通）时的一般复杂度仍未解决。

Abstract: The Partial Drawing Extensibility problem, for short PDE, takes as input a triple $\langle G,H,Γ_H\rangle$, where $G$ is a planar graph, $H$ is a subgraph of $G$, and $Γ_H$ is a straight-line planar drawing of $H$, and asks whether $Γ_H$ can be extended to a straight-line planar drawing of $G$. Patrignani [Int. J. Found. Comput. Sci. (2006)] proved that the PDE problem is NP-hard, exploiting instances in which $H$ is highly disconnected. In this paper, we study the PDE problem under the requirement that the initial partial drawing $Γ_H$ is biconnected. We show that PDE remains NP-hard even for instances in which $H$ is a biconnected graph with faces of bounded size, $G$ is subcubic, and the part of $G$ that is not in $H$ consists of length-$2$ paths. The complexity of PDE remains however open when $H$ is connected (or even biconnected) if $G$ has a fixed embedding. In this setting both a polynomial-time algorithm or an NP-hardness proof seem to be elusive targets. As a step towards tackling this problem, we study instances of PDE in which $H$ is biconnected, $G$ has a fixed embedding, and the rest of the graph consists of $p$ length-2 paths, and present an $O(p^2 n)$-time algorithm, a result in sharp contrast with the NP-hardness of the variable embedding setting. Moreover, with an approach based on the Existential Theory of the Reals, we show that, if $H$ is biconnected, the problem is FPT parameterized by the vertex cover number of $G$, both in a fixed and in a variable embedding setting.

</details>

Abstract: Let $Y=Γ\backslash G/H$ be a compact standard quotient of a non-Riemannian semisimple symmetric space $X=G/H$. Abstract: We introduce an extremal invariant associated with Chowla-type order conditions in finite groups. A nonempty subset $S$ of a finite group $G$ is called a Chowla set if every element of $S$ has order greater than $|S|$, and we write $\Ccal(G)$ for the maximum cardinality of such a set. We first show that $\Ccal(G)$ is determined by the distribution of element orders in $G$. For cyclic groups, we derive an exact divisor formula and characterize the integers $n$ for which $\Ccal(\mathbb Z/n\mathbb Z)=\varphi(n)$. We prove that $\liminf_{n\to\infty}\Ccal(\mathbb Z/n\mathbb Z)/\varphi(n)=1$, whereas $\limsup_{n\to\infty}\Ccal(\mathbb Z/n\mathbb Z)/\varphi(n)=\infty$, and we determine the corresponding lower and upper limits under normalization by $n$. For finite abelian groups, we obtain an explicit formula in terms of the invariant-factor decomposition, together with a closed formula for finite abelian $p$-groups.
  We then develop a linear analogue for finite field extensions. A nonzero $K$-subspace $A$ of an extension $L/K$ is called a Chowla subspace if $[K(a):K]>\dim_K A$ for every nonzero $a\in A$. Since this condition depends on $\dim_K A$, it does not generally require every nonzero element of $A$ to generate $L$ over $K$. Nevertheless, when $L/K$ is finite and separable, we prove the exact formula $\Ccal(L/K)=[L:K]-d_{\max}(L/K)$, where $d_{\max}(L/K)$ is the largest degree over $K$ of a proper intermediate field. For finite fields, we give a direct proof in every degree using a normal-basis construction.
  This work was developed through an expert-guided human--AI collaboration. A reasoning-focused configuration of Co-Scientist was used to explore examples and potential proof strategies. The authors formulated the problem, independently verified and completed all arguments, and wrote the final proofs.

Abstract: Let $d \geq 1$ be an integer, $G$ be an abelian group and $ν, w_1, \dots, w_{2d}: G \to [0, \infty)$ be functions with finite, non-empty supports. A standard Fourier analytic argument delivers the estimate \[ E_{2d,ν}(w_1, \dots, w_{2d}) \leq \prod_{1 \leq i \leq 2d} E_{2d, ν}(w_i)^{1/2d}.\] In this note, we present a purely combinatorial proof of the above inequality. In particular, our proof does not use any Fourier or spectral analysis and relies on repeated applications of Cauchy--Schwarz inequality combined with a discrete convexity extension type argument. Abstract: We study the intersection cohomology of minimally compactified Shimura varieties of PEL type AC using Igusa stacks and the work of Fargues-Scholze. Abstract: We focus on refined Humbert invariants of principally polarized superspecial abelian surfaces, introduced by Kani in 1994. Then, we present several applications of computing this invariant for isogeny-based cryptography. First, we provide a decision algorithm to check if two given polarizations are isomorphic. Abstract: This paper studies finite-time blow-up of pseudospherical metrics induced by solutions of the Cauchy problem for the Degasperis--Procesi equation. Abstract: We reconsider the analysis of the Classical Painlevé Problem developed by F. Génot and B. Brogliato ([1] New results on Painlevé paradoxes. - European Journal of Mechanics-A/Solids, 18(4):653{677, 1999), focusing on the consistency of their results with Galilean invariance. We show that certain conclusions concerning the dynamical evolution of the mechanical system are not invariant under changes of Galilean observer and therefore cannot, in their present form, be interpreted as intrinsic properties of the system. In particular, we show that the classi?cation of motion states depends on the observer through the velocity{dependent characterization of the frictional constraint, and we trace the origin of this dependence to the Galilean velocity-addition theorem. We present and discuss possible reformulations of the model aimed at restoring observer-invariant descriptions of the problem.

Abstract: The problem of finding lower and upper bounds on multivariate homogeneous polynomials is both difficult and important given its applications to questions ranging from dynamical stability in complex potential landscapes to data analysis. From the standpoint of tensor eigenvalue theory, the question is equivalent to finding the smallest and the largest eigenvalues of the coefficient tensor corresponding to the given polynomial. Standard approaches outlined in the literature amount to running nonlinear iterations in search for the optimal rays along which the growth of the polynomial is fastest or slowest. Unlike the case of matrices (or their corresponding multivariate quadratic forms) convergence of such algorithms for higher-rank tensors is capricious due to the complex topography of polynomial objective functions. In this essay, a very different strategy, inspired by quantum-mechanical variational methods, is introduced for finding bounds on polynomials. The original polynomial is replaced by an operator acting in a suitably chosen (large) space of states, such that in an appropriate "classical" limit this operator approaches the original polynomial expression made of commutative variables. As a result, approximating the smallest and largest eigenvalues of the coefficient tensor, and thus finding bounds on polynomials, amounts to diagonalizing the resulting quantum operator, represented as a large matrix, and then inspecting the smallest and largest eigenvalues of this matrix. This approach is then successfully applied to standard test examples from tensor eigenvalue literature and other problems of interest in mathematical physics including Strichartz-type inequalities.

</details>

Abstract: In classical statistical physics, the Mori-Zwanzig projection operator technique is used to derive generalized Langevin equations for a random variable (observable). Standard derivations implicitly assume the existence of solutions to the so-called orthogonal dynamics equation as well as the validity of the variation of constants formula (Dyson identity). It was pointed out by Givon, Hald and Kupferman that the existence is a subtle issue for infinite-rank projections such as Zwanzig's projection [D. Givon, O. H. Hald, R. Kupferman, Israel Journal of Mathematics, 145 (221-241), 2005]. The authors proved the existence of weak solutions for Zwanzig's projection in the context of stationary Hamiltonian systems. To this date, this is the only existence proof that allows for an infinite-rank projection, whereas the uniqueness and regularity remain open problems.
  In this article, we generalize the existence proof by Givon et al. to nonstationary non-Hamiltonian systems whose time evolution is given by a quasicontraction semigroup. We establish growth bounds as well as the uniqueness for sufficiently regular solutions (if existent). Finally, we apply our results to Zwanzig's projection using the damped harmonic oscillator as an example.

Abstract: In electromagnetic heating and other applications, we need to compute the volume enclosed by an isosurface of the 3D temperature distribution that is numerically represented on a rectangular grid. This situation arises naturally when the temperature distribution is obtained by solving a partial differential equation numerically using a finite difference method. Given the temperature distribution on the 3D grid, the isosurface of a prescribed value is represented approximately by a triangulation, a collection of triangles with vertices on the grid lines. The vertices are determined by a linear interpolation to approximate the locations where the temperature is at the prescribed level. The region enclosed by the isosurface is approximated by that enclosed by the triangulation, which is a set of tetrahedrons. The enclosed volume is approximated by summing those of tetrahedrons. This volume approximation is analogous to approximating a curve using line segments and is limited in accuracy. In this study, we combine extrapolation with the triangulation approximation to develop a more accurate method for computing the volume enclosed by an isosurface of the 3D temperature distribution on a rectangular grid.

</details>

We also give an explicit description of the Dennis trace map at level $0$, i.e. from the zeroth $K$-theory group to the zeroth Hochschild homology group. Method: 开发新型数值积分器，同时实现质量转移和2.5阶后牛顿运动学模拟，对白矮星-致密天体双星进行从弱引力辐射主导的慢速旋进到质量转移主导的快速外旋的全过程数值模拟。

Result: 1) 双星经历慢速旋进和快速外旋两阶段；2) 波形在过渡频率处有信号积累；3) 最大截止频率强烈依赖白矮星质量和致密程度；4) 银河系外双星无法被LISA或地基探测器探测；5) DECIGO每年可探测数百万旋进和数千外旋事件，LGWA可探测数十例白矮星-中子星双星最终旋进；6) 双星命运多样：低/高伴星质量导致共有包层或并合，中等质量不接触；7) 最高频率10-20 Hz。

Conclusion: 白矮星-致密天体双星末期是未来分赫兹引力波探测器的重要信源，其引力波特征和最终命运高度依赖于系统参数，但不会与亚太阳质量致密天体信号混淆。

Abstract: We investigate the end stages of circular white-dwarf--compact-object binaries by developing a novel integrator implementing mass transfer and 2.5-order Post-Newtonian kinematics. White-dwarf--compact-object binaries are subject to two phases: a slow inspiral during which binary evolution is dominated by weak-gravity radiation reaction and a quicker ``outspiral" dominated by mass transfer. These systems have unique gravitational waveforms with several characteristic features including an accumulation of signal near the maximum cutoff frequency set by the transition between in- and outspiral. We investigate trends in gravitational wave parameters based on component masses. In particular, maximum cutoff frequency depends strongly on white dwarf mass and compactness. It is unlikely that extragalactic binaries will be measured by LISA or current or future terrestrial detectors. However, white-dwarf--compact-object binary end stages will be an important source for future decihertz detectors such as DECIGO and LGWA: The final inspiral of tens of white-dwarf--neutron-star binaries will be detected annually by LGWA, and millions of inspirals along with thousands of outspirals will be detected by DECIGO. The ultimate fates of these binaries are diverse and dependent upon the intricacies of angular momentum loss. In particular, we show that white dwarfs with partners of sufficiently low/high mass can end their lives in common envelopes/mergers, while white dwarfs do not enter contact with partners of intermediate mass. The highest frequencies produced by white-dwarf--compact-object binaries in our tests are 10--20 Hz---incapable of mimicking sub-solar-mass compact object signals in terrestrial detectors or explaining S251112cm-like signals.

Abstract: The Milky Way hosts astrophysical objects that accelerate cosmic rays to energies beyond the reach of terrestrial particle accelerators. It remains a longstanding goal to locate the sites of these powerful Galactic engines and understand how cosmic rays propagate through the Galaxy, leading to the production of high-energy neutrinos. In this paper, we combine event morphologies characteristic of all three neutrino flavours and apply recent improve- ments in ice modelling, calibration and reconstruction to 12 years of IceCube data. With a predefined, global analysis we establish high-energy neutrino emission from the Galactic plane at 5.7 $σ$ significance. A further study shows that the inner region of the Galaxy is a prominent neutrino source, with 217 shower events with visible energy above 5 TeV com- pared with an expected background of 154.4 $\pm$ 4.1. These results herald a new era of Galactic multi-messenger astronomy, creating new opportunities to study cosmic-ray propagation and probe neutrino properties over kiloparsec distances.

<details>

Method: 开发了首个综合模拟包，对RET-CR浅层冰中探测器接收到的三种信号成分（空气簇射射电辐射、冰内次级级联的Askaryan射电辐射、以及发射无线电信号被冰内次级级联电离径迹反射的雷达信号）进行联合模拟和特征分析。

Result: 描述了模拟框架，给出了浅层冰中探测器接收到的无线电和雷达信号的空间足迹和时间特性等关键特征。

Conclusion: 该模拟研究验证了RET-CR同时探测空气簇射射电信号、Askaryan辐射和雷达反射信号的可行性，为RET-N中利用雷达技术探测高能中微子提供了关键的技术验证和模拟工具基础。

Abstract: To explore neutrino astronomy at high energies (> 10 PeV), the Radar Echo Telescope for Cosmic Rays (RET-CR) was developed to assess the feasibility of a radar technique for detecting particle cascades in ice, serving as a precursor to the Radar Echo Telescope for Neutrinos (RET-N). The main concept of RET-CR is that, as a high-energy cosmic-ray air-shower core propagates into the high-altitude ice sheet, a dense secondary-particle cascade is created, which is very similar to that of an in-ice high-energy neutrino-induced cascade. At RET-CR, the expected signal consists of three distinct components: radio emission from the in-air particle shower, Askaryan radio emission from the secondary in-ice cascade, and the radar signal itself arising from the reflection of the transmitted radio signal from the ionisation trail of the in-ice secondary cascade. In this work, we present the first combined simulation-based package and study aimed at characterising the combined radio and radar signals at the in-ice receivers at RET-CR. We describe the simulation framework and provide a detailed discussion of the salient features of the radio and radar signals, including their spatial footprints and temporal characteristics, as predicted for the shallow in-ice detectors.

</details>

Abstract: Context. Gamma-Loud Protostars (GLPs) have been recently reported as Galactic hadronic accelerators whose acceleration site is situated in their protostellar jets. Theoretical and observational analysis situate radio cm luminosity as a thermal tracer of jet activity, presenting an unique opportunity to study jet properties as accelerators.
  Aims. We aim to develop a way to estimate the kinetic power of protostellar jets and compare their energetics with those extracted from the non-thermal gamma-ray side of GLPs.
  Methods. We combine theoretical and phenomenological relations to estimate the jet mechanical luminosity based on the radio cm luminosity. We relate the resulting values to the non-thermal contribution of GLPs, studying its behaviour and efficiency.
  Results. The derivation of the jet power successfully reproduces infrared and radio observations. The cosmic-ray energy correlates to the injected mechanical energy, implying an acceleration efficiency of 1--10%. Future radio observations are needed to find the definite accelerators and obtain reliable efficiencies.

</details>


### [51] [Tightening Bounds on Warm Dark Matter with High-Redshift Gamma-Ray Bursts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25261)
*Jun-Jie Wei,Jing-Meng Hao,Ding-Fang Hu,Yang Liu,Bao Wang,Xi Kang,Xue-Feng Wu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用过去20年Swift卫星的118个高红移伽马射线暴（GRB）数据，通过最大似然分析，在假定GRB形成率与宇宙恒星形成率成比例（含额外红移演化参数）的条件下，对温暗物质粒子质量m_x给出了稳健下限：m_x≳1.3 keV（95%置信度）；若GRB率精确追踪恒星形成率，则下限收紧至m_x≳3.4 keV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷暗物质模型在大尺度上成功但小尺度上存在困难，温暗物质（WDM）可通过抑制小尺度结构形成来缓解这些问题。高红移处已形成的塌缩结构对WDM粒子质量m_x施加了严格下限，因此需要利用高红移探针来约束m_x。

Method: 使用Swift卫星过去20年积累的GRB数据，选取118个z<10且L≥4.0×10^52 erg/s的GRB样本；假设GRB形成率与宇宙恒星形成率（SFR）成正比，并引入额外红移演化参数(1+z)^α；采用最大似然分析法推导m_x的下限约束。

Result: 当GRB率含自由红移演化参数α时，得到m_x≳1.3 keV（95% CL）；当α=0（GRB率精确追踪SFR）时，下限收紧至m_x≳3.4 keV（95% CL）。

Conclusion: GRB是探测早期宇宙的强大工具，对WDM模型给出了稳健约束；进一步理解GRB率与SFR之间的关系将能获得更紧的限制。

Abstract: The cold dark matter paradigm successfully explains large-scale structure but faces persistent tensions on small scales. Warm dark matter (WDM) with $\mathrm{keV}$-scale particles can alleviate these issues by suppressing small-scale structure formation. The presence of collapsed structures at high redshifts places strong lower limits on the WDM particle mass $m_x$. Gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) are ideal high-redshift probes due to their extreme brightness. Using the most recent \emph{Swift} GRB data accumulated over the past two decades, we derive robust constraints on $m_x$ by conservatively assuming that the comoving GRB formation rate is proportional to the cosmic star formation rate (SFR), with an additional redshift evolution parameterized as $(1+z)^α$. Applying a maximum-likelihood analysis to 118 GRBs with redshift $z<10$ and luminosity $L\ge 4.0\times10^{52}\,\mathrm{erg\,s^{-1}}$, we obtain $m_x \gtrsim 1.3\,\mathrm{keV}$ at the 95\% confidence level (CL). When the GRB rate is assumed to exactly trace the SFR (i.e., $α=0$), the lower limit tightens to $m_x \gtrsim 3.4\,\mathrm{keV}$ at the same CL. These robust constraints demonstrate that GRBs are a powerful probe of the early Universe. A better understanding of the relationship between the GRB rate and the SFR would enable even tighter limits on WDM models.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种快速贝叶斯管道，用于在脉冲星计时阵列数据中搜索处于相对论性偏心轨道的超大质量黑洞双星产生的连续引力波，该方法将圆形轨道的QuickCW框架扩展至偏心轨道，通过将模型参数分为投影参数和形状参数实现计算效率的大幅提升，并经过模拟数据验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星计时阵列已探测到纳赫兹引力波背景，下一步关键里程碑是探测单个超大质量黑洞双星的连续引力波并识别其电磁对应体。现有搜索通常假设圆形轨道，但理论研究表明在PTA频段发射引力波的双星可能保留显著偏心率，因此需要能够高效处理偏心轨道的搜索方法。

Method: 将QuickCW圆形双星框架扩展至相对论性偏心轨道，遵循参数分解策略：将模型参数分为投影参数（可快速更新）和形状参数（固定不变）；对每组固定形状参数执行大量投影参数更新，利用两者计算速度的数量级差异实现高效贝叶斯推断。

Result: 通过模拟PTA数据集的探测分析和上限分析验证了管道的有效性；该框架使得在下一代包含更多脉冲星的PTA数据集中搜索偏心SMBHB在计算上变得可行。

Conclusion: 该偏心贝叶斯管道为未来PTA数据中搜索偏心轨道超大质量黑洞双星连续引力波提供了计算上可行的方案，有望推动单个SMBHB及其电磁对应体的识别。

Abstract: Recent pulsar timing array (PTA) results have provided evidence for the presence of a nanohertz gravitational wave (GW) background, most likely originating from a population of supermassive black hole binaries (SMBHBs). The next major milestone is the detection of continuous GWs (CGWs) from an individual SMBHB and the identification of its electromagnetic counterpart. Typically, searches for CGWs in PTA data assume binaries in circular orbits. However, theoretical studies indicate that binaries emitting GWs in the PTA frequency band may retain significant eccentricity. In this paper, we present a fast and efficient Bayesian pipeline to search for CGWs from individual SMBHBs in relativistic eccentric orbits in PTA datasets. Our approach extends the QuickCW framework for circular binaries, in which model parameters are divided into projection and shape parameters. Computational efficiency is achieved by performing many updates of the projection parameters for a fixed set of shape parameters, an operation that is orders of magnitude faster than updating the shape parameters. We validate the pipeline through both detection and upper-limit analyses of simulated PTA datasets. This framework makes the search for eccentric SMBHBs computationally feasible, even in the next-generation PTA datasets with many more pulsars.

</details>

### [60] [Laplace-PSN-IRT: Uncertainty Quantification for Neural Item Response Theory Models of LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25257)
*Juan Francisco,Mandujano Reyes*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出Laplace-PSN-IRT方法，在已训练的PSN-IRT模型上通过最后一层拉普拉斯近似实现近似贝叶斯后验推断，从而在无需重新训练的情况下获得模型能力和项目难度的校准不确定性。实验表明，标准LLM排行榜上12个模型的大多数两两比较在统计上不可区分；点估计下的Fisher信息在单个参考能力处可能接近零，而后验期望的Fisher信息在能力范围内更稳定；后验期望的Fisher信息在大多数情况下能从小基准子集中更准确地恢复完整基准的能力排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经IRT方法（如PSN-IRT）仅提供点估计，缺乏不确定性量化能力，限制了后续统计推断（如置信区间、模型间概率比较、基于Fisher信息的项目选择）。

Method: 在已训练的PSN-IRT模型上应用最后一层拉普拉斯近似（last-layer Laplace approximation），对模型能力和项目难度参数进行近似贝叶斯后验推断，无需重新训练。

Result: 1) 12个模型的大多数两两比较在统计上不可区分；2) 点估计Fisher信息在单个参考能力处可能接近零，后验期望Fisher信息更稳定；3) 后验期望Fisher信息在多数设置下更准确地从小基准子集恢复完整排名，最小子集时与点估计持平；4) 将项目难度视为随机、区分度固定时可获得校准良好的不确定性。

Conclusion: Laplace-PSN-IRT为LLM基准评估提供了有效的不确定性量化框架，揭示了点估计排名的统计不可区分性问题，并展示了后验期望Fisher信息在项目选择和子集排名恢复中的优势。

Abstract: Item Response Theory (IRT) has recently been proposed as a framework for evaluating large language model (LLM) benchmarks by separating a model's latent ability from the properties of individual benchmark items. Existing neural IRT approaches, including PSN-IRT, estimate these quantities using point estimates, limiting uncertainty quantification and downstream statistical inference. We introduce Laplace-PSN-IRT, a post-hoc last-layer Laplace approximation that augments a trained PSN-IRT model with approximate Bayesian posterior inference, recovering calibrated uncertainty over model ability and item difficulty without retraining. The resulting posterior enables credible intervals, probabilistic comparisons between models, and propagation of parameter uncertainty into Fisher-information-based item selection. We show that most pairwise comparisons among 12 models on a standard LLM benchmark leaderboard are not statistically distinguishable despite differing point-estimate ranks. We further show that point-estimate Fisher information can become nearly zero for many benchmark items because it is evaluated at a single reference ability, whereas posterior-expected Fisher information remains substantially more stable across the ability range. Finally, posterior-expected Fisher information more accurately recovers full-benchmark ability rankings from small benchmark subsets in most experimental settings while matching point-estimate performance for the smallest subsets. We validate the calibration of the approximate posterior using held-out predictive coverage and find that modeling item difficulty as random while treating item discrimination as fixed produces well-calibrated uncertainty in this architecture.

</details>


### [61] [Validation of methods to estimate the uncertainty of buildings energy savings in a controlled numerical setting and Bayesian energy signature with autocorrelated errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25382)
*Léa Gondian,Thimothée Thiery*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文针对建筑能效领域节能测量与验证（M&V）中不确定性估计的关键问题，系统回顾了线性和非线性回归模型的多种不确定性计算方法，改进了基于贝叶斯建模的自相关残差非线性回归方法，并通过大规模合成数据集（数千次随机热动态模拟）进行数值测试。结果表明，所提出的贝叶斯方法能够在线性及自相关残差的非线性回归模型中提供一致的不确定性估计，为M&V提供了一种可靠方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在建筑能效领域，节能措施后的节能测量与验证（M&V）高度依赖校准统计模型，而不确定性估计是获取可靠结果的关键。然而，近期研究对现有不确定性计算方法的准确性提出严重质疑，尤其是在细时间分辨率（小时级和日级）数据下，自相关问题更加突出。因此，需要系统评估和改进现有方法。

Method: 本文做了三方面工作：(i) 全面综述了线性和非线性回归模型的多种不确定性计算方法（包括线性模型精确公式、ASHRAE 14近似公式和重采样方法）；(ii) 改进了基于贝叶斯建模的方法，用于处理自相关残差的非线性回归模型；(iii) 基于包含数千次随机热动态模拟的合成数据集进行大规模数值测试，在受控数值环境中评估各方法的准确性。

Result: 与先前研究结论不同，本文发现所提出的贝叶斯方法在合成数据集上能够产生一致的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 所提出的贝叶斯方法为M&V中常见的自相关残差非线性回归模型提供了一种可靠的不确定性估计方法。

Abstract: In the field of building energy efficiency, the measurement and verification (M&V) of energy savings following energy efficiency measures often relies on the use of a calibrated statistical model. In order to obtain reliable estimates, the estimation of uncertainties associated with this procedure is recognized as a crucial aspect of M&V. Several approaches have been proposed in the literature to compute uncertainties but a recent work has raised serious doubts on their accuracy. This is especially the case for energy data with fine time resolution (hourly and daily), for which the issues associated with autocorrelations become more important. This work focuses on this issue for daily data with three main contributions: (i) a detailed comprehensive review of several methods proposed in the literature for linear and non-linear regression models (including exact formulas for linear models, the ASHRAE 14 approximate formula and resampling approaches) ; (ii) the improvement of approaches based on Bayesian modeling for non-linear regression models with autocorrelated residuals ; (iii) a large scale numerical test of the different approaches based on a synthetic dataset containing thousands of stochastic thermal dynamic simulations. Our results permit to test the accuracy of different approaches in a controlled numerical setting. In contrast with previous work, we find in particular that our Bayesian approach leads on our dataset to consistent estimates of uncertainties. This suggests that the proposed Bayesian approach offers a convincing method to estimate uncertainties in non-linear regression models with autocorrelated residuals often encountered M&V.

</details>


### [62] [Can a small additional claim lower the premium? Credibility orders for collective risk models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25623)
*Jihyun Park,Jieun Kim,Taehan Bae,Jae Youn Ahn*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了集体风险模型中经验费率系统的单调性排序问题。作者发现传统模型可能存在反常现象：增加一个小额索赔反而可能降低保费。为此，他们提出了适用于集体风险模型的 credibility 序，并给出了保证预测分布单调性的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体风险模型中的经验费率系统要求历史经验越差的投保人应获得越高的未来损失预测（即单调性），但由于该模型的历史数据涉及索赔次数和个体索赔金额的多变量信息，这一单调性尚未被系统性地建立。作者发现标准模型可能存在违反单调性的病理现象，从而激励策略性报告行为，损害经验费率的完整性。

Method: 论文首先给出一个反例，展示标准集体风险模型可能违反单调性。然后形式化定义了适用于集体风险模型的 credibility 序，并推导出保证聚合损失预测分布关于历史经验单调的充分条件。通过数值研究和真实保险数据实证分析来验证理论结果。

Result: 标准集体风险模型规范可能违反单调性：增加一个足够小的索赔可能导致保费下降。论文提出的充分条件能够排除这种病理性的违反情况，保证预测分布单调性。

Conclusion: 本文为集体风险模型建立了系统性的 credibility 单调性理论，提供了可操作的充分条件，并通过理论和实证验证了其有效性，对保险精算中的经验费率制定具有重要实践意义。

Abstract: The collective risk model is a fundamental framework in insurance ratemaking for modeling aggregate losses by combining claim frequency and claim severity components. A key structural requirement for a reliable experience rating system is a monotone ordering property: policyholders with worse past experience should receive a stochastically larger prediction for future losses, and hence a higher premium. Such credibility-type monotonicity is well documented in the insurance literature for univariate outcomes under classical random-effect models. However, extending this principle to the collective risk model is nontrivial because the relevant history is inherently multivariate, involving both claim counts and individual claim amounts. Hence, despite its practical importance, a corresponding credibility-type ordering has not been systematically developed for predictive distributions of aggregate loss. In this paper, we provide an example showing that standard collective risk model specifications can violate monotonicity: adding an additional but sufficiently small claim may decrease the premium, thereby creating perverse incentives for strategic reporting and potentially undermining the integrity of experience rating. Motivated by this pathology in view of insurance, we formalize a credibility order tailored to collective risk models and derive tractable sufficient conditions under which the predictive distribution of aggregate loss is monotone in past experience, ruling out such pathological violations. Numerical studies and an empirical illustration using real insurance data accompany our theoretical results.

</details>


### [63] [Edgington's Combination Method for Two-Study Meta-Analysis: An Empirical Evaluation in 1226 Meta-Analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25819)
*Samuel Pawel,Saverio Fontana,Jinyu Chen,Leonie Stoltefuß,Frank Weber,Guido Skipka,Sibylle Sturtz,Ralf Bender,Leonhard Held*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了Edgington的p值组合法在两项研究荟萃分析中的应用，通过对1226个来自IQWiG的真实数据集分析，证明该方法无需估计异质性方差，能够自适应调整置信区间宽度，在固定效应和随机效应方法之间提供了一种折衷方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两项研究的荟萃分析在证据综合中很常见，但面临重大统计挑战：只有两项研究时，研究间方差无法可靠估计，导致标准随机效应方法不稳定。因此需要寻找替代方法。

Method: 采用Edgington的p值组合法，应用于1226个来自德国卫生保健质量与效率研究所(IQWiG)的两项研究荟萃分析。与固定效应荟萃分析类似，该方法在同质性假设下校准，但能根据观察到的研究间差异自适应调整置信区间宽度，无需显式估计异质性。还提出了加权扩展版本。

Result: 在双重检验α=0.05水平上，Edgington方法与固定效应荟萃分析在统计显著性上的一致性达91%。当研究结果不一致时，该方法给出更宽的置信区间；当结果高度一致时，则给出更窄的区间。加权扩展版本将点估计向更精确的研究偏移，同时保留了自适应特性。

Conclusion: Edgington方法为两项研究荟萃分析提供了一种原则性强且实用的补充方法，在标准固定效应和随机效应方法之间占据中间地带。

Abstract: Two-study meta-analyses are common in evidence synthesis but pose major statistical challenges. With only two studies, the between-study variance cannot be reliably estimated, rendering standard random-effects methods unstable. Here, we investigate meta-analyses based on Edgington's p-value combination method as an alternative approach, applying it to 1226 two-study meta-analyses from the German Institute for Quality and Efficiency in Health Care (IQWiG). Like fixed-effect meta-analysis, Edgington's method is calibrated under homogeneity. However, it adapts confidence interval width to observed between-study discrepancy without requiring explicit heterogeneity estimation. In all of the examined meta-analyses, this leads to confidence intervals that contain both study-specific estimates but remain informative. Edgington's method agrees with fixed-effect meta-analysis on statistical significance (at two-sided $α$ = 0.05) in 91% of all meta-analyses, but can give wider intervals when study results are discrepant and narrower intervals when results are highly consistent. Weighted extensions of Edgington's method shift point estimates toward the more precise study while preserving much of this adaptive behavior. We conclude that Edgington's method offers a principled and practically useful complement to existing approaches for two-study meta-analysis, occupying a middle ground between standard fixed-effect and random-effects approaches.

</details>

*Xiaozhu Lin,Xu Huang,Hongru Dai,Xiaopei Liu,Junzhi Yu,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SWiFT框架，通过强化学习结合计算流体力学仿真与真实实验，使BCF仿生机器鱼在未知湍流中仅凭自我感知反馈即可实现精确的定位置航（station-holding），性能显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器鱼在自然水流中接近目标位置并保持驻留的能力长期发展不足，主要原因在于自由游动机器鱼与水流之间高度非线性的流固耦合作用尚未被充分刻画。

Method: 提出SWiFT框架，包含自由游动流水槽实验装置、基于CFD的高效物理一致仿真器，以及系统的sim-to-real迁移流程，通过强化学习训练仅依赖自我感知（无显式流速传感）的定位置航策略。

Result: 该策略在所有指标上显著超越现有方法，尤其是距离均方根误差（RMSE）。同时验证了仅凭自我感知反馈即可在未知湍流中实现定位置航，类似于生物趋流性。

Conclusion: 该自我感知定位置航策略不仅推动了机器鱼控制向实际部署迈进，也展示了SWiFT作为解决水下机器人复杂游泳任务基础工具的潜力。

Abstract: Approaching a target position and holding station in flowing water is a fundamental and critical capability for robotic fish operating in natural aquatic environments. Despite decades of advances in enhancing swimming efficiency and maneuverability, this capability remains underdeveloped, largely owing to the insufficiently characterized, highly nonlinear fluid-structure interactions inherent to freely swimming robotic fish in flows. To bridge this gap, we propose the SWiFT framework, a Swimming With Flow Toolbox that enables the efficient exploration of an egocentric station-holding policy for a body and/or caudal fin (BCF) robotic fish in unknown and turbulent background flows via reinforcement learning (RL). Our SWiFT integrates a free-swimming flow-tank experimental setup with a highly efficient, physically consistent computational fluid dynamics (CFD)-based simulator and a systematic sim-to-real transfer pipeline. The resulting policy achieves substantial improvements over state-of-the-art methods across all metrics, most notably root-mean-square error (RMSE) of distance. Furthermore, we validated that egocentric feedback alone, without any explicit flow sensing, enables station-holding in unknown turbulent flows, closely mirroring the biological phenomenon of rheotaxis. Accordingly, the success of this egocentric station-holding policy not only advances robotic fish control toward real-world deployment, but also highlights SWiFT's promise as a foundation for tackling complex swimming tasks for underwater robots.

</details>


### [65] [Steeringless Drifting: Differential-Torque Control of a Four-Wheel Independently Driven Vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24863)
*Sheng Zhao,Zexin Wu,Dongyang Zhou,Bolin Zhao,Xiaodong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对无转向四轮独立驱动车辆，本文提出了一种仅利用差动扭矩实现漂移控制的方法，通过建立双轨车辆模型、漂移平衡计算和闭环控制器，在1:10缩比车辆上实现了约20°侧偏角的稳态圆周漂移和8字漂移轨迹跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴车辆底盘架构（如无转向四轮独立驱动）在接近操控极限时的自动驾驶控制方法尚欠缺，传统漂移控制依赖机械转向和后轮饱和，需要探索仅利用差动扭矩实现漂移控制的可行性。

Method: 建立包含四轮差动驱动的双轨车辆模型，提出漂移平衡计算方法，开发闭环漂移控制器，通过仿真和1:10缩比车辆实验验证。

Result: 车辆实现了约20°侧偏角的稳态圆周漂移，并能完成8字漂移轨迹跟踪。

Conclusion: 仅利用差动轮扭矩即可实现漂移控制，为无转向架构车辆的近极限控制提供了新思路。

Abstract: Control methods for emerging vehicle chassis architectures are important for autonomous driving near handling limits. Unlike conventional drift control, which relies on mechanical steering and rear-tire saturation, a steering-free four-wheel independently driven (4WID) vehicle can generate direct yaw moment through differential wheel torques. This paper proposes a differential-torque drift control method for such a vehicle. A double-track vehicle model incorporating four-wheel differential actuation is established, based on which a drift-equilibrium calculation method and a closed-loop drift controller are developed. The proposed approach is validated through simulations and experiments on a 1:10-scale vehicle. The results show that the vehicle can achieve steady circular drifting with a sideslip angle of approximately 20$^\circ$ and perform figure-eight drift tracking. This study demonstrates the feasibility of drift control using only differential wheel torques and provides a new perspective on near-limit control for steering-free vehicle architectures.

</details>


### [66] [Amortising Trajectory Optimisation for Residual MPC via Implicit Contact Differentiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24959)
*Daniel Layeghi,Thomas Corbères,Calum Arnott,Aditya Kamireddypalli,Hashim Al-Obaidi,Steve Tonneau,Michael Mistry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于隐函数定理（IFT）的AD辅助隐式微分方法，用于可微仿真中的正则化光滑接触模型，在Mujoco MJX中实现。该方法通过对容忍度收敛解处的驻留残差进行微分，避免了求解器展开和手动组装KKT系统。与展开AD相比，IFT内存随求解器迭代次数几乎不变（变化<4%），而AD增长10.6倍；在256个接触点时内存减少20倍。此外，引入优化器蒸馏用于残差MPC，将批量化全时域iLQR蒸馏为策略，指导短时域残差iLQR，在Finger、Franka和Unitree上六步成功率提升28-98个百分点。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可微仿真方法在处理接触丰富的轨迹优化时存在计算效率低、内存开销大或推导复杂等问题：有限差分法昂贵且对步长敏感；展开AD存储不断增长的计算图；KKT灵敏度推导繁琐且依赖具体求解器。因此需要一种高效、内存友好且通用的可微仿真方法。

Method: 1) 基于隐函数定理（IFT）的AD辅助隐式微分方法：对正则化光滑接触模型，在容忍度收敛的解处对驻留残差进行微分，避免求解器展开和手动组装KKT系统。2) 优化器蒸馏：将批量化全时域iLQR蒸馏为一个策略网络，用于指导短时域残差iLQR，实现残差MPC的摊销。

Result: IFT方法在MJX中，编译临时内存随求解器迭代次数几乎恒定（变化<4%），而展开AD增长10.6倍。IFT内存随活跃接触数和模型维度增长更慢：256接触时节省20倍，16接触+96自由度时节省6倍。优化器蒸馏在Finger、Franka、Unitree上，六步成功率为标准iLQR提升28-98个百分点。

Conclusion: IFT隐式微分方法有效解决了可微仿真中内存爆炸和推导复杂的问题，结合优化器蒸馏的残差MPC显著提升了短时域轨迹优化的成功率，为接触丰富的机器人运动规划提供了高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Differentiable simulation can accelerate contact-rich trajectory optimisation by exposing local sensitivities of task outcomes to controls. Existing approaches either use finite differences, which are expensive and step-size sensitive; differentiate iterative contact solvers by unrolling automatic differentiation (AD), which stores a growing computation trace; or require intricate, solver-specific KKT sensitivity derivations. We introduce an AD-assisted implicit derivative for regularised smooth contacts and apply it to Mujoco MJX, based on the Implicit Function Theorem (IFT). The method differentiates the stationarity residual at the tolerance-converged solution, avoiding both solver unrolling and hand-assembled KKT systems. IFT keeps compiled temporary memory nearly constant with solver effort, changing by less than 4$\%$ from one to ten iterations versus 10.6$\times$ growth for unrolled AD. IFT memory grows slower with active contacts and model dimension, using 20$\times$ less memory at 256 contacts and 6$\times$ less at 16 contacts and 96 DoF. We further introduce optimiser distillation for residual MPC, amortising batched full-horizon iLQR into a policy that guides short-horizon residual iLQR. Across Finger, Franka, and Unitree, this raises six-step success by 28-98 percentage points over standard iLQR.

</details>


### [67] [FIRMGrasp: A Friction-Informed Risk Margin for Robust Grasp Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25049)
*Clinton Enwerem,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FIRMGrasp，一种基于条件风险价值(CVaR)的摩擦波动感知抓取质量度量，用于评估抓取在不确定摩擦系数下保持力闭合的概率保证。实验表明，名义Ferrari-Canny度量认证的53%的抓取在不利摩擦尾部会失去力闭合，而所提度量ε^(β)在排序抓取成功率和抗扰动能力上显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典抓取质量度量假设单一的确定性摩擦系数，无法预测接触表面在摩擦系数变化范围内能否保持力闭合，导致实际应用中抓取失败。

Method: 基于条件风险价值(CVaR)风险度量，定义摩擦波动感知的抓取质量度量FIRMGrasp。通过评估不利摩擦尾部在CVaR折扣均值下的力闭合裕度，得到风险调整后的力闭合裕度ε^(β)，即风险调整力旋量空间的内接球半径。建立了ε^(β)关于置信水平β的单调性、关于抓取参数的可微性，以及概率闭合保证。

Result: 在1,599个LEAP Hand和Allegro Hand抓取上，名义Ferrari-Canny裕度认证的53%的抓取在不利摩擦尾部失去力闭合。名义裕度对摇晃成功和抓取成功的排序概率仅0.53和0.67（接近随机），而ε^(β)分别达到0.63和0.78。在摩擦系数0.2的侧向拉力实验中，ε^(β)认证的抓取成功率达70%，名义裕度认证但ε^(β)拒绝的抓取仅25%。

Conclusion: FIRMGrasp度量能有效识别对摩擦波动敏感的抓取，提供概率性的力闭合保证，在抓取质量评估和排序上显著优于忽略摩擦不确定性的传统方法。

Abstract: Classical grasp quality metrics assume a single deterministic friction coefficient, so they cannot predict whether a grasp retains force closure across the range of friction values the contacting surfaces may exhibit. To predict these failures, we present FIRMGrasp, a family of friction-volatility-aware grasp quality metrics grounded in the Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) risk measure. Unlike standard grasp quality assessors that assume a single friction realization, our metric evaluates the force-closure margin at the CVaR-discounted mean of the adverse friction tail, yielding a risk-adjusted margin $\varepsilon^{(β)}$, the inscribed-ball radius of the risk-adjusted wrench space. We establish its monotonicity in the confidence level $β$, its differentiability in the grasp parameters, and a probabilistic closure certificate that guarantees force closure with probability at least $β$ whenever $\varepsilon^{(β)}$ is positive. Under a calibrated friction distribution, analytic evaluation shows our $\varepsilon^{(β)}$ metric identifies friction-sensitive grasps that the nominal Ferrari-Canny epsilon rates as high-quality, and we compare against the nominal epsilon and recent differentiable baselines. Across 1,599 LEAP Hand and Allegro Hand grasps, 53% of the grasps the nominal Ferrari-Canny margin certifies lose force closure in the adverse friction tail. On the same set, the nominal margin separates realized shake and pick success with probabilities of only 0.53 and 0.67, near chance on shake success, whereas $\varepsilon^{(β)}$ orders the pair correctly with probabilities of 0.63 and 0.78, respectively. In simulated lift trials with gravity enabled at an adverse friction coefficient of 0.2, grasps $\varepsilon^{(β)}$ certifies reach a 70% success rate under lateral pull, against 25% for grasps the nominal margin certifies but $\varepsilon^{(β)}$ rejects.

</details>


### [68] [Motion Generation With Environmental Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25053)
*Előd Páll,Oliver Brock*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出环境约束利用（ECE）方法，通过有意与环境接触来降低运动规划的维度与计算复杂度，结合RRT规划器在真实场景中验证了其提升规划效率与鲁棒性的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维空间和不确定环境中传统无碰撞运动规划面临维度灾难和计算复杂度过高的问题。

Method: 提出环境约束利用（ECE），将主动接触环境作为规划策略的一部分，以此降维并简化搜索空间；集成到RRT系列规划器中，偏置采样到任务相关区域，并利用接触来减少不确定性。

Result: 基于RRT的规划器在ECE框架下显著提升了规划效率与执行鲁棒性，并在真实机器人应用中验证了实用性。

Conclusion: ECE能有效简化运动规划，在复杂环境中提升适应性与性能，是对传统无碰撞范式的重要补充与扩展。

Abstract: Robot motion planning faces challenges in high-dimensional spaces and uncertain environments, often constrained by the need for collision-free motions. We advocate an alternative approach, Environmental Constraint Exploitation (ECE), where deliberate contact with the environment simplifies planning by reducing dimensionality and computational complexity. By integrating ECE into motion planning algorithms, we bias exploration to task-relevant regions and leverage contact for uncertainty reduction to improve robustness during execution. We evaluate ECE benefits with RRT-based planners and demonstrate their practical benefits in a real-world application. This work consolidates and extends prior research, showcasing how ECE simplifies motion planning while enhancing adaptability and performance in complex environments.

</details>


### [69] [Hybrid Artificial Potential Fields and Spatio-Temporal Transformers for Real-Time AUV Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25056)
*Khadija Rais,Abdelmadjid Benmachiche,Imene Soualmia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对13种AUV路径规划算法进行了综合比较，重点提出了一种混合APF+时空Transformer方法。在五种水下场景中，所有算法均100%完成任务，但混合方法在路径最优性（平均943.15单位）、碰撞率（0.031）和计算时间（0.96秒）方面表现最佳，推荐用于实时AUV导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV在复杂非结构化水下环境中运行，高效安全的路径规划对任务成功和能量节约至关重要，现有各类算法在路径最优性、避障能力和计算开销之间存在显著权衡，亟需一种能够平衡这些指标的综合性解决方案。

Method: 对13种路径规划算法（包括经典图搜索A*、Dijkstra，采样RRT*，元启发式PSO、GA、ACO、BCO，以及学习型架构）进行系统比较，重点提出了一种结合人工势场法（APF）与时空Transformer的混合方法，在高分辨率水下地形图上通过五种导航场景进行评估。

Result: 所有算法均实现100%任务完成率。混合APF+ST-Transformer取得了最短平均路径（943.15单位）、低碰撞率（0.031）和高效计算时间（0.96秒），优于其他方法。经典算法路径过长且延迟高，元启发式方法轨迹复杂不适合严格能量约束，单独学习模型需回退机制。

Conclusion: 混合APF+ST框架被推荐作为实时AUV导航的主要方法，它在反应式避障与全局路径最优性之间实现了良好平衡，为资源受限的水下系统提供了稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous Underwater Vehicles (AUVs) operate in complex, unstructured environments where efficient and safe path planning is critical for mission success and energy conservation. This paper presents a comprehensive comparative evaluation of thirteen path planning algorithms, ranging from classical graph-search methods (A*, Dijkstra) and sampling-based approaches (RRT*) to metaheuristics (PSO, GA, ACO, BCO) and learning-based architectures. Special emphasis is placed on a proposed hybrid approach combining Artificial Potential Fields (APF) with a Spatio-Temporal (ST) Transformer. Evaluated across five navigation scenarios on high-resolution underwater terrain maps, all algorithms achieved 100\% task completion; however, significant trade-offs emerged in path optimality, collision avoidance, and computational load. The Hybrid APF + ST-Transformer demonstrated superior balanced performance, achieving the shortest average path length (943.15 units), a low collision rate (0.031), and efficient computation time (0.96 s), outperforming standalone learning models, which required fallback mechanisms and classical methods that incurred higher latency. While classical algorithms guaranteed collision-free paths, their excessive path lengths and processing times render them less suitable for dynamic underwater operations. Conversely, metaheuristic approaches introduced trajectory complexity unsuitable for strict energy constraints. Based on these findings, the Hybrid APF + ST framework is recommended as a principal approach for real-time AUV navigation, offering a robust solution that harmonizes reactive obstacle avoidance with global path optimality in resource-constrained underwater systems.

</details>


### [70] [Input Shaping for Point-to-Point Motion with a Continuum Robot Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25071)
*Rodolfo Hdz. Ibarra,Karan Baker,Parsa Molaei,Adrian Stein,Hunter B. Gilbert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对缆索驱动连续体机器人在点对点运动末端的残余振动问题，提出应用时延滤波器作为输入整形器来抑制振动模态激励。基于线性系统模型设计了非鲁棒和鲁棒两种输入整形器，并与速度驱动脉冲输入进行对比。实验结果表明，输入整形器能有效减小超调量和稳定时间，且鲁棒整形器效果更优，为连续体机器人的精确鲁棒控制提供了重要参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缆索驱动连续体机器人手臂是一种欠驱动机构，在执行点对点（rest-to-rest）机动时末端会产生残余振动，影响定位精度和运动稳定性。现有速度驱动脉冲输入方法无法有效抑制这种振动，需要寻找更优的振动抑制方案。

Method: 将时延滤波器（time-delay filter）作为输入整形器应用于系统，基于线性系统模型设计了两种输入整形器：（1）非鲁棒时延滤波器；（2）鲁棒时延滤波器。通过对比速度驱动脉冲输入，在连续体机器人上进行实验验证。

Result: 与速度驱动脉冲输入相比，两种输入整形器均能有效降低末端超调量和稳定时间，显著减少机动末端振荡。鲁棒整形器的性能优于非鲁棒整形器。

Conclusion: 输入整形方法对抑制连续体机器人残余振动有效且实用，鲁棒整形器能进一步提升控制效果。该成果对实现连续体机器人在任意末端执行器轨迹下的精确鲁棒控制具有重要意义。

Abstract: A cable-driven continuum robot arm is an underactuated mechanism and may suffer residual vibration at the end of a rest-to-rest maneuver. In this work, a time-delay filter is applied as an input shaper to the system to eliminate the excitation of vibratory modes. A non-robust and a robust time-delay filter are designed based on a linear system model and demonstrate improved response compared to a velocity-driven pulse input. Experimental results using the continuum robot validate the application of the input shaper, with reduced overshoot and settling time exemplifying the reduction in oscillation at the end of the maneuver. It is also shown that utilizing the robust shaper further improves the response of the arm in comparison to applying the non-robust shaper. These results are significant towards the precise and robust implementation of continuum robots in applications involving arbitrary end-effector trajectories.

</details>


### [71] [Reactive 3D Motion Planning for a Franka Arm via Star-World Workspace Reshaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25138)
*Gia Dcosta,Saayuj Deshpande,Samhitha Vedire*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究Star-World工作空间重塑方法，用于解决三维冗余机械臂（Franka Emika Panda）在安全膨胀导致障碍物重叠时的反应式规划问题。通过聚类重叠障碍物并替换为星形代理，结合动力学系统的末端控制器和零空间人工势场实现臂体避障。在6个PyBullet场景的12次初步试验中，重塑方法在5/6场景成功到达目标（基线为4/6），解决了典型重叠墙案例，单次更新耗时0.68-8.70ms，但存在路径增长、近平衡态和过激进合并导致通道关闭等局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全膨胀会导致相邻障碍物重叠，违反基于调制的反应式规划器所需的障碍物互不重叠假设，因此需要一种能够处理重叠障碍物的工作空间重塑方法。

Method: 在每个更新周期，将相交的膨胀障碍物聚类并替换为星形（star-shaped）代理，然后基于动力学系统的末端执行器控制器进行评估，同时结合零空间人工势场项实现臂体避障。

Result: 在6个PyBullet场景的12次初步试验中，重塑方法在5/6场景成功到达目标（基线为4/6），解决了典型重叠墙案例，单次更新耗时0.68-8.70ms，但路径增长、在2个场景出现近平衡态，且过激进合并关闭了可通行通道。

Conclusion: 结果表明将Star-World工作空间几何保证迁移到通过逆运动学控制的冗余机械臂具有前景，但也存在实际局限性。

Abstract: Safety inflation can cause nearby obstacles to overlap, violating the disjoint-obstacle assumptions used by many modulation-based reactive planners. We investigate Star-World workspace reshaping for three-dimensional reactive control of a Franka Emika Panda manipulator. At each update, intersecting inflated obstacles are clustered and replaced by star-shaped proxies before a dynamical-system-based end-effector controller is evaluated. A null-space artificial-potential-field term provides complementary arm-body avoidance. We compare reshaped and unreshaped obstacle representations in six PyBullet scenarios using goal attainment, path-length ratio, and computation time. In this preliminary 12-trial evaluation, reshaping reaches the goal in five of six scenarios, compared with four of six for the unreshaped baseline. It resolves the canonical overlapping-wall case and requires 0.68--8.70\,ms per workspace update for scenes containing one to seven obstacles. However, it also increases path length, produces near-equilibria in two cases, and closes a navigable corridor through over-aggressive merging. These results show both the promise and the practical limitations of transferring Star-World guarantees from workspace geometry to a redundant manipulator controlled through inverse kinematics.

</details>


### [72] [Decentralized Scalable Exploration via Emergent Adaptive Lévy Walks on Minimal-Sensing Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25195)
*Wai Lun Leong,Teo Swee Huat Rodney*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对重量小于50克、配备稀疏局部感知的纳米无人机，提出了一种轻量级传感器驱动Lévy游走（SDLW）控制器，通过离散Lévy步长采样和基于冯·米塞斯分布的传感器反应航向策略，在无通信条件下实现多机协同探索，在多种仿真环境中覆盖率和碰撞率均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掌上纳米无人机在感知、计算和续航能力上严重受限，实现高效自主探索极具挑战性，亟需一种轻量级、可扩展的多机探索框架。

Method: 提出传感器驱动Lévy游走（SDLW）控制器：每个机器人从均匀先验中独立采样Lévy指数以多样化探索行为（无需机间通信）；利用方向性距离测量，基于冯·米塞斯分布选择航向，偏向开放方向同时保持超扩散探索特性；控制器计算成本恒定，支持可扩展多机探索。

Result: 在开放场地、房间走廊和杂乱环境三种仿真场景中，覆盖率分别提升79.6%、43.1%和13.6%，碰撞率分别降低13.0%、7.1%和1.4%（与均匀航向Lévy游走基线相比）。

Conclusion: 该工作为资源受限的纳米无人机提供了实用的多机器人可扩展探索框架，验证了在极低感知和计算条件下实现高效协同探索的可行性。

Abstract: Efficient autonomous exploration with palm-sized nano-UAVs remains challenging due to severe limitations in sensing, computation, and flight endurance. We present a lightweight sensor-driven Lévy walk (SDLW) controller for aerial robots weighing under 50 grams and equipped with sparse local sensing. The method combines discrete Lévy step-length sampling with a sensor-reactive heading policy using directional range measurements. Each robot independently samples its Lévy exponent from a uniform prior to diversify exploration without inter-robot communication for exploration control. Each robot then selects headings using a von Mises distribution that biases motion toward open directions while preserving superdiffusive exploration properties. The controller operates at constant computational cost, enabling scalable multi-UAV exploration. Simulation results show coverage improvements of 79.6% in open arenas, 43.1% in rooms-and-corridors layouts, and 13.6% in cluttered environments, with collision reductions of 13.0%, 7.1%, and 1.4%, respectively, relative to a uniform-heading Lévy walk baseline. This work provides a practical framework for scalable multi-robot exploration on minimal-sensing, resource-constrained nano-UAVs.

</details>


### [73] [Leveraging Semantic Maps for City-Scale Cross-View Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25215)
*Ethan Fahnestock,Erick Fuentes,Philip R Osteen,Nicholas Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用OpenStreetMap丰富语义信息和VLM的机器人跨视角定位方法，通过蒸馏轻量级匹配器实现高效全局定位，并在多种恶劣环境下验证了泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨视角定位方法要么忽略语义信息直接匹配全景图与俯视图，要么将语义压缩为少量固定类别，无法充分利用OpenStreetMap等先验数据中的丰富语义信息。因此需要一种能够提取机器人视角中有用语义信息并与大规模语义地图高效关联的方法。

Method: 利用VLM从全景图中提取相关地标，并识别与先验俯视图地标间的对应关系；为解决VLM在大规模地图中计算效率低的问题，将VLM蒸馏为轻量级匹配器，一次性计算地图中所有实体的对应关系；将匹配结果构建为观测似然，通过贝叶斯滤波器随时间融合，生成时间序列上的位姿估计。

Result: 在单个城市晴好天气数据上训练，能泛化到不同地点、光照、天气（如暴风雪、夜间）等挑战性场景。发布了涵盖11个环境的语义提取数据集和评估轨迹。

Conclusion: VLM结合语义先验能有效实现跨视角定位，蒸馏轻量匹配器解决了大规模地图下的缩放问题，该方法在多种环境条件下展现了良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: We want robots to localize in previously untraversed environments against commonly available prior data. Rich semantic data available from OpenStreetMap can be useful in this task. However, existing methods either ignore this semantic information, directly matching panoramas and overhead imagery, or dramatically compress the semantic information, working with a small set of fixed classes. To leverage this rich semantic information, two challenges need to be overcome. First, useful semantic information needs to be extracted from the robot's egocentric observations. Second, the observed information must be quickly associated with the large prior semantic map (e.g., up to 628 km^2). We show that VLMs are effective at both extracting relevant landmarks from panoramas, and identifying feasible correspondences between these landmarks and prior overhead landmarks. However, using VLMs to propose all correspondences scales poorly as the number of mapped landmarks increases. Instead, we propose distilling a lightweight matcher from a VLM which computes correspondences for all entities in a map. We use this output to form an observation likelihood which is fused over time with a Bayes filter to create a time series of pose estimates. To support further investigation into generalizable cross-view methods that leverage semantic information, we release a dataset of extracted semantics and evaluation trajectories spanning eleven environments, including panoramas we collected in a snowstorm and at night in Boston. We demonstrate our method, trained on a single city's fair-weather data, generalizes across location, lighting, weather, and other challenges. Code and datasets are available at https://efahnestock.github.io/loci/.

</details>


### [74] [SONG: A Photorealistic 3D Gaussian Simulation Platform for Benchmarking Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25219)
*Weiqi Huang,Dianyi Yang,Jiaxin Li,Shuangyi Dong,Hao Xu,Zan Wang,Wei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SONG是一个基于3D高斯溅射的社交导航平台，支持视觉观察、逼真的场景与行人表示，通过大语言模型生成语义轨迹并合成全身运动。该平台附带SONG-Bench基准测试和多维评估指标，系统评估表明视觉社交导航远未解决，安全缺陷先于社交礼仪，真实数据比模型规模更重要，且在该平台数据上微调能有效提升真实环境成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交导航仿真平台无法同时提供视觉观察、移动人体化身以及逼真的外观和行人行为，限制了视觉社交导航的研究发展。

Method: SONG平台采用3D高斯溅射进行场景和化身表示，利用大语言模型生成语义引导的行人轨迹，并通过轨迹条件生成器合成全身运动，实现连续自然的行人行为。

Result: 系统评估表明：(a) 视觉社交导航远未解决；(b) 安全缺陷优先于社交礼仪问题；(c) 真实世界数据比模型规模更重要。在该平台数据上微调能有效提升真实环境中的成功率。

Conclusion: SONG为下一代视觉社交导航研究提供了一个忠实且严格的测试平台。

Abstract: Social navigation has progressed from simplified 2D environments toward a more general vision-based setting, in which a robot needs to achieve socially compliant behavior purely from onboard visual observations. Yet supporting simulation platforms have not kept pace: existing options either lack visual observations, lack moving human avatars, or fall short of real-world fidelity in appearance and pedestrian behavior, offering limited support for advancing vision-based social navigation. We introduce SONG, a SOcial Navigation platform powered by 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS). It leverages 3DGS for both scene and avatar representations, drives pedestrians using semantically grounded trajectories generated by a large language model, and synthesizes their full-body motion with a trajectory-conditioned generator to produce continuous, natural movement. On top of the platform, we curate SONG-Bench, a set of evaluation episodes stratified by difficulty, and propose a multi-dimensional metric suite covering effectiveness, safety, and social compliance. A systematic evaluation of representative navigation baselines reveals three findings: (a) vision-based social navigation is far from solved; (b) a critical safety deficit precedes social etiquette; (c) real-world data matters more than model scale. Crucially, we demonstrate that fine-tuning on our curated data effectively improves the success rate in real-world environments.
