We hope our platform provides a faithful and rigorous testbed for the next generation of vision-based social navigation research.

</details>


### [75] [Belief-Aware Influence and Trust (BAIT): Shaping Human Belief During Repeated Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25327)
*Ye-Ji Mun,Mahsa Golchoubian,Shahabedin Sagheb,Yan Bai,Tianhao Ji,Dylan P. Losey,Katherine Driggs-Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了BAIT控制器，通过分层粒子滤波推断人类信念演变，结合信念感知的MPPI规划器，在长期影响与人类信任之间显式优化权衡，在仿真、用户实验和实车部署中实现了与基线相当的任务性能但显著更高的用户信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HRI框架将交互视为孤立事件，导致人类感知漂移时累积任务性能下降；或通过不可预测行为维持长期影响但侵蚀信任且计算不可扩展。

Method: BAIT控制器：①分层粒子滤波（推断快速策略转移和慢速信念更新）；②信念感知的MPPI规划器；③显式优化长期影响与信任的权衡，同时将即时任务性能作为硬约束。

Result: 在仿真、人类受试者实验和真实GEM车辆重复变道场景中，BAIT在任务性能上与通过不可预测性优化长期影响的基线相当，但用户信任显著更高。

Conclusion: BAIT通过信念感知的规划框架，在不牺牲任务性能的前提下提升了人机交互中的人类信任，为解决长期HRI中的适应性挑战提供了可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Repeated human-robot interaction (HRI) requires proactively accounting for humans who continually adapt to evolving beliefs about the robot. Prior frameworks often treat encounters as isolated events, suffering cumulative task performance decay as human perception drifts, or maintain long-term influence through erratic, unpredictable behavior that erodes perceived human trust and relies on computationally unscalable formulations. To address these gaps, we introduce the Belief- Aware Influence and Trust (BAIT) controller. BAIT integrates a hierarchical particle filter, which infers both fast human strategic shifts and slow perceptual belief updates, with a belief-aware Model Predictive Path Integral planner. BAIT explicitly optimizes the trade-off between long-horizon influence and human trust, while enforcing immediate task performance as a strict constraint. Across simulations, a human-subject study, and a real-world GEM vehicle deployments in repeated lane-merging scenarios, BAIT achieves task performance comparable to baselines that optimize long-term influence through unpredictability while yielding significantly higher user trust. The video demonstrating our experiments is available at https://youtu.be/GsPfHRujzVs.

</details>


### [76] [SGTP: Sampling-based Game-Theoretic Planning for Real-Time Multi-Vehicle Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25388)
*Zhouheng Li,Fangguo Zhao,Mattia Piccinini,Baha Zarrouki,Yuan Gao,Zitong Shan,Johannes Betz,Chen Lv,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SGTP（基于采样的博弈论规划框架），通过结合博弈论推理与GPU加速采样，实现了多车自主赛车的实时、多样化、安全策略规划，在仿真中达到95.24%胜率和99.35%任务完成率，平均计算时间0.095秒，并支持最多10个智能体的大规模场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主多车赛车规划器难以兼顾策略多样性与计算效率，在激烈交互中无法实时生成多样化的竞争行为。

Method: 提出SGTP框架，将博弈论推理与GPU加速的控制序列采样和动力学 rollout 相结合；通过博弈感知代价函数对采样轨迹排序以生成多样化赛车行为；通过可行性选择显式强制执行赛道边界和动态碰撞避免约束，确保安全可靠的策略切换。

Result: 在挑战性赛道仿真中，SGTP达到95.24%胜率、99.35%任务完成率，平均计算时间0.095秒（多轮迭代求解），并成功扩展至10个智能体的大规模场景。

Conclusion: SGTP有效平衡了策略多样性与实时性，为多智能体自主赛车提供了高效安全的规划方案，并开源了代码与基准。

Abstract: Autonomous multi-vehicle racing requires real-time planning of diverse competitive behaviors in intense interactions. Existing planners often struggle to balance strategic diversity and computational efficiency. To address this challenge, we propose Sampling-based Game-Theoretic Planning (SGTP), a real-time framework that combines game-theoretic reasoning with GPU-accelerated sampling of control sequences and dynamics rollouts. Sampled trajectories are ranked using a game-aware cost to capture competitive interactions and generate diverse racing behaviors. Our planner then performs feasibility selection by explicitly enforcing track-boundary and dynamic collision-avoidance constraints, ensuring safe and reliable transitions between racing strategies. Extensive simulations on challenging tracks show that SGTP achieves a 95.24% win rate and a 99.35% task-completion ratio in highly interactive races, with a mean computational time of 0.095 s over multiple iterative solving steps. We also demonstrate the successful application of SGTP in large-scale scenarios with up to 10 agents. We release our code and provide an open-source benchmark of multi-agent autonomous racing algorithms to facilitate future research. Project page: https://sgtp-racing.github.io/.

</details>


### [77] [Decompose and Reorganize: Planning with Primitives and Visuomotor Policies Learned from Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25397)
*Yizhou Chen,Hang Xu,Dongjie Yu,Yupu Lu,Tengye Xu,Zeqing Zhang,Wei Zhang,Yi Ren,Ben M. Chen,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DR-LfD通过将人类演示分解为基于接触关系的原子技能，并以TAMP兼容的形式建模视觉运动策略，实现了高层符号推理与底层精细执行的融合，显著降低了长任务操作的学习复杂度，在多步骤、未见场景和物理约束任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TAMP在符号规划方面表现出色，但在接触密集操作中鲁棒性差；而模仿学习在视觉反馈操作中有效，但在空间泛化和多阶段操作中能力有限。两者互补的优劣势促使作者寻求一种能够融合二者优势的框架。

Method: DR-LfD框架：首先基于接触关系将人类演示分解为原子技能；然后将这些技能以视觉运动策略或物体为中心的原语形式复现；最后将视觉运动策略的启动、终止和约束建模为TAMP兼容形式，实现跨来源技能的重组。

Result: 在多种真实世界和仿真场景的基准测试中，DR-LfD在多步骤任务、未见设置和物理约束任务上展现出强劲性能。

Conclusion: DR-LfD将学习问题从需要指数级演示数据（覆盖所有可能的技能序列）转变为仅需线性数量（按不同技能类型数）的演示数据，有效降低了数据需求，同时提升了泛化能力。

Abstract: Successfully automating dexterous, long-horizon robotic manipulation requires frameworks capable of both high-level reasoning and fine-grained execution. Traditional task and motion planning (TAMP), while excellent at symbolic planning, is often brittle in contact-rich operations. Simultaneously, imitation learning (IL), while effective in manipulation tasks with visual feedback, is limited by its low capability in spatial generalization and multi-stage operation. To reconcile their complementary strengths and limitations, we propose DR-LfD (Decomposed and Reorganized Skills Learned from Demonstrations), a framework that seamlessly integrates visuomotor policies into a TAMP-gated decision-making system. Based on contact relationships, DR-LfD decomposes human demonstrations into atomic skills, which are reproduced as visuomotor policies or object-centric primitives. The initiation, termination, and constraints of the visuomotor policies are carefully modeled and implemented in a TAMP-compatible form, enabling reorganization of skills learned from different sources. DR-LfD transforms the learning problem from one requiring exponential demonstration data over possible skill sequences to one whose demonstration burden scales with the number of distinct skill types, with limited data for each skill. Through comprehensive real-world and simulation benchmarking across diverse scenarios, we demonstrate the strong performance of DR-LfD on tasks involving multiple steps, unseen setups, and physical constraints. Project website: https://dr-lfd.github.io/DR-LfD-website.

</details>


### [78] [Room-Mediated Co-occurrence for Zero-Shot Object-Centric Semantic Navigation via Frontier Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25448)
*Adam Scicluna,Gavin Paul,Alen Alempijevic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于房间概率向量（RPV）的无训练零样本目标导航方法，通过CLIP将物体标签映射到房间概率分布，利用分布重叠度量计算物体间共现关系，并结合快速行进法进行地理传播和价值图构建，在HM3D数据集上相比图像整体基线方法，成功率（SR）提升3%，SPL提升1.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本目标导航（Zero-shot ObjectNav）方法通常使用视觉-语言先验，但潜在空间中直接的物体-物体相似度往往是空间共现的弱代理。现有方法无法有效捕捉物体间的空间共现关系，需要更有效的语义导航管道。

Method: 1) 将每个物体标签映射为CLIP导出的房间概率向量（RPV）；2) 通过RPV分布重叠计算物体-目标共现得分；3) 使用快速行进法（Fast Marching Method）进行测地线洪水填充传播，构建价值图；4) 根据语义得分对前沿进行排序导航；整体为无训练、开放词汇的零样本导航管道。

Result: 在HM3D数据集验证集上，相比图像整体基线方法，成功率（SR）提升3%，SPL（成功率加权路径长度倒数）提升1.3%。

Conclusion: 该物体为中心的零样本导航方法在保持可解释性和开放词汇灵活性的同时，优于图像整体基线方法，且无需训练即可实现导航。

Abstract: Zero-shot ObjectNav methods increasingly use vision-language priors, but direct object-object similarity in the latent space is often a weak proxy for spatial co-occurrence. We present an analytical, training-free semantic navigation pipeline that mediates object relationships through a compact room lexicon. Each object label is mapped to a CLIP-derived Room Probability Vector (RPV), and object-target co-occurrence is computed from RPV distribution overlap. These scores are projected onto a value map using geodesic flood-fill propagation (Fast Marching Method), with adaptive signal decay, and are used to rank frontiers by semantic score for navigation. Together, these components form an integrated, training-free, object-centric pipeline for open-vocabulary zero-shot navigation. Results show that our object-centric approach improves Success Rate (SR) and Success by weighted inverse Path Length (SPL) by a relative 3% and 1.3%, respectively, compared to image-holistic baselines on the HM3D dataset validation split, while preserving interpretability and open-vocabulary flexibility. Code is available at: uts-ri.github.io/RPV-SemNav.

</details>


### [79] [A Causality-aware Infer-diagnose-refine Framework for Test-time Modality Adaptation in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25516)
*Haoyu Zhang,Yuwei Wu,Jin Chen,Gao Zhi,Zhenxin Diao,Mingyang Gao,Kun Wu,Yongchun Liu,Fan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出IDR框架，通过因果推断在测试时动态调整视觉观测的重要性，以提升VLA模型在机器人操作任务中的动作预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型中多模态融合仍是一个开放问题，因为机器人操作涉及长距离移动和近距离交互等动态阶段，视觉观测的重要性随时间变化，现有方法无法自适应调整。

Method: 提出推断-诊断-精炼（IDR）框架：首先在事实和反事实视觉场景下推断动作，然后诊断视觉观测的因果效应作为估计的动态重要性，最后以无需训练的方式精炼动作预测。具体包括零填充干预生成反事实动作、基于范数的量化诊断因果效应、门控残差融合精炼动作。

Result: 在仿真基准和真实世界任务上的大量实验表明，IDR在多种VLA主干网络上均提升了整体性能，证明了测试时动态调整视觉重要性的有效性。

Conclusion: IDR是一种模型无关的框架，能够有效融入多种VLA架构，通过测试时因果推断动态调整视觉重要性，提升机器人操作任务的性能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models predict sequential actions to execute tasks specified by language instructions, conditioned on visual observations and proprioceptive states. However, how to fuse modalities in VLA models remains an open problem, since robot manipulation involves dynamic phases, such as long-distance movements and close-range interactions, in which the importance of visual observations may vary over time. In this paper, we propose an infer-diagnose-refine (IDR) framework, a model-agnostic framework that can be integrated with diverse VLA architectures for refining action predictions at test time. IDR first infers actions under factual and counterfactual scenarios of visual observations, and then diagnoses the causal effects of visual observations as the estimated dynamic importance, which is finally used to refine the action predictions in a training-free manner. We further design a causality-aware action refiner to realize the IDR framework, including zero-padding interventions for inferring counterfactual actions, norm-based quantification for diagnosing causal effects, and gated residual fusion for refining actions. Extensive experiments on both simulation benchmarks and real-world tasks show improvements in overall performance across multiple VLA backbones, demonstrating the efficacy of dynamically adjusting visual importance at test time.

</details>


### [80] [P3: Probabilistic Policy Propagation for Stable VAE-Based Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25541)
*Liyun Yan,Jianming Ma,Yang Zhang,Shengcheng Fu,Zhanxiang Cao,Keqi Zhu,Yizhi Chen,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出P³（Probabilistic Policy Propagation）框架，解决VAE随机隐变量与PPO策略优化之间的不匹配问题。传统方法使用单样本近似导致代理损失的高方差和偏差，P³通过基于矩的概率方法结合采样校准，实现稳定的分布感知优化。实验表明，P³将数据效率从64.6%提升至>96%，收敛步数减少>20%，并在高难度人形机器人跑酷任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAE的随机隐变量与PPO策略优化之间存在根本性不匹配：PPO策略需要边缘化隐变量分布，但现有实现仅使用单一样本估计概率比和KL散度，导致代理损失出现显著的方差和偏差，这一问题在理论上被长期忽视。

Method: 提出P³（Probabilistic Policy Propagation）框架，包含两个核心组件：1）基于矩的概率方法，用于稳定高效的分布感知学习；2）基于采样的校准机制，确保隐变量不确定性下策略行为的鲁棒性。

Result: 数据效率从64.6%提升至>96%，收敛步数减少>20%，在挑战性人形机器人跑酷任务上表现优异。

Conclusion: P³有效解决了VAE-PPO的不匹配问题，为基于VAE的策略与PPO结合提供了有效基础框架。

Abstract: Variational Autoencoders are widely used to encode high-dimensional and noisy observations in robotics. However, their stochastic latent creates a mismatch with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO): an effective policy marginalizes over the latent distribution, whereas former implementations estimate its probability ratio and KL divergence using only one latent sample. We identify a fundamental but overlooked theoretical cause: naive single-sample approximations in stochastic latent space induce significant variance and bias in the surrogate loss. To address this, we introduce P^3 (Probabilistic Policy Propagation), a distribution-aware optimization framework for VAE-based policies. $P^3$ couples moment-based probabilistic method for stable and efficient learning with sampling-based calibration for robust policy behavior under latent uncertainty. In our experiments, P^3 boosts data efficiency from 64.6% to >96%, reduces convergence steps by >20%. Furthermore, P^3 is evaluated on challenging humanoid parkour tasks and shows an effective foundation for VAE-based PPO. Code is available at https://github.com/ylyem9x/P3_Open.

</details>


### [81] [When Does Legacy Data Start to Help? Emergent Transfer in Cross-Configuration Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25593)
*Tao Wang,Hudson Hou,Yingdong Hu,Yufeng Liu,Qinghai Li,Yingjie Jiang,Yingzhi Wang,Cheng Ma,Richard Wang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究跨硬件代际的机器人演示数据复用问题，发现存在"类grokking"的三阶段迁移模式：旧数据在升级机器人达到最低任务能力前无效，超过阈值后协同训练收益激增，接近饱和时收益递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人硬件不断升级，但演示数据往往绑定特定传感器与执行器配置，遗留数据何时能帮助升级后的机器人是一个实际但未充分探索的问题。

Method: 在轮式人形机器人的两代硬件平台（摄像头和夹爪改变，形态不变）上开展真实机器人操控实验，结合梯度对齐与残差策略不确定性的理论分析。

Result: 观察到三阶段模式：低能力阶段无收益（10.0%→10.0%），阈值后收益激增（花朵插入23.3%→86.7%），高能力阶段收益递减（笔插入85.0%→93.3%）；并在移动双臂浇水任务上得到验证。

Conclusion: 提出基于阶段的决策规则：何时收集新硬件数据、何时复用遗留演示数据，为跨配置数据复用提供了理论解释和实用指导。

Abstract: Robotic hardware evolves over time, but demonstration data is often tied to a specific sensor and actuator configuration. This raises a practical and underexplored question: when does legacy data begin to benefit an upgraded robot? We study this question on a wheeled humanoid platform across two hardware generations, where both the camera and gripper are changed while the overall morphology remains fixed. Contrary to the common assumption that more cross-configuration data is always helpful, we observe a grokking-like transition: legacy data remains ineffective until the upgraded configuration acquires a minimum level of task competence, after which co-training gains rise sharply before diminishing near saturation. We hypothesize that this task-dependent transition is governed by a transfer threshold and characterize the resulting three-phase pattern. Across real-robot manipulation tasks, we observe all three phases: no measurable benefit at low competence ($10.0\% \rightarrow 10.0\%$), a sharp gain after crossing the threshold ($23.3\% \rightarrow 86.7\%$ on flower insertion), and diminishing returns at high competence ($85.0\% \rightarrow 93.3\%$ on pen insertion). We provide a theoretical account based on gradient alignment and residual policy uncertainty, and derive a phase-aware rule for deciding when to collect more new-hardware data and when to reuse legacy demonstrations. We further validate this three-phase pattern on a mobile dual-arm watering task, with results consistent with our predictions.

</details>


### [82] [Shared Voxel-Map-Based Cooperative Indoor UAV Guidance with a Multi-Agent Soft Actor-Critic Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25728)
*Thomas Hickling,Dylan Wynne,Yu Su,Nabil Aouf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合共享体素地图世界模型与多智能体SAC（MASAC）控制器的室内无人机协同制导框架。多架无人机将360°激光雷达观测融合到公共世界坐标系占据图中，转换为紧凑的鸟瞰图（BEV）表示，并为每个智能体提供以自身为中心对齐的局部裁剪。这种"在世界中融合、在自身坐标系中行动"的设计在保持去中心化连续控制的同时实现了多无人机一致的空间融合。策略将BEV地图特征、近场障碍物观测、紧凑目标及同伴状态信息整合到集中训练-分散执行框架中。仿真中，学习到的控制器在走廊导航中达到90.3%的成功率，优于A*规划、人工势场控制器及先前的引导方法。为弥补仿真到现实的差距，仿真训练的策略通过真实数据的离线模仿微调进行适应。在GNSS拒止的室内环境中的真实实验展示了双机协同操作在不同难度障碍物布局下的稳定性。结果表明，共享体素地图表示为学习型室内无人机协同制导提供了有效且可扩展的空间基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GNSS拒止的室内环境中，多无人机协同导航面临空间感知不一致和通信受限的双重挑战。现有方法要么依赖全局规划但缺乏实时性，要么采用端到端学习但难以处理多机空间融合。本文旨在设计一种既能实现多机空间信息一致融合，又能保持各智能体去中心化实时控制的协同制导框架。

Method: 1) 多架无人机将360°激光雷达观测融合到共享的世界坐标系体素占据图中；2) 体素图转换为紧凑的BEV表示，每个智能体获取以自身为中心的局部裁剪作为观测；3) 采用集中训练-分散执行的MASAC框架，策略输入包括BEV特征、近场障碍物、目标及同伴状态；4) 仿真训练后通过真实数据离线模仿微调（offline imitation fine-tuning）来弥补sim-to-real差距。

Result: 仿真中在走廊导航任务达到90.3%的成功率，优于A*规划、人工势场控制器和先前引导方法。真实实验中在GNSS拒止的室内环境实现了双机稳定协同操作，并能应对不同难度的障碍物布局。

Conclusion: 共享体素地图表示是学习型室内无人机协同制导的有效且可扩展的空间基础，"在世界中融合、在自身坐标系中行动"的设计范式既保证了多机空间一致性，又维持了去中心化控制的灵活性，结合离线模仿微调可有效缓解仿真到现实的差距。

Abstract: This paper presents a cooperative indoor UAV guidance framework that combines a shared voxel-map world model with a multi-agent Soft Actor-Critic (MASAC) controller. Multiple drones fuse 360 LiDAR observations into a common world-frame occupancy map, which is converted into a compact bird's-eye-view (BEV) representation and provided to each agent as an ego-aligned local crop. This integrate-in-world, act-in- ego design enables consistent multi-UAV spatial fusion whilst retaining decentralised continuous control. The policy combines BEV map features, near-field obstacle observations, and compact goal and peer-state information within a centralised-training, decentralised-execution framework. In simulation, the learned controller achieves a 90.3% success rate in corridor navigation, outperforming Astar planning, an artificial potential field controller, and a prior guidance method. To address residual sim-to-real mismatch, the simulation-trained policy is further adapted using offline imitation fine-tuning from real-world data. Real-world experiments in GNSS-denied indoor environments demonstrate stable two-UAV cooperative operation across increasingly chal- lenging obstacle layouts. The results show that shared voxel-map representations provide an effective and scalable spatial substrate for learned cooperative indoor UAV guidance.

</details>


### [83] [Tri-Manual Visuomotor Imitation Learning of Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25731)
*James Zhao,Mingyuan Ba,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TriManPolicy，通过DATS重定时方法，使单操作员能演示三臂机器人行为，解决双操作通道与三臂控制不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双人遥操作假设操作员与机器人控制通道数匹配，但三臂系统中单操作员只能同时控制两臂，传统的成对模式切换会导致行为克隆复制接口延迟而非任务需求。

Method: 提出Dependency-Aware Tri-Arm Scheduling (DATS)方法，离线重定时演示数据：保留固定持续时间的局部感觉运动片段，根据任务顺序和手臂使用约束重新定位这些片段，生成同步三臂策略。

Result: 在6个真实世界任务中，DATS重定时后的演示训练的策略展现出更高效的协调性，同时保持可比的观测任务成功率。离线分析表明DATS改变的是跨臂监督信号，而非简单去除空闲时段。

Conclusion: TriManPolicy有效解决三臂遥操作中通道数不匹配问题，DATS重定时方法能保留演示动作的同时优化时序，提升多臂协调效率。

Abstract: Bimanual teleoperation provides an effective way to collect robot demonstrations, but it assumes that the operator and robot have matching numbers of simultaneous control channels. This assumption breaks for tri-manual systems: the robot can coordinate three arms concurrently, whereas a single operator can continuously control only two. Pairwise mode switching may therefore record otherwise independent motions sequentially, causing behaviour cloning to reproduce delays imposed by the interface rather than required by the task. We present TriManPolicy, a tri-manual imitation learning system that allows one operator to demonstrate behaviours for three arms. Its central component is Dependency-Aware Tri-Arm Scheduling (DATS). The key idea is to preserve the demonstrated arm motions while reconsidering when they occur. DATS retimes demonstrations offline by preserving local sensorimotor segments of fixed duration and repositioning them according to constraints on task order and arm usage that are reviewed by a human. The resulting data train a single synchronous policy for all three arms, while deployment requires neither the dependency graph nor the scheduler. Across six challenging tasks performed in the real world, policies trained on demonstrations retimed by DATS exhibit more efficient coordination while maintaining comparable observed task success. Offline analysis further shows that DATS changes the supervision across arms rather than merely removing idle periods.

</details>


### [84] [Cooperative Multi-UAV Navigation in Complex Environments via Systematic Multi-Agent Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25754)
*Yu Su,Nabil Aouf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种多智能体深度强化学习框架，通过协同探索、示范利用、安全课程调度和结构感知泛化，解决多无人机协同导航中的局部最优、稀疏奖励、学习不平衡和跨场景泛化不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机协同导航在复杂环境中面临局部最优陷阱、稀疏奖励、智能体间学习不平衡以及跨场景泛化不足等关键挑战。

Method: 1) 结合记忆访问状态、方向性新颖度估计和惩罚反向传播的感知机制；2) 分层协同示范缓冲区配合分级行为克隆；3) 安全感知的双条件课程调度机制；4) 基于局部几何特征的结构感知门控网络与混合专家机制。

Result: 在混合静态-动态障碍物场景下，该方法在协作成功率、导航鲁棒性、零样本跨场景泛化和动态环境适应性方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了多无人机协同导航中的多个核心难题，具备良好的跨场景泛化能力和动态环境适应性。

Abstract: Cooperative navigation of multi-agent UAVs in complex environments faces key challenges including local optima traps, sparse rewards, learning imbalance among agents, and insufficient cross-scenario generalisation. This paper proposes a multi-agent deep reinforcement learning framework that addresses these issues through coordinated exploration, demonstration exploitation, safe curriculum scheduling, and structure-aware generalisation. First, a perception mechanism combining memory of visited states, directional novelty estimates, and penalty backpropagation enables agents to proactively detect and escape local optima. Second, a hierarchical collaborative demonstration buffer with tiered behaviour cloning manages trajectories by degree of team collaboration and applies differential supervision to the actor network, improving demonstration utilisation under sparse collaborative signals. Third, a safety-aware dual-condition curriculum scheduling mechanism reviews mastered scenarios through back-testing and experience pre-filling during training, suppressing catastrophic forgetting while ensuring both task performance and flight safety. For generalisation, local geometric features computed from sensor readings are abstracted into a domain parameter, through which a structure-aware gating network and mixture-of-experts mechanism condition the policy on local structural patterns rather than scenario-specific coordinates, enabling cross-scenario transfer without exposure to the target environment. The framework is further validated under mixed static-dynamic obstacle settings, showing robust adaptability to dynamic disturbances. Simulation results confirm strong performance in collaboration success rate, navigation robustness, zero-shot cross-scenario generalisation, and dynamic environment adaptability.

</details>


### [85] [Tripody: An Overconstrained 3-SPR-like Parallel Robot for High-Reach Construction Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25781)
*Julien Kindle,Jakub Raczy,Riccardo Balbi,Andrea Alessandretti,Cesar Cadena,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Tripody，一种33kg的轮式3自由度并联机器人，通过将经典3-SPR机构的基座球铰替换为万向铰并利用弹性变形吸收过约束，实现1.7-3.4m伸展高度、32kg连续负载、亚毫米级精度和显著提升的扭转刚度（最高达454%），并成功完成天花板钻孔任务验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有天花板施工多依赖重型串联机械臂，难以在杂乱室内环境部署，亟需轻量化、现场即用、兼具毫米级精度和刚度的替代方案。

Method: 采用过约束3-SPR类并联机构（基座球铰→万向铰），利用分布式弹性变形吸收运动学不兼容性；设计定制直线执行器；构建包含SE(3)状态估计、正运动学和任务空间控制的控制栈。

Result: 在1.7m、2.6m、3.4m高度，扭转刚度分别提升67%、196%、454%；闭环定位误差<0.6mm；纯模型外推95分位误差2.7mm（最大3.6mm）；钻孔15孔位最大相对误差4.5mm。

Conclusion: 过约束且利用柔顺吸收的3-SPR类架构是构建轻量、高可达、毫米级精度施工机器人的可行路径。

Abstract: Many ceiling construction tasks still rely on heavy serial manipulators that are difficult to deploy in cluttered interiors, motivating lightweight, field-ready alternatives that reach ceiling height while maintaining millimeter-level accuracy and the stiffness demanded by overhead tool loads. We introduce Tripody, a wheeled 3-DoF parallel robot for high-reach tasks that replaces the base spherical joints of a classical 3-SPR (3 legs; S: base spherical joint; P: actuated prismatic joint; R: end-effector revolute joint) morphology with universal joints, intentionally overconstraining the mechanism; small, distributed elastic deflections absorb the resulting incompatibilities, preserving predominantly translational motion. The 33kg system extends from 1.7m to 3.4m in height, supports a continuous 32kg payload, and offers a modular end-effector interface for ceiling operations. We detail the mechanical design - including custom linear actuators and a kinematic-compatibility analysis - and a control stack for accurate positioning that combines SE(3) state estimation, forward kinematics, and task-space control. In experiments, Tripody exhibits similar in-plane stiffness to a spherical-base variant but substantially higher torsional stiffness - an increase of 67% at 1.7m, 196% at 2.6m, and 454% at 3.4m - while maintaining negligible cross-axis coupling. Closed-loop positioning with a total station converges below 0.6mm across the entire workspace; pure model extrapolation achieves a 95th-percentile error of 2.7mm (max 3.6mm). Finally, we demonstrate task-level ceiling-drilling feasibility in an open-loop study by drilling a 15-hole pattern with 4.5mm maximum relative hole-position error after rigid alignment. These results support overconstrained, compliance-absorbing 3-SPR-like architectures as a practical path to lightweight, high- reach, millimeter-accurate construction robots.

</details>


### [86] [Motion-Acceleration Calibration and Compensation in IMUs without External Equipment for Attitude Estimation Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25784)
*Fabian Arzberger,Andreas Nüchter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用陀螺仪测量值补偿惯性测量单元（IMU）在运动中产生的向心加速度和切向加速度的方法，并引入了一种无需外部设备的标定方法，可估计IMU内部参数（轴偏差、偏置、尺度）以及从基座到IMU的外部位移向量，从而提升依赖重力方向的姿态估计滤波器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在运动系统中，IMU距离系统基座越远，额外加速度（向心加速度和切向加速度）对局部重力测量的干扰越强，导致姿态估计误差累积。现有滤波器（如Madgwick、Mahony）在高角速度时倾向于依赖陀螺仪积分，但长期误差会沿重力方向累积，需要更鲁棒的补偿方法。

Method: 利用陀螺仪测量值对IMU运动引起的向心加速度和切向加速度进行补偿；同时提出一种标定方法，无需外部设备即可估计IMU的轴偏差、偏置、尺度等内部参数以及基座到IMU的外部位移向量。

Result: 仿真和真实世界实验表明，该方法能够改进任何依赖重力方向的姿态估计滤波器，并在高动态系统和IMU无法置于旋转中心的系统中（如球形移动测绘系统）验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 通过补偿运动诱导的附加加速度并引入无需外部设备的标定方法，显著提升了姿态估计在动态场景下的精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Attitude estimation based on inertial sensing requires measurements of local angular velocities and local gravity via gyroscopes and accelerometers.
  However, during the motion of a mobile system the inertial measurement unit (IMU) will be subject to additional accelerations which skews the measurement of local gravity.
  This effect gets amplified the further away the IMU is from the base of the system.
  Many attitude estimation filters, such as "Madgwick" or "Mahony", account for this by relying more on gyroscope integration for periods of high angular velocity.
  However, this approach is prone to accumulate long term error especially around the gravity vector.
  In this work we utilize the gyroscope measurements to compensate the additional accelerations induced by the motion of the system, i.e., centripetal- and tangential-accelerations.
  Additionally, we introduce a calibration method that estimates intrinsic IMU parameters such as axes misalignment, bias, scale, as well as the extrinsic base-to-IMU vector without the necessity for additional external equipment.
  Our evaluation in simulation as well as in the real-world shows that this method improves any attitude filter that relies on the direction of gravity.
  Furthermore we demonstrate the effectivenes on highly dynamic systems, and systems that are unable to put the IMU at the center of rotation, using our real-world spherical mobile mapping system.

</details>


### [87] [Transformer Transformer: A Unified Model for Motion-Conditioned Robot Co-design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25798)
*Huy Ha,C. Karen Liu,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Transformer Transformer，一种基于扩散Transformer的机器人协同设计框架，通过对机器人形态、状态和动作进行统一分词化（RoboTokens），利用动力学自引导（Dynamics Self-Guidance）实现零样本优化机器人设计以跟踪目标轨迹，并在多种形态空间中取得优于进化方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作性能中一个常被忽视的因素是机器人自身的形态设计，现有方法通常针对特定奖励函数过拟合，缺乏跨形态、跨任务的通用性。作者旨在解决如何生成能够跟踪目标末端执行器轨迹并优化用户定义奖励的完整机器人设计的问题。

Method: 提出Transformer Transformer框架：1) RoboTokens统一分词化，将机器人形态、状态和动作编码为统一令牌；2) 扩散Transformer作为动力学模型，进行奖励无关的状态和动作预测；3) 动力学自引导（Dynamics Self-Guidance），将奖励无关预测转化为奖励特定的价值预测，用于引导形态扩散生成高价值设计。

Result: 在多个设计空间上的实验表明，该方法能够零样本优化未见过的奖励和轨迹，性能优于进化基线方法且运行时更短。实际制造优化后的ALOHA设计相比原始设计将跟踪误差降低了70%以上。

Conclusion: Transformer Transformer作为一种统一的动力学模型，能够在无需针对特定奖励重新训练的情况下，通过动力学自引导实现跨形态、跨任务的机器人协同设计优化，具有零样本泛化能力和实际制造可行性。

Abstract: An often overlooked factor of robot manipulation performance is the embodiment of the robot itself. Motivated by this problem, we study motion-conditioned robot co-design, where the goal is to generate complete robot designs that track target end-effector trajectories (from human demonstrations) while optimizing user-defined rewards. We introduce Transformer Transformer, a diffusion transformer trained on RoboTokens, a unified tokenization of robot embodiments, states, and actions. The same architecture can be used across embodiment spaces (e.g., wheeled bimanual, quadrupeds, humanoids) and use cases (embodiment generation, cross embodiment controller). Rather than overfitting to one reward function, Transformer Transformer is a dynamics model, whose reward-agnostic state and action predictions can be converted into reward-specific value predictions. These value predictions are used to steer embodiment diffusion towards high value robot designs, through a procedure we call Dynamics Self-Guidance. Experiments across multiple design spaces show zero-shot optimization of unseen rewards and trajectories, improving performance and runtime over the evolutionary baseline. Finally, we fabricated an optimized ALOHA design, which reduced tracking error by over 70% compared to the original design.

</details>


### [88] [Modular Robotic Catheters for Endovascular Aneurysm Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25807)
*Alex Ranne,Jinshi Zhao,Ali Anil Demircali,Songli Moey,Ayhan Aktas,Burak Temelkuran,Nassir Navab,Ferdinando Rodriguez y Baena*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对FEVAR/BEVAR手术中血管分支插管困难的问题，提出了一种4自由度2段可导向导管系统，采用热纤维拉制与激光微加工制造，并设计了可扩展的模块化腱驱动平台。仿真与体外实验验证了其弯曲刚度和可导向性，有望缩短手术时间、处理复杂临床病例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标准商用器械在FEVAR/BEVAR手术中难以高效进入主动脉分支血管，导致手术时间延长并增加并发症风险，亟需提升导管灵巧度的解决方案。

Method: 1）设计4自由度2段可导向导管；2）开发可扩展模块化腱驱动平台以处理系统冗余；3）采用热纤维拉制技术规模化制造高长径比导管，并通过激光微加工软化导管尖端；4）通过仿真评估弯曲刚度，并在血管模型中进行体外可导向性实验。

Result: 仿真验证了导管的弯曲刚度特性，体外血管模型实验证明了系统的可导向性。该手持式机器人可导向导管系统具有缩短腔内手术时间的潜力。

Conclusion: 该可导向导管系统有望为临床医生提供处理复杂病例的工具，缩短未来腔内血管手术时长。

Abstract: Fenestrated/Branched endovascular aneurysm repair (FEVAR/BEVAR) require surgeons to navigate catheters and guidewires into various branches of the abdominal aorta, before deploying stent grafts to alleviate pressure on the aneurysm. Previous clinical studies suggests that surgeons continue to struggle with vessel access using standard commercial instruments, prolonging the procedural time and inducing further complications. In this work, we present two contributions to solving this problem: 1) A bespoke 2-segment steerable catheter, consisting of 4 degrees of freedom to enhance dexterity. 2) An expandable, modular tendon-driven actuation platform that can accommodate for the redundancies introduced in our system. To fabricate the catheter, we capitalized on thermal fiber drawing, a technique that creates high-aspect ratio devices at scale, and processed the catheter with laser micro-machining to soften its tip. We evaluated the system using simulations, where we investigated the catheter's bending stiffness, then its steerability with in-vitro experiments in vascular phantoms. This handheld, robotic steerable catheter system has the potential to shorten the length of future endovascular surgeries, and give clinicians the tools to resolve challenging clinical cases.

</details>


### [89] [HiFi-UMI: Learning Deployable Manipulation Policies from High-Fidelity UMI Data Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25895)
*Simple AI,:,Yuteng Wei,Jinming Ma,Jiawei Wang,Weitao Zhou,Yushen Zuo,Ke Rui,Minglei Li,Jinhao Zhang,Zhikang Pan,Xiang Wang,Haoran Jia,Huan Du,Zicheng Zeng,Jun Ma,Guiyu Qin,Di Zhang,Xiaofei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HiFi-UMI，一种无需外置追踪设备即可达到3mm精度的便携式数据采集系统，通过提升无机器人UMI数据的保真度（头戴式立体惯性SLAM、原生相对位姿、微秒级GPIO同步、超宽视场角），实现了零机器人后训练——仅用HiFi-UMI数据训练的模型可直接部署到真实机器人上，在三个策略族中与遥操作基线性能基本持平（成功率差异-2.5到+3.1个百分点），最强策略在精密插入任务上达到85%成功率的。此外，4000小时预训练将十个未见任务的动作误差降低41%，在StarVLA-QwenPI上进一步提升真实机器人成功率18.1个百分点。开源了HiFi-UMI-2K（2000小时微秒同步超宽FoV演示数据）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实的遥操作数据采集成本高昂难以规模化，而现有无机器人UMI采集方法虽易扩展但保真度不足，通常只能用于预训练，仍需少量真实遥操作数据作为"锚点"进行后训练。本文想探究：能否通过提升无机器人UMI数据的保真度，完全消除对真实机器人后训练数据的依赖。

Method: 设计HiFi-UMI数据采集系统：①头戴式离线立体惯性SLAM实现轨迹精度；②使用原生而非重建的夹爪间相对位姿；③微秒级GPIO触发共享同步；④每只手配备两个超广角相机覆盖约200°视场。系统无需外部追踪设备，即可实现3mm工作空间局部末端执行器精度。

Result: ①零机器人后训练：仅用HiFi-UMI数据后训练的模型在真实机器人上成功率与遥操作基线差异为-2.5、+3.1、-0.6个百分点；最强策略精密插入任务达85%。②4000小时预训练：十项未见任务动作误差降低41%；StarVLA-QwenPI上真实机器人成功率再提升18.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过提升无机器人数据采集的保真度，可以完全消除对真实机器人后训练数据的需求，实现零机器人后训练（zero-robot post-training）。同时，大规模高保真数据对预训练效果显著，表明数据质量与数量的结合对机器人学习至关重要。

Abstract: Learning deployable manipulation policies is bottlenecked by the scarcity of data that is both high-fidelity and scalable. Real-robot teleoperation is accurate but costly to scale; robot-free UMI capture scales readily, and current practice uses the resulting data mainly for pre-training, adding a small real-robot "anchor" at post-training. We ask whether raising the fidelity of robot-free UMI data, rather than shrinking the real-robot fraction, can remove that anchor. We present HiFi-UMI, a portable UMI data-production system co-designed for trajectory accuracy, inter-gripper relative pose, synchronization, and field of view: head-mounted offline stereo-inertial SLAM, native rather than reconstructed relative pose, a shared microsecond GPIO trigger, and two wide-angle cameras per hand covering ~200 degrees. It reaches 3 mm workspace-local end-effector accuracy without external tracking infrastructure. Using this corpus, we demonstrate zero-robot post-training: a policy post-trained solely on HiFi-UMI demonstrations deploys directly on a real robot and matches in-domain teleoperation across three backbones spanning the vision-language-action and world-action-model families, with success-rate differences of -2.5, +3.1, and -0.6 percentage points on StarVLA-QwenPI, OpenPI-pi_0.5, and LingBot-VA; the strongest policy reaches 85% on a precision insertion task, even though the teleoperation baseline is collected in the evaluation scene and no HiFi-UMI trajectory is. Pre-training on 4,000 hours from the same corpus lowers action error on ten unseen tasks by 41% and, on StarVLA-QwenPI, raises real-robot success by a further 18.1 percentage points. We open-source HiFi-UMI-2K, 2,000 hours of microsecond-synchronized, ultra-wide-FoV demonstrations, each automatically reconstructed and validated through simulation replay, as a large-scale, high-fidelity resource for the robot-learning community.

</details>


### [90] [SAM3D-Guided Object-Centric Representation Alignment for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25912)
*Zonghe Liu,Shanyuan Jie,Xiaoquan Sun,Chen Cao,Zetian Xu,Zongsheng Liu,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个基于π₀的物体级3D表示对齐框架，利用SAM3D作为冻结的3D教师模型，在训练时对齐目标物体的3D先验与π₀的中间视觉特征，使策略内化3D信息的同时保持原始RGB-语言-动作推理流程，且测试时无需深度、点云、掩码或SAM3D。在LIBERO上达99.1%，CALVIN平均长度4.11，真实实验在长时程多子任务场景中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型大多基于2D视觉-语言骨干，缺乏对目标物体的细粒度3D理解，尤其在遮挡、姿态变化、尺度变化和精确空间交互场景下表现不佳。

Method: 基于π₀框架，使用SAM3D作为冻结的3D教师模型；通过物体识别模型定位任务相关物体并生成掩码，用SAM3D提取密集物体级3D表示，与π₀的中间视觉特征进行对齐训练，使策略内化目标物体的3D信息，测试时无需额外3D模块。

Result: LIBERO达99.1%，CALVIN平均长度4.11；真实实验中在长时程多子任务操作场景中效果显著。

Conclusion: 通过训练时的3D表示对齐，能够有效提升VLA模型在复杂操作任务中的表现，尤其是涉及多目标的长时程任务，且不增加测试时的计算负担。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for general robot manipulation, but most existing models rely on 2D visual-language backbones and lack fine-grained 3D understanding of target objects, especially under occlusion, pose variation, scale changes, and precise spatial interaction. We propose an object-centric 3D representation alignment framework built upon $π_0$, using SAM3D as a frozen 3D teacher to provide target-object 3D priors during training. Specifically, we localize task-relevant objects with object recognition models, generate corresponding object masks, and use SAM3D to extract dense object-level 3D representations, which are aligned with intermediate visual features of $π_0$. This enables the policy to internalize target-object 3D information while preserving the original RGB-language-to-action inference pipeline without requiring depth, point clouds, masks, SAM3D, or additional 3D modules at test time. Simulation experiments show consistent improvements, achieving 99.1\% on LIBERO and an average length of 4.11 on CALVIN. Real-world experiments further demonstrate that our method is particularly effective in long-horizon manipulation scenarios where the robot must focus on different target objects across multiple subtasks.

</details>


### [91] [DC-WAM: Dynamic-Centric Visual Supervision and Reasoning for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25918)
*Haoyuan Ji,Lingxiang Fan,Shang Su,Yinqiao Lu,Mengkai Shi,Jun Gao,Shuo Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DC-WAM框架，通过动态中心化的未来视频预测（时序差分流匹配+轨迹引导加权+DynaRoute注意力偏置），将RGB预测分支从外观重建转向控制相关的动态建模，在仿真和真实操作任务中提升策略性能，尤其对光照、外观、背景等分布外扰动具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有World-Action Models (WAMs)利用未来视频预测增强机器人策略，但RGB视觉分支倾向于学习纹理、光照、背景等与动作选择弱相关的外观信息，计算开销大且容量分配不合理。近期高效WAM变体暗示视频分支的主要收益可能并非来自预测的未来图像本身，而是训练过程中诱导出的控制相关视觉表征。因此，需要重新设计RGB预测分支，使其从外观主导转向交互诱发的视觉动态。

Method: 提出DC-WAM框架，包含两个层面：(1) 监督层面——时序差分流匹配（temporal-difference flow matching）结合轨迹引导加权（trajectory-guided weighting），强调密集时间变化和夹爪、操作物体、接触区域等局部动态区域；(2) 推理层面——DynaRoute模块预测token级别的动态相关性并将其转换为注意力偏置（attention bias），引导模型关注控制相关的未来token。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界操作任务中，DC-WAM一致提升了策略性能，尤其在光照、物体外观、背景纹理等分布外扰动条件下表现更为显著。

Conclusion: 通过将未来视频预测从外观重建重新定向为动态中心的学习，无需引入额外的模态特定预测或在线输入，即可更有效地提取控制相关的视觉表征，提升机器人策略的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) augment robot policies with future visual prediction, but it remains unclear what the visual modality should learn for control. While photorealistic future prediction provides dense supervision, it also incurs substantial computation and can allocate capacity to texture, illumination, and background variations that are only weakly related to action selection. Recent efficient WAM variants suggest that the main benefit of the video branch may not lie in the rendered future itself, but in the control-relevant visual representations induced during training. In this work, we revisit future video prediction from a dynamic-centric perspective and ask whether an existing RGB-based WAM can be redirected from appearance-dominated reconstruction toward interaction-induced visual dynamics without introducing additional modality-specific predictions or online inputs at deployment. We propose DC-WAM, a dynamic-centric WAM framework that redistributes supervision and computation in the RGB video branch. At the supervision level, DC-WAM combines temporal-difference flow matching with trajectory-guided weighting, emphasizing dense temporal changes and localized regions where the gripper, manipulated objects, and contact areas move. At the reasoning level, DynaRoute predicts token-wise dynamic relevance and converts it into an attention bias, guiding the model toward control-relevant future tokens. Experiments in simulation and on real-world manipulation tasks show that DC-WAM consistently improves policy performance, especially under out-of-distribution perturbations in lighting, object appearance, and background texture.

</details>


### [92] [Physics-Aware End-to-End Deep Reinforcement Learning for Quadcopter Control with Actuator Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25985)
*Ya-Chia Shen,Woei-Leong Chan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究基于物理感知的深度强化学习(DRL)方法，直接作用于四旋翼无人机底层体坐标系输入(总推力和三轴力矩)，在MATLAB Simulink高保真环境中对DDPG、TD3、PPO、SAC四种算法进行两阶段评估，结果表明SAC和TD3在稳定性和探索效率上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼无人机欠驱动动力学特性(仅4个控制输入需要调控6个自由度)给自主控制带来独特挑战，现有方法对底层控制中的执行器延迟和空气动力学效应的建模不足。

Method: 采用物理感知的端到端深度强化学习，直接输出底层体坐标控制量(总推力T和体转矩τ_x, τ_y, τ_z)；构建12状态刚体模型的Simulink高保真仿真器，包含基于Moore-Penrose伪逆的Action2RPM分配和一级执行器动力学(时间常数T_m=0.076s)；设计包含指数位置势阱、姿态惩罚和二次速度成本的成型奖励函数；分两阶段(S1推力悬停、S2带俯仰扭矩和平移目标的悬停)评估DDPG、TD3、PPO、SAC四种算法。

Result: SAC和TD3在稳定性和探索效率上表现优越，PPO样本效率较低，表明执行器延迟和空气动力矩建模对稳定底层控制至关重要。

Conclusion: 研究突出了建模执行器滞后和空气动力矩对实现稳定低层控制的重要性，并提供了一个可复现的四旋翼无人机DRL基准。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), particularly quadcopters, present unique challenges for autonomous control due to their underactuated dynamics: only four available control inputs must govern six degrees of freedom. This paper investigates a physics-aware, end-to-end deep reinforcement learning (DRL) approach that acts directly on low-level body inputs, total thrust and body torques $(T, τ_x, τ_y, τ_z)$, and closes the loop through a high-fidelity Simulink environment. Our simulator integrates a 12-state rigid-body model (MATLAB Level-2 S-Function) with (i) an Action2RPM allocation based on the Moore-Penrose pseudo-inverse of a coefficient matrix derived from thrust and drag terms, and (ii) first-order actuator dynamics for each motor (time constant $T_m = 0.076$ s), including rotor gyroscopic coupling. A shaped reward balances goal-reaching and stability using an exponential position well, attitude penalties, and quadratic velocity costs. Four DRL algorithms, DDPG, TD3, PPO, and SAC, are evaluated in two stages: (S1) thrust-only hover and (S2) hover with pitch torque and a translated goal. Results show that SAC and TD3 achieve superior stability and exploration efficiency, while PPO is less sample-efficient. The study highlights the significance of modeling actuator lags and aerodynamic moments for stable low-level control and provides a reproducible benchmark for quadcopter DRL.

</details>


### [93] [S2A2: Audio-Visual Imitation Learning for Manipulation Tasks Using Acoustic Spatial Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26047)
*Kaneyoshi Hiratsuka,Benjamin Yen,Ryosuke Kojima*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合视觉与声学空间/信号信息的多模态模仿学习框架S2A2，用于机器人需利用听觉线索确定操作目标的声学感知操作任务，在仿真和真实机器人实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声学信息包含物体位置、材料属性及接触/运动引起的丰富变化，但现有机器人操作任务缺乏对听觉感知的利用，作者希望引入声学感知的操作任务，让机器人通过听觉线索确定操作目标。

Method: 提出S2A2（Spatial-Spectral Audio Action）多模态模仿学习框架，整合视觉特征与声学空间信息（声音定位）及声学信号信息（音色识别），并集成ACT、Diffusion Policy、VQ-BeT、π₀等多种策略。

Result: 仿真实验表明，该方法在需要同时定位位置和识别音色的任务中表现最优；真实机器人实验验证了所提任务和框架在真实操作场景中的可行性。

Conclusion: 声学感知信息能有效辅助机器人操作任务，S2A2框架通过融合视觉与多维度声学信息，在位置与音色双重需求的任务上具有显著优势，且具备真实环境部署能力。

Abstract: Acoustic information provides rich cues about object location, material properties, and changes caused by contact or motion. This paper introduces a new set of acoustic-aware manipulation tasks for imitation learning, in which robots must use auditory cues to determine manipulation targets. These tasks require sound source localization and identification for active exploration in robotic manipulation. Also, we propose a multimodal imitation learning framework, Spatial-Spectral Audio Action (S2A2), that integrates visual features with acoustic spatial and acoustic signal information for the acoustic-aware manipulation tasks. We implemented S2A2 models that integrates policies such as ACT, Diffusion Policy, VQ-BeT, and $π_0$, into our framework. Simulation experiments showed that the proposed method is the most effective for tasks requiring both position and timbre. Furthermore, real-robot experiments confirm the applicability of the proposed tasks and framework to real-world manipulation.

</details>


### [94] [$π\mathbf{R}^2$: Reactive Real-time Flow Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26055)
*Sungjae Park,Shubham Tulsiani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: πR²通过引入快速本体感觉通道和异步慢速视觉语言通道，以及延迟自适应流调度，使基于大预训练骨架的动作分块流策略实现实时闭环反应式控制，在GR00T-N1.7上实现约25Hz的闭环重规划，成功率提升高达30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的通用操作策略采用基于大预训练骨架的动作分块流策略，但动作块以开环方式执行，无法对执行过程中的感觉输入做出反应；频繁重规划虽能恢复反应性，但感知到动作的管线（大骨架+多步去噪）延迟过高，导致策略难以用于动态闭环控制。

Method: 基于扩散强制中的每位置噪声调度，提出两个核心思想：1) 将条件分为快速通道（本体感觉，每tick更新）和异步更新的慢速通道（视觉-语言特征），使策略在块内对本位感觉做出反应同时容忍陈旧的视觉信息；2) 延迟自适应流调度将在途动作视为修复条件，每次调用只需一步去噪即可输出动作，使单一训练模型适应不同硬件延迟。

Result: 在GR00T-N1.7上应用于真实xArm6+XHand平台，闭环重规划速度约为基线策略的4倍（A5000 GPU上约25Hz），每40ms基于新观测采取行动。在仿真和真实操作任务中，相比最强基线，仿真成功率提升高达23%，真实环境提升高达30%。

Conclusion: πR²通过最小化对现有架构的修改，即可从预训练策略微调得到，使基于大骨架的动作分块流策略具备反应性和实时性，同时保留大骨架、多模态策略和多动作预测能力，适用于动态闭环控制场景。

Abstract: Generalist manipulation policies increasingly take the form of action-chunking flow policies built on large pretrained backbones. Such chunks run open-loop, so the policy cannot react to sensory input arriving mid-execution, sacrificing \emph{reactivity}. Replanning more often would restore it, but the perception-to-action pipeline (a large backbone plus multiple denoising steps) is too slow: this \emph{latency} forbids frequent replanning and leaves committed actions stale, making such policies ill-suited for dynamic, closed-loop control. We present $π\mathbf{R}^2$, which makes these policies reactive and real-time while retaining large backbones, expressive multi-modal policies, and multi-action prediction. Built on the per-position noise schedule of diffusion forcing, $π\mathbf{R}^2$ contributes two ideas. First, it splits conditioning into a fast channel (proprioception, fresh every tick) and an asynchronously updated slow channel (vision-language features), so the policy reacts to proprioception within a chunk while tolerating stale vision. Second, a latency-adaptive flow schedule treats in-flight actions as inpainting conditioning and emits actions in one denoising step per call, letting one trained model adapt to varying hardware latency. Requiring minimal modification to existing architectures, $π\mathbf{R}^2$ can be finetuned from a pretrained policy: applied to GR00T-N1.7 on a real xArm6+XHand platform, it replans closed-loop roughly $4\times$ faster than the base policy (~$25$Hz on an A5000 GPU), acting on a fresh observation every $40$ms. Across simulation and real-world manipulation tasks, $π\mathbf{R}^2$ improves the success rate by up to $23\%$ in simulation and $30\%$ in the real world over the strongest baseline. Project page: https://pi-r2-flow.github.io/

</details>


### [95] [INTACT: Isomorphic Intent-to-Action Learning for Search-Free World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26056)
*Junhan Sun,Hao Zhao,Guofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: INTACT是一种端到端联合嵌入预测架构(JEPA)，将意图直接映射为动作，无需测试时搜索。在LeWM任务上，零搜索模型达到85.78%-100%成功率，结合CEM搜索以23.44倍采样缩减提升16个百分点，推理仅需2.9-5.5ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前向潜变量世界模型需要通过昂贵的测试时搜索来从期望变化中恢复动作，缺乏高效的意图到动作的端到端映射机制。

Method: 提出INTACT架构：1) 物理意图使用$z_{t+1}-z_t$，部署意图使用$\operatorname{sg}(z_g)-z_t$；2) 采用四槽语法和共享参数的同构架构；3) 通过相同预测器诱导动作律语义而非逐点潜变量相等；4) 非对称端点梯度用于物理后继和未来目标锚点。

Result: 在四个官方LeWM任务上，单轮零搜索模型分别达到85.78%、100.00%、97.67%和97.89%成功率。可选的局部CEM搜索达到96.86%宏平均成功率，采样量从9000降至384(23.44倍缩减)，比纯CEM提升16.00个百分点。共享编码器达到89.39%宏平均。推理时间2.9-5.5ms。

Conclusion: INTACT成功将表示学习与控制结合，无需逐点潜变量匹配或全局线性动力学假设，实现了搜索无关的零搜索策略和高效的意图到动作映射。

Abstract: Forward latent world models predict how actions change a scene, but recover actions for a desired change only through expensive test-time search. We introduce INTACT (INtent-To-ACTion), an end-to-end JEPA that turns action-labeled, reward-free trajectories into a deployable intent-to-action interface. Each transition supplies physical intent $z_{t+1}-z_t$, while a future goal supplies deployment intent $\operatorname{sg}(z_g)-z_t$. The architecture is isomorphic between the local and goal motion-intent backbone-input graphs through an identical four-slot grammar and shared parameters, and between supported local and goal motion-intent families through action-law semantics induced by the same predictor rather than pointwise latent equality. INTACT also provides intact transfer from RGB evidence to action-effective latent intent coordinates and from intent families to their corresponding action-law families. Asymmetric endpoint gradients ground physical successors and fix future goals as anchors, joining representation learning and control without pointwise latent matching or globally linear dynamics. The resulting coordinates support a robust distributional action law: its conditional mean serves directly as a search-free policy, while sampling remains available for diversity or optional verification. On the four official LeWM tasks, one-epoch, zero-search models reach 85.78\%, 100.00\%, 97.67\%, and 97.89\% success. Optional local CEM centered on the Direct plan reaches 96.86\% macro success using 384 instead of 9,000 candidate sequences, reducing sampling by $23.44\times$ while improving pure CEM by 16.00 points. One shared four-task encoder reaches 89.39\% E5 Direct macro and improves every task over jointly trained LeWM, while predicted--expert action-family kNN tracks Direct success at $r=0.954$. Direct inference takes 2.9--5.5 ms.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [Parallel Model-Based Derivative-Free Optimization via Rank-Two KKT Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24813)
*Donghan Wu,Pengcheng Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于反射操作的并行无导数优化方法，利用Sherman-Morrison-Woodbury公式将模型构建的计算复杂度从O(m³)降至O(n²)，并通过坐标轴反射保持插值集的平衡性和等距性，在530个基准问题上验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于模型的信赖域方法在构建二次插值模型时需要求解KKT系统（计算复杂度O(m³)），限制了并行扩展性。作者旨在降低模型参数更新的计算成本并提升并行性能。

Method: 利用最小Frobenius范数更新模型的KKT矩阵仅依赖于偏移坐标的内积这一性质；通过沿单坐标轴反射插值集来保持内积不变，仅改变KKT矩阵一行一列，产生秩至多2的扰动，进而使用Sherman-Morwoodbury公式实现O(n²)的逆矩阵更新；反射在中心化欧几里得信赖域中为等距变换，保持插值集的平衡常数；嵌入主从并行架构中，配合截断共轭梯度子问题求解器。

Result: 在530个基准问题上进行了数值实验，与已有的DFO求解器进行了性能对比。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持一阶全局收敛性的前提下，显著降低了模型构建的计算复杂度，提升了并行可扩展性，数值结果验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Derivative-free optimization (DFO) addresses unconstrained problems $\min_{\x\in\RR^n} f(\x)$ where $f$ is accessed only through a zeroth-order oracle. Model-based trust-region methods construct underdetermined quadratic interpolation models from $\mathcal{O}(n)$ points and solve a KKT system to determine model parameters, costing $\mathcal{O}(m^3)$ operations and limiting parallel scalability. It is shown that the KKT matrix for the minimum Frobenius norm updating model depends entirely on inner products of shifted coordinates. Reflecting the interpolation set across a single coordinate axis preserves these inner products and changes only one row and column of the KKT matrix, inducing a rank-at-most-two perturbation whose inverse update via the Sherman-Morrison-Woodbury formula costs $\mathcal{O}(n^2)$ when $m=\mathcal{O}(n)$. The reflection is an isometry in centered Euclidean trust regions and preserves the poisedness constant of the interpolation set; together with standard fully linear model-management assumptions this supports first-order global convergence. The mechanism is embedded in a master-worker parallel algorithm with a Truncated Conjugate Gradient subproblem solver. Numerical results on 530 benchmark problems compare performance against an established DFO solver.

</details>


### [97] [Extremal problem of area approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24828)
*O. O. Pokutnyi,R. R. Salimov,M. V. Stefanchuk*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究光滑曲线最优分段线性逼近问题，以最小化原函数与插值折线间封闭区域的面积。对于二次抛物线，证明了最优划分是均匀的并给出了最小面积的精确值；对于三次函数，证明了最优插值节点非均匀分布，并给出了两个内点情形下的精确坐标与最小面积。结果可应用于CAD系统中基于最小偏差面积准则的刀具路径优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在激光切割、等离子切割、铣削、增材制造等工艺中，需要将光滑曲线用分段线性折线近似以生成CNC控制程序。传统的逼近准则多关注最大误差，而实际加工中偏差面积（即材料去除量或加工余量）对效率和精度影响更大，因此需要研究以最小化封闭区域面积为目标的优化问题。

Method: 采用变分法和解析优化方法。对于二次抛物线，通过推导面积表达式并求解极值条件，证明最优划分的均匀性及最小面积的精确解。对于三次函数，建立优化问题的目标函数，通过求解非线性方程组获得两个内点情形下的最优节点坐标和最小面积。

Result: 1) 对于二次抛物线，最优插值节点是均匀分布的，且给出了最小封闭区域面积的精确值。2) 对于三次函数，最优插值节点呈非均匀分布；当内点个数为2时，求出了节点坐标的精确解及对应的最小面积。

Conclusion: 本文建立了以偏差面积为准则的分段线性逼近优化理论，为CAD/CAM系统中刀具路径规划提供了新的优化目标和方法，特别适用于需要控制材料去除量的数控加工场景。

Abstract: This paper investigates the problem of optimal piecewise linear approximation of a smooth curve, in which the area of the region enclosed between the graph of the original function and the constructed interpolating polyline is minimized. This problem arises in technological processes such as laser and plasma cutting of metals, milling, additive manufacturing, and in the preparation of control programs for CNC equipment. For a quadratic parabola, the exact value of the minimum area is established, and it is shown that the optimal partition of the interval is uniform. For a cubic function, it is demonstrated that the optimal interpolation nodes are distributed non-uniformly; in the case of two internal partition points, their exact coordinates and the corresponding minimum area are obtained. The results can be applied in CAD systems for optimizing tool paths based on the criterion of minimum deviation area.

</details>


### [98] [Functional H_infinity Filtering for Descriptor Systems with Incrementally Quadratic Nonlinearities under Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25000)
*Rishabh Sharma,Nutan Kumar Tomar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非线性描述符系统提出了一种功能型H_infinity滤波器，采用显式状态空间框架、支持任意初始值、滤波器阶数不超过待估计功能向量的维度，通过增量二次约束刻画非线性，并基于秩条件和线性矩阵不等式给出存在性判据，实现了估计误差的渐近稳定与L2性能有界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统H_infinity滤波方法对描述符系统施加了严格的正则性假设，并采用隐式的描述符形式滤波器，导致实际实现困难；此外，当仅需要估计状态的特定功能时，全阶或降阶状态估计计算效率低下。

Method: 采用显式状态空间框架直接设计滤波器，允许任意初始值；滤波器阶数小于等于待估功能向量维度以降低计算复杂度；非线性通过增量二次约束（含乘子矩阵参数化）刻画，涵盖Lipschitz、单边Lipschitz、单调等非线性；存在性判据由系统矩阵的秩条件和一组线性矩阵不等式给出。

Result: 在所提条件下，估计误差动态渐近稳定，外部扰动对误差的影响被限制在预设的L2性能范围内；数值仿真验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的功能型H_infinity滤波器有效克服了传统方法的正则性限制和实现困难，降低了计算复杂度，并为非线性描述符系统的状态功能估计提供了系统的LMI设计框架。

Abstract: This paper develops a functional H_infinity filter for nonlinear descriptor systems subject to external disturbances. Conventional H_infinity filtering approaches for descriptor systems impose restrictive regularity assumptions and employ implicit descriptor-form filters, leading to practical implementation difficulties. Moreover, existing approaches mainly target full-or reduced-order state estimation, which is computationally inefficient when only a specific functional of the state is required. To address these limitations, the filter is formulated directly in an explicit state-space framework and can be initialized with arbitrary initial values. The filter order is chosen to be less than or equal to the dimension of the functional vector to be estimated, thereby reducing computational complexity. The considered nonlinearities are characterized using incremental quadratic constraints parameterized by appropriate multiplier matrices, which encompass Lipschitz, one-sided Lipschitz, monotone, and many other nonlinearities. Sufficient criteria for the existence of the proposed filter are established through a rank condition imposed on the system matrices together with a set of linear matrix inequalities (LMIs). Under these conditions, asymptotic stability of the estimation error dynamics is guaranteed, while the influence of external disturbances on the error is bounded within a prescribed L2-performance framework. Finally, numerical simulations demonstrate and validate the effectiveness of our theoretical results.

</details>


### [99] [Derivative-free optimization approach for structured symmetric matrices with fixed eigenvalues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25046)
*Carmo P. Brás,Evelin H. M. Krulikovski,Marcos Raydan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种基于导数的无导数优化（DFO）模型，用于求解具有指定特征值的逆结构化对称矩阵问题。矩阵中部分元素被预先固定（零或非零），其余元素完全自由。通过计算对称矩阵的特征值来评估非可微目标函数，并采用确定性DFO方法（尤其是全局变体GLODS）以及多种启发式策略进行求解，讨论了基于Lipschitz连续目标函数的收敛性，并通过数值实验比较了不同方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆结构化对称矩阵问题在多个应用领域具有重要价值，且与稀疏模式下的加权无向图确定问题相关。现有方法在处理同时满足指定特征值和元素约束的复杂逆特征值问题时存在困难，亟需一种有效的优化求解框架。

Method: 构建DFO优化模型，以对称矩阵的特征值计算作为非可微目标函数的评估方式；采用确定性DFO方法（全局变体GLODS，属于方向直接搜索家族）以及多种启发式策略；基于目标函数的Lipschitz连续性证明收敛性。

Result: 给出了GLODS方法的收敛性分析（基于Lipschitz连续目标函数）；通过初步数值实验展示了确定性DFO方法和启发式策略在不同场景下的性能对比。

Conclusion: 所提出的DFO模型能有效求解具有指定特征值和元素约束的逆结构化对称矩阵问题；确定性DFO方法（GLODS）与启发式策略各有优势，为实际应用提供了多种可行求解方案。

Abstract: A Derivative-Free Optimization (DFO) model is developed and analyzed for solving inverse structured symmetric matrix problems for which the eigenvalues are specified. Some (zero and nonzero) entries are preassigned and cannot be changed, while others should be nonzero but their values are not given. The rest of the entries are completely free. The obtained matrix must meet these criteria and have the specified eigenvalues. This specialized inverse eigenvalue problem is relevant to various applications and is linked to determining the graph, with weights on the undirected edges, of the matrix associated with its sparse pattern. Our novel optimization model requires computing the eigenvalues of a symmetric matrix to evaluate the non-differentiable objective function. We apply deterministic DFO schemes, specifically the global variant GLODS of the well-known family of directional direct search (DDS) methods. We discuss its convergence properties which are based on the fact that the objective function of our model is Lipschitz continuous. Additionally, we explore the potential benefits of using several well-established heuristic strategies to solve the proposed optimization model. We present preliminary numerical results to illustrate and compare the performance of the considered deterministic and heuristic DFO options in various possible scenarios.

</details>


### [100] [Functional Subspace Projection for Detection of Coordinated Stealthy Attacks in Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25093)
*Koto Omiloli,Satish Vedula,Olugbenga Moses Anubi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对多区域电力系统中协调动态虚假数据注入攻击（FDIAs）的建模与检测问题，提出了时间聚合攻击模型和核嵌入功能子空间检测方法（KEFSD），通过RKHS约束的函数主成分分析识别异常时间模式，仿真验证了其优于传统残差2-范数检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代电网面临协调网络攻击威胁，尤其是可逃避传统残差检测器的虚假数据注入攻击。现有检测方法多依赖瞬时测量，无法有效检测在每一步保持隐蔽但产生结构化时间偏差的协调动态攻击。

Method: 首先构建时间聚合攻击模型捕捉攻击的时间演化与区域间协调特性；然后提出核嵌入功能子空间检测（KEFSD）方法，在再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）中建模残差轨迹，利用RKHS约束的函数主成分分析（PCA）识别异常时间模式。

Result: 在改进的IEEE 14节点系统上进行的仿真实验表明，所提方法相比传统的基于残差2-范数检测器取得了更优的检测性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的联合建模与检测框架能够有效应对多区域电力系统中协调动态虚假数据注入攻击，KEFSD方法通过捕获残差的时间结构特征显著提升了检测能力。

Abstract: Modern power grids are increasingly vulnerable to coordinated cyber-attacks, particularly false data injection attacks (FDIAs) that can evade conventional residual-based detectors. While most existing detection methods rely on instantaneous measurements, coordinated dynamic attacks can remain stealthy at each time step while introducing structured temporal deviations. This paper develops a joint framework for modeling and detecting such attacks in multi-area power systems. A time-aggregated attack model is first formulated to capture temporal evolution and inter-area coordination. For detection, a kernel-embedded functional subspace detection (KEFSD) method is proposed, which models residual trajectories in a reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) and employs RKHS-constrained functional principal component analysis (PCA) to identify anomalous temporal patterns. Simulation results on a modified IEEE 14-bus system demonstrates the proposed method achieves improved detection performance compared to the conventional residual based 2-norm detector.

</details>


### [101] [Intercity EV Charging Readiness in Türkiye: A Countrywide Scheduling and Economic Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25143)
*Taner Cokyasar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过基于有向无环图的调度框架，评估了土耳其全国9641个充电站在200万次城际电动车行程中的成本竞争力，发现电动车运营成本优势为23-26%，全生命周期成本优势为10-12%，但基础设施投资回报高度依赖充电站规模（5插头为正回报，50插头为负回报），放电率是最关键的经济杠杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 填补新兴市场国家层面城际电动车充电基础设施准备度评估的空白，为政策制定者提供基础设施缺口、充电站规模权衡和采用激励方面的可行见解。

Method: 基于有向无环图（DAG）的调度框架，优化200万次合成城际电动车行程的充电停靠；评估9641个持牌充电站；八场景案例研究（充电功率5-350kW与充电站容量5和50插头的组合）；200次参数敏感性分析（续航、放电率、充电功率、荷电状态运行边界）；三层经济分析（运营成本、全生命周期成本、基础设施投资）。

Result: 电动车运营成本优势23-26%（得益于土耳其有利的燃料与电力价格比）；全生命周期成本优势10-12%（尽管购车价格更高）；基础设施投资在5插头/站时正回报，50插头/站时负回报；放电率是主导性经济杠杆。

Conclusion: 土耳其城际电动车充电基础设施的整体准备度呈现积极但结构性敏感的特征，充电站规模决策和车辆放电率优化对经济可行性至关重要。

Abstract: This paper assesses the readiness of Türkiye's electric vehicle (EV) charging infrastructure to support cost-competitive intercity travel compared to internal combustion engine (ICE) vehicles. Utilizing a directed acyclic graph-based scheduling framework, charging stops are optimized for over 2 million synthetic intercity EV trips across the national highway network, evaluating 9,641 licensed charging stations. An eight-scenario case study examines the interplay between charger power (5--350 kW) and station capacity (5 and 50 plugs), supplemented by a 200-run parametric sensitivity analysis on EV range, discharge rate, charger power, and state-of-charge (SoC) operating bounds. A three-tier economic analysis isolates operational costs, total cost of ownership (TCO), and infrastructure investments. Economically, EVs demonstrate a 23--26% operational cost advantage driven by Türkiye's favorable fuel-to-electricity price ratio, maintaining a 10--12% TCO advantage despite higher purchase prices. However, full-system infrastructure savings heavily depend on station sizing, yielding positive returns at 5 plugs per station but negative returns at 50. Ultimately, vehicle discharge rate emerges as the dominant economic lever. This study provides the first countrywide, vehicle-level assessment of intercity EV charging readiness for an emerging market, offering actionable insights for policymakers on infrastructure gaps, station sizing tradeoffs, and adoption incentives.

</details>


### [102] [A Foundational Perspective for Partitional Clustering on Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25144)
*Derya Ipek Eroglu,Cem Iyigun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对网络上的分区聚类（包括硬分配和软分配方案）进行了理论分析，发现P-Median和概率距离聚类倾向于将聚类中心置于顶点，而平方和聚类和模糊C均值则可能将最优中心置于边上，这些发现对设施选址、网络设计和基于嵌入图的检索系统有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络上的分区聚类在设施选址、网络设计以及现代检索系统的嵌入图聚类中具有广泛应用，但不同聚类模型在网络上（尤其是当聚类中心可位于边上时）的结构性质尚缺乏系统的理论分析。

Method: 对四种聚类模型（硬分配：P-Median和平方和聚类；软分配：概率距离聚类和模糊C均值）进行数学分析，比较它们在网络上的结构特性，重点关注聚类中心是否可位于边上以及分配瓶颈点的意义。

Result: 平方和聚类和模糊C均值可以在边上产生最优中心，而P-Median和概率距离聚类则固有地偏好顶点放置；发现了分配瓶颈点等结构性质对不同模型的影响差异。

Conclusion: 这些理论发现为设计高效聚类算法提供了新方向，并对设施选址、网络设计以及现代检索系统中嵌入图的聚类具有实际指导意义。

Abstract: This study presents a theoretical analysis of partitional clustering on networks, analyzing both hard and soft assignment schemes with different objective functions. Cluster centers are not restricted to vertices but can also be located along the edges. We examine four key models: P-Median (PMP) and Sum of Squares Clustering (SSC) under hard assignment, and Probabilistic Distance Clustering (PDC) and Fuzzy C-Means (FCM) under soft assignment. Through mathematical analysis, we uncover structural properties that differentiate these models, such as the significance of assignment bottleneck points and the role of vertex-restricted solutions in determining optimal cluster centers. Our findings reveal that, while SSC and FCM can yield optimal centers along edges, PMP and PDC inherently favor vertex placement, leading to insights into clustering behavior on networks. These insights offer new directions for designing efficient algorithms and have implications ranging from facility location and network design to clustering on the embedding graphs that power similarity search in modern retrieval systems.

</details>


### [103] [Distributed Nonlinear Equality-Constrained Optimization via Feedback Linearization and Singular Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25193)
*Zihao Ren,Lei Wang,Hongye Su,Guodong Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非凸目标函数与非线性等式约束下的分布式优化问题，提出了一种基于反馈线性化和奇异摄动的统一框架。该框架将共识/可行性残差调节与沿可行流形的优化分离，通过快速残差跟踪动力学替代非局部代数解，实现了仅依赖局部和邻域信息的分布式实现，并在局部二次增长条件下证明指数收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指数收敛方法大多依赖凸性假设或仿射约束，无法处理非凸目标与非线性等式约束耦合时的分布式优化问题。可行性约束是网络耦合的，传统方法难以分布式实现，亟需一种既能处理非线性约束、又能保持指数收敛率的分布式框架。

Method: 1) 针对两类问题（分布式局部非线性等式约束和聚合非线性等式约束），构建反馈线性化动力学，使输出行为可显式指定，零输出动力学为可行流形上的投影梯度流；2) 用快速残差跟踪动力学替代状态依赖的网络耦合代数方程，实现奇异摄动分布式实现；3) 采用显式欧拉离散化，证明其保持指数收敛性。

Result: 1) 在可行流形上的局部二次增长条件下，理想动力学和分布式动力学均局部指数收敛；2) 时间尺度分离足够强时，分布式实现的收敛率可任意接近理想动力学；3) 显式欧拉离散化保持局部指数收敛；4) 在更强流形正则性条件下，吸引域扩展至可行流形的管状邻域，对仿射局部等式约束可扩展至整个容许状态空间。

Conclusion: 本文提出的反馈线性化-奇异摄动框架有效解决了非凸非线性等式约束下分布式优化的指数收敛难题，理论上建立了理想与分布式实现之间的收敛率关系，为实际离散化实现提供了理论保障，扩展了分布式非凸优化的适用范围。

Abstract: Distributed optimization becomes particularly challenging when nonconvex objectives are combined with nonlinear equality constraints: feasibility is network coupled, while most existing exponentially convergent methods rely on convexity or affine constraints. This paper introduces a feedback-linearization and singular-perturbation framework for two representative problem classes involving, respectively, distributed local nonlinear equality constraints and nonlinear aggregate equality constraints. The framework separates the regulation of consensus and feasibility residuals from optimization along the feasible manifold. Specifically, an ideal feedback-linearized dynamics is constructed whose output behavior can be explicitly assigned, while its zero-output dynamics coincides with the projected gradient flow of the aggregate objective on the feasible manifold. The ideal feedback-linearizing input, however, is determined by a state-dependent, network-coupled algebraic equation and is therefore not directly implementable in a distributed manner. To overcome this obstruction, we replace the nonlocal algebraic solution with a fast residual-tracking dynamics, yielding a singular-perturbation realization that uses only local and neighboring information. Under a local quadratic-growth condition imposed only on the feasible manifold, we establish local exponential convergence of both the ideal and distributed dynamics. For sufficiently strong time-scale separation, the convergence rate of the distributed realization can be chosen arbitrarily close to that of the ideal dynamics. Explicit Euler discretizations are also proved to preserve local exponential convergence. Under stronger manifold regularity conditions, the region of attraction extends to a tubular neighborhood of the feasible manifold and, for affine local equality constraints, to the whole admissible state space.

</details>


### [104] [Projection and contraction methods with double inertial steps for variational inclusion problems on Hilbert spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25203)
*Moin Uddin,Mohammed Alshahrani,Qamrul Hasan Ansari*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出三种融合双重惯性技术的投影-收缩算法求解希尔伯特空间中的变分包含问题，分别实现弱收敛、R-线性收敛和强收敛（最小范数解），并应用于分裂可行问题和弹性网络正则化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分包含问题广泛存在于优化、博弈论和工程等领域，现有投影-收缩算法收敛速度有限，且对算子Lipschitz常数的先验知识要求限制了实用性。引入双重惯性技术可加速收敛并增强算法灵活性。

Method: 将双重惯性技术融入投影-收缩框架，设计三种算法：\algoO（单调+Lipschitz连续算子，自适应步长，弱收敛）；\algoT（强单调假设下改进版，R-线性收敛）；\algoDIM（无需强单调性，强收敛到最小范数解）。

Result: 理论分析证明了三种算法的收敛性质；在抽象变分包含问题、分裂可行问题和弹性网络正则化问题上的数值实验验证了算法有效性，并对比了现有方法；数值模拟确认了\algoT的R-线性收敛速率。

Conclusion: 双重惯性技术可有效提升投影-收缩算法的收敛性能，所提三种算法在不同假设条件下各有优势，为变分包含问题提供了更灵活的求解工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose three projection--contraction algorithms for solving variational inclusion problems in the setting of Hilbert spaces, each incorporating a double inertial technique into the projection--contraction framework. The first algorithm \algoO achieves weak convergence under monotonicity and Lipschitz continuity of the single-valued operator, with an adaptive stepsize rule that does not require prior knowledge of the Lipschitz constant. A modified variant \algoT of \algoO attains $R$-linear convergence under the strong monotonicity assumption. The third algorithm \algoDIM obtains strong convergence to the minimum-norm solution without requiring strong monotonicity. We illustrate the proposed methods on an abstract variational inclusion problem and apply them to the split feasibility problem and the elastic net regularization problem, comparing them with existing methods in the literature. The $R$-linear convergence of \algoT is also verified through numerical simulations.

</details>


### [105] [Event-Triggered Discrete-Time Multivariable Extremum Seeking Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25223)
*Victor Hugo Pereira Rodrigues,Tiago Roux Oliveira,Miroslav Krstić,Frank Allgöwer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种离散时间事件触发极值搜索框架，用于多变量非线性系统的实时优化，通过状态依赖的触发条件实现非周期性输入更新，在保证优化性能的同时显著降低驱动和通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离散时间极值搜索依赖周期性输入更新，导致不必要的驱动和通信资源消耗；需要一种能减少更新次数但仍保持优化性能的方法。

Method: 将事件触发控制与离散时间极值搜索结合，设计状态相关的触发条件决定何时更新控制输入；结合离散时间平均理论与Lyapunov分析证明收敛性与稳定性。

Result: 系统轨迹实际收敛到未知极值点邻域，平均动力学指数稳定；数值仿真表明能以显著更少的驱动事件达到相近的优化性能。

Conclusion: 恰当设计的事件触发规则能保留经典极值搜索的核心优化机制，同时大幅减少输入更新次数，适用于资源受限的数字和网络化控制场景。

Abstract: This paper introduces a discrete-time event-triggered extremum seeking framework for real-time optimization of multivariable nonlinear systems. In contrast to conventional discrete-time extremum seeking implementations that rely on periodic input updates, the proposed scheme updates the control action only when a state-dependent triggering condition is met, enabling aperiodic execution and substantial reduction of actuation and communication effort. The resulting closed-loop architecture reconciles two structurally different paradigms: the periodic excitation required for gradient recovery in extremum seeking and the aperiodic philosophy of event-triggered control. By combining discrete-time averaging arguments with Lyapunov-based analysis, we prove practical convergence of the system trajectories to a neighborhood of the unknown extremum and exponential stability of the corresponding average dynamics despite the loss of uniform sampling. The results show that appropriately designed triggering rules preserve the essential optimization mechanisms of classical extremum seeking while drastically reducing the number of input updates. Numerical simulations demonstrate that comparable optimization performance can be achieved with significantly fewer actuation events, highlighting the suitability of the method for resource-aware digital and networked control implementations.

</details>


### [106] [Proximal Gradient Methods for Unconstrained Set Optimization Problems with Set-Valued Maps of Finite Cardinality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25252)
*Ravi Raushan,Debdas Ghosh,Anshika,V. Vetrivel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对无约束集值优化问题，提出了两种近端梯度方法（带线搜索与不带线搜索），在锥序关系下建立了弱最小点的刻画、平稳性条件及全局收敛性，并证明了两方法均达到O(1/√k)的收敛速率，数值实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集值优化问题的梯度方法研究尚不充分，尤其是缺乏近端梯度框架下的收敛性分析与理论保证。本文旨在填补这一空白，为无约束集值优化问题提供可理论保证的求解方法。

Method: 1) 利用弱有效点刻画弱最小点；2) 建立平稳性条件并连接弱最小点；3) 定义非平稳点处的下降方向；4) 提出Armijo型线搜索条件并证明步长存在性；5) 分别设计带线搜索和不带线搜索的两种近端梯度方法；6) 在温和假设下证明全局收敛性，并分析O(1/√k)的收敛速率。

Result: 两种近端梯度方法均全局收敛，收敛速率为O(1/√k)。带线搜索的方法在理论上优于已有的最速下降法。数值实验验证了方法的实际性能。

Conclusion: 近端梯度方法可有效求解无约束集值优化问题，线搜索策略能带来理论上的改进。未来可进一步扩展至有约束或更一般的集值问题。

Abstract: This work presents two different types of proximal gradient methods, with line search and without line search, for solving unconstrained set-valued optimization problems under the lower set-less ordering relation induced by a solid cone that is convex, pointed, and closed. The objective mapping of the problem involves finitely many functions, with each one being the sum of a continuously differentiable function and a convex function that is proper and closed. We present an approach to characterize weakly minimal points of the problem with the help of weakly efficient points of a family of vector optimization problems. Thereafter, we establish a stationarity condition along with its connection with weakly minimal points of the problem under study. Based on the stationary condition, the concept of a descent direction at a non-stationary point is discussed. In view of the line search-based method, we formulate an Armijo-type line search condition and establish the existence of such a step-size. For the proposed methods, global convergence is established under mild assumptions. The convergence analysis of the proximal gradient method with line search provides a theoretical advancement over the convergence results previously established for the steepest descent method in set-valued optimization problems. In addition, we analyze the computational complexity of the proposed methods and show that both methods achieve a convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{k})$. Numerical results are reported to test the performance of the methods in practice.

</details>


### [107] [Microsecond-Class Powered-Descent Optimization via Exact Condensation and Strong Convex Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25260)
*Wenbo Li,Ziqi Xu,Dai Shen,Shengping Gong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出三种结构约简方法，将燃料主导的凸优化动力下降问题从300个原始变量和174个等式乘子缩减为90个控制变量和6个终端乘子，并通过固定预算的外推比例-积分投影梯度迭代在C17数组上实现，求解时间仅68.2微秒（P99=128.1微秒），迭代次数从2744次降至93次，燃料消耗仅增加0.033%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准全状态epigraph形式的燃料最优动力下降问题含有大量状态变量、燃料epigraph变量和动力学等式约束，且纯燃料目标函数缺乏强凸曲率，导致求解效率低下。

Method: 三种结构约简：(1) 添加维度一致的低权重能量项使控制解唯一；(2) 终端状态灵敏度递推精确消除所有中间状态，结合可逆行归一化将30节点基线问题从300原始变量+174等式乘子缩减为90控制变量+6终端乘子；(3) 利用燃料范数与推力球的共享径向结构，推导出由群收缩后接幅度裁剪组成的精确闭式近端算子。最后采用固定预算的外推比例-积分投影梯度迭代在固定大小C17数组上求解。

Result: 在火星动力下降算例中（能量权重0.02，相对参考解容差10^{-3}），迭代次数从纯燃料全状态epigraph基线的2744次降至93次，燃料指标仅增加0.033%；平均端到端求解时间68.2微秒，P99为128.1微秒（Intel i7-10875H）。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于凸核心的快速求解而非新的无损凸化定理，适用于推力集已是凸球的情形，实现了微秒级的实时轨迹求解。

Abstract: Fuel-dominant powered descent can be written as a convex program, but the usual full-state epigraph formulation still carries many state variables, fuel epigraph variables, and dynamics equalities. In addition, the pure-fuel objective provides no strong-convexity curvature. This paper combines three structural reductions. First, a dimensionally consistent low-weight energy term makes the control solution unique. Second, a terminal-state sensitivity recursion eliminates every intermediate state exactly; invertible row normalization turns the 30-node baseline with 300 primal variables and 174 equality multipliers into a problem with 90 control variables and six terminal multipliers. Third, the shared radial structure of the fuel norm and thrust ball gives an exact closed-form proximal operator consisting of group shrinkage followed by magnitude clipping. The condensed problem is solved with a fixed-budget extrapolated proportional--integral projected-gradient iteration implemented in fixed-size C17 arrays. In a Mars powered-descent case with energy weight 0.02 and relative reference-solution tolerance $10^{-3}$, the iteration count decreases from 2744 for the pure-fuel full-state epigraph baseline to 93, while the fuel metric increases by only 0.033\%. The mean end-to-end solve time is \SI{68.2}{\micro\second}, and P99 is \SI{128.1}{\micro\second}, on an Intel i7-10875H. Because the thrust set in this test case is already a convex ball, the contribution is fast solution of the convex core rather than a new lossless-convexification theorem.

</details>


### [108] [A Numerical Investigation of the Rayleigh-Faber-Krahn Inequality for Polyhedral Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25419)
*Josué Díaz-Avalos,Antoine Laurain*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在三维凸多面体中，给定面数约束下最小化拉普拉斯算子第一Dirichlet特征值的问题，通过支持超平面参数化和拉格朗日框架进行数值求解，得到了柏拉图立体、棱柱、十四面体等最优形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题可视为经典Rayleigh-Faber-Krahn不等式的多面体版本，旨在探索在固定面数约束下，何种三维凸多面体形状能使拉普拉斯第一特征值最小化，填补了高维形状优化中多边形近似最优形状的理论空白。

Method: 通过支持超平面对多面体进行参数化，将问题转化为等价的有穷维约束最小化问题；采用拉格朗日框架进行数值求解；在假设每个顶点恰好与d个面相邻的条件下，结合多面体面的有穷维扰动和特征值与体积的无穷维形状导数，进行拉格朗日函数的灵敏度分析。

Result: 数值结果根据面数不同得到了多种最优形状：包括柏拉图立体（如正四面体、立方体等）、棱柱以及十四面体（tetrakaidecahedron），还发现了一些具有多种对称性的非正则最优形状。

Conclusion: 该数值方法能够有效求解给定面数约束下三维凸多面体的特征值最小化问题，所得最优形状既有已知的正则多面体，也有非正则但对称性显著的形状，为理解形状优化与特征值问题的关系提供了新见解。

Abstract: We present numerical results for the problem of minimizing the first Dirichlet eigenvalue of the Laplacian among three-dimensional convex polyhedra with a prescribed number of facets. This problem can be viewed as a polyhedral version of the classical Rayleigh-Faber-Krahn inequality. Using a parameterization of polytopes by supporting hyperplanes, the problem is first reformulated as an equivalent finite-dimensional constrained minimization problem. A Lagrangian framework is employed for the numerical solution. Under the assumption that each vertex is incident to exactly $d$ facets, the sensitivity analysis of the Lagrangian combines finite-dimensional perturbations of the facets of $d$-dimensional polyhedra with infinite-dimensional shape derivatives of both the eigenvalue and the volume. Depending on the number of facets, the numerical results yield well-known polyhedra, such as Platonic solids, prisms, and the tetrakaidecahedron, or reveal nonregular optimal shapes exhibiting several symmetries.

</details>


### [109] [Exact discrete-adjoint optimization of trap timing and placement in a Stieltjes-time reaction-diffusion model: A Galicia case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25450)
*Francisco J. Fernández,Iván Area*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对季节性种群扩散模型，提出了一种基于Stieltjes时间反应-扩散框架的有限维时空控制问题，推导了全离散残差的精确伴随，并通过Vespa velutina（黄脚胡蜂）陷阱控制案例验证了方法的精度、效率和可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 季节性种群模型需要同时处理扩散、非活跃期和突发生物转移，同时保持对局部干预的可微性，现有方法难以兼顾这些需求。

Method: 构建Stieltjes时间反应-扩散模型的有限维定时-布点控制问题；采用连续有限元和隐式Stieltjes-Euler格式进行离散；推导全离散残差的精确伴随；对箱约束惩罚问题证明适定性、可微性、极小值存在性及一阶最优性条件。

Result: 基准测试达到机器精度（低于3×10⁻¹⁵），伴随加速比随陷阱数从1增至4由约7.5提升至30.9；在加利西亚网格上对Vespa velutina的陷阱控制应用后，联合控制优于仅时间控制和参考控制，诊断目标降低0.49209%。

Conclusion: 该框架完全可复现，但不对全局最优性或校准的现场管理方案作声称，且目标降低依赖于未校准的陷阱-死亡率强度，不应被解释为现场捕获效能。

Abstract: Seasonal population models must combine diffusion, inactive periods and abrupt biological transfers while remaining differentiable with respect to localized interventions. We formulate a finite-dimensional timing-and-placement control problem for a Stieltjes-time reaction--diffusion model and derive the exact adjoint of the fully discrete residual. Continuous finite elements and an implicit Stieltjes--Euler scheme represent propagation, compartment replacement, resets and historical phase averages. For the resulting box-constrained penalized problem, we prove well-posedness and differentiability of the discrete control-to-state map, existence of a minimizer, first-order stationarity, and equivalence between direct-sensitivity and adjoint gradients. Benchmark tests show machine-precision agreement (below $3\times10^{-15}$) and adjoint speed-ups from about $7.5$ to $30.9$ as the number of traps increases from one to four. The method is applied to \textit{Vespa velutina} trap campaigns on a realistic Galicia mesh. Smoothed activation windows, domain-normalized moving kernels and the complete adjoint are independently verified. The final two-cluster control remains admissible and has a scaled gradient infinity norm of $2.97\times10^{-5}$ with 120 temporal subdivisions per month. Fixed-control evaluations at 60, 120 and 240 subdivisions yield a refinement-increment ratio of $0.4984$. At the finest level, the joint control outperforms time-only and reference controls, reducing the diagnostic objective by $0.49209%$. This reduction depends on uncalibrated trap-to-mortality intensities and should not be interpreted as field capture efficacy. The framework is fully reproducible, without claiming global optimality or a calibrated field-management prescription.

</details>


### [110] [Optimal Parameter Design for DIGing on Minimizing Unweighted Sum of Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25463)
*Qiuchen Tian,Li Chai,Jinming Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式非精确梯度跟踪(DIGing)算法，通过将其表示为动态线性系统并分解为不同图频率的解耦子系统，利用控制理论中的劳斯稳定性判据，推导出了最优最差情况收敛速率的显式公式及对应参数，发现即使对于最简单的目标函数，DIGing的收敛速度也较慢，因此加速是必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式优化算法中缺乏设计合适参数以实现更快收敛的通用方法，因此需要为DIGing算法探索参数设计的最优方法。

Method: 将DIGing迭代算法表示为动态线性系统，分解为不同图频率的解耦子系统，利用控制理论中的劳斯稳定性判据推导最优最差情况收敛速率及对应参数的显式公式。

Result: 得到了最优最差情况收敛速率的显式公式及对应参数；发现即使对于最简单的平方和目标函数，DIGing算法的收敛速度也较慢。

Conclusion: 加速是必要的，所提出的方法可视为DIGing算法在求解一般目标函数时实现最优参数设计的第一步。
