Abstract: There is no general method for designing proper parameters to achieve faster convergence in distributed optimization algorithms. In this paper, we consider the distributed inexact gradient tracking (DIGing) algorithm with the objective function being the unweighted sum of squares. By representing the iteration algorithm as a dynamical linear system, we decompose it into different graph frequencies and obtain a set of decoupled subsystems, on which we can easily analyze the convergence rate. By using Routh stability criterion from control theory, we derive the explicit formula of the optimal worst-case convergence rate and the corresponding parameters. We can see that the convergence rate of DIGing is slow even for the simplest objective functions, thus acceleration is necessary for general application. The proposed method can be viewed as the first step toward optimal parameter design of DIGing algorithm in solving general objective functions.

</details>


### [111] [Conditional gradient methods on unbounded feasible regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25594)
*R. Díaz Millán,Tuan Thanh Lu,Julien Ugon*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对无界可行区域的条件梯度法紧致限制原理，通过两种方案（固定紧致集和动态更新紧致集）将条件梯度步骤应用于无界可行区域，在保持全局最优收敛性的同时仅需在紧致子集上求解线性最小化，实现了平滑凸目标的超线性收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典条件梯度法的收敛理论主要针对紧致可行集，但许多自然的凸可行区域是闭且无界的，现有方法无法直接应用。

Method: 提出两种紧致限制方案：方案一使用包含初始目标子水平集的固定紧致凸集；方案二沿迭代更新紧致凸集的交集。线性最小化仅在紧致子集上求解。

Result: 平滑凸目标获得标准超线性收敛率；非光滑条件次梯度扩展在曲率假设下获得次线性收敛率；数值实验验证了固定和动态限制在无界可行区域上的行为。

Conclusion: 通过紧致限制原理，条件梯度法可有效推广到无界可行区域，在保证全局最优收敛的同时维持原有收敛速度。

Abstract: The conditional gradient method is attractive when linear minimization over the feasible region is substantially cheaper than projection. Its classical convergence theory, however, is formulated for compact feasible sets, whereas many natural convex feasible regions are closed and unbounded. This paper studies a simple compact-restriction principle for applying conditional gradient steps to unbounded feasible regions. The first scheme uses one compact convex set containing the initial objective sublevel set. The second scheme updates the restriction by intersecting compact convex sets generated along the iterations. In both cases the linear minimization oracle is solved only over compact subsets, but the resulting objective values converge to the global optimum of the original problem, provided the compact restrictions contain the corresponding objective sublevel sets. For smooth convex objectives we obtain the standard superlinear convergence rate of the objective. We also record constructive restrictions based on strong convexity, exact sublevel sets, and epigraph caps, and include a nonsmooth conditional subgradient extension with a sublinear convergence rate under a curvature assumption. Numerical experiments illustrate the behaviour of the fixed and dynamic restrictions on unbounded feasible regions.

</details>


### [112] [On the boundedness of infinite products of relaxed projections: perturbations resilience and dynamic string-averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25797)
*Daniel Reem,Yair Censor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在Bauschke-Tung（2026）关于无穷维Hilbert空间中松弛投影无穷乘积有界性的结果基础上，引入了扰动、混合半空间与超平面、动态加权求和与动态字符串等推广，并给出了反例说明推广到任意闭凸集的局限性，同时建立了一般赋范空间中非扩张映射无穷乘积一致有界/无界性的独立定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展Bauschke和Tung（2026年）关于无限维Hilbert空间中有限族闭仿射子空间上松弛投影无穷乘积有界性的结果，使其适用于更一般的场景（如带扰动、混合约束类型、动态加权等），并揭示该结果推广到任意闭凸集的局限性。

Method: 通过引入扰动项证明扰动弹性（perturbation resilience）；考虑闭半空间与闭超平面的混合情形；采用动态加权和与动态字符串（动态长度）的松弛投影迭代过程；构造反例族说明非循环甚至非几乎循环控制下，推广到一般闭凸集的不可能性；建立赋范空间中非扩张映射无穷乘积的一致有界性与无界性的一般定理。

Result: 将Bauschke-Tung结果推广到带扰动的场景、混合约束类型以及动态加权/动态字符串迭代过程；给出了大量反例，证明在非循环或非几乎循环控制下，该结果无法推广到任意闭凸集；得到了关于非扩张映射无穷乘积一致有界性的独立结论。

Conclusion: Bauschke-Tung结果可以沿多个方向（扰动、混合约束、动态迭代）进行推广，但推广到任意闭凸集存在本质困难，非循环控制下可能不成立。

Abstract: Very recently (2026), Bauschke and Tung extended from finite- to infinite-dimensional Hilbert spaces a result published by Meshulam in 1996 (following an earlier result of Aharoni-Duchet-Wajnryb from 1984) regarding the boundedness of infinite products of relaxed projections onto a finite family of closed affine subspaces. In the present note we extend in various ways the result of Bauschke and Tung by allowing certain perturbations and proving perturbation resilience, by considering a mixture of closed half-spaces and closed hyperplanes, and by using dynamic weighted sums of dynamic strings (of dynamic lengths) of relaxed projections in the iterative process. We also discuss the limitation to generalize the Bauschke-Tung result to arbitrary closed and convex sets by presenting a large family of counterexamples in which the associated control is not cyclic and not even almost cyclic. Along the way we establish a general theorem of independent interest regarding the uniform boundedness and uniform unboundedness of infinite products of nonexpansive mappings in a normed space setting.

</details>


### [113] [Bilinear Koopman-Based Robust Model Predictive Control for Unknown Nonlinear Systems via Contraction Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25658)
*Yuki Higuchi,Kazuhiro Sato*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对基于双线性Koopman实现的数据驱动未知非线性系统，提出了一种鲁棒模型预测控制（RMPC）框架。通过将预测的 lifted 状态重新投影回原流形，构建了误差感知的离散时间控制仿射预测器，并基于鲁棒控制收缩度量设计了 tube-based RMPC，证明了鲁棒约束满足、递归可行性和收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Koopman的RMPC方法多采用线性实现，精度有限；而双线性Koopman实现虽精度更高，但其鲁棒MPC框架仍缺乏。核心困难在于有限维Koopman预测器不保持 lifted 流形，多步预测可能离开一步误差证书有效区域。

Method: 1) 将每个预测的 lifted 状态重新投影回原流形，获得原状态空间中误差感知的离散时间控制仿射预测器；2) 基于离散时间鲁棒控制收缩度量构造同伦 tube；3) 设计带终端约束的 tube-based RMPC。

Result: 数值实验证明了该方法对非线性系统的鲁棒镇定效果，以及在控制性能上优于现有基于Koopman的RMPC方法。

Conclusion: 提出的框架能够鲁棒地满足原始非线性约束，保证递归可行性和收敛到目标状态邻域，有效克服了双线性Koopman预测器在多步预测中的流形漂移问题。

Abstract: Data-driven model predictive control (MPC) using Koopman operator theory is a promising approach for constrained control of unknown nonlinear systems. While linear Koopman realizations are commonly used due to their simplicity, bilinear Koopman realizations can provide significantly higher approximation accuracy for nonlinear control systems. However, robust MPC (RMPC) formulations that account for modeling errors in bilinear Koopman realizations remain limited. This paper proposes a RMPC framework for unknown nonlinear systems with general nonlinear constraints based on data-driven bilinear Koopman realizations. A central difficulty is that finite-dimensional Koopman predictors need not preserve the manifold of valid lifted states, so multi-step prediction in lifted coordinates may leave the region where one-step error certificates apply. We address this issue by reprojecting each predicted lifted state back onto the manifold, thereby obtaining an error-aware discrete-time control-affine predictor in the original state space without impractical assumptions. For this predictor, we develop a discrete-time robust control contraction metric based homothetic tube construction, and then formulate a tube-based RMPC problem with terminal ingredients. Under the proposed formulation, we prove robust satisfaction of the original nonlinear constraints by the true closed-loop trajectory, recursive feasibility, and convergence to a neighborhood of the target state. Numerical experiments demonstrate robust stabilization of nonlinear systems and the advantages of the proposed method over existing Koopman-based RMPC approaches in terms of performance.

</details>


### [114] [Face Structure in Partial Facial Reduction of Semidefinite Relaxations of Binary Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25689)
*Hao Hu,Mingming Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究二元优化问题的半定松弛中，基于可处理内逼近锥（非负对角、对角占优、缩放对角占优）的部分面约化方法，证明了其恢复的结构特征（变量固定为0/1或强制相等），给出了面暴露的不等式刻画，并揭示了该方法在奇异度、等式检测方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半定松弛的完全面约化虽能恢复严格可行性，但需要求解与原始松弛同等代价的辅助问题。作者希望找到一种计算代价更低的**部分面约化**方法，仅基于可处理的内逼近锥来近似处理，同时尽可能多地恢复问题结构。

Method: 研究了基于非负对角、DD和SDD锥这三类可处理内逼近的部分面约化。通过理论分析刻画了SDD-部分FR恢复的终端面的结构（变量固定与分组相等），利用{0,1}基矩阵给出简化的稀疏保持形式。对多面体内逼近，通过外层松弛的活跃不等式刻画暴露的面。构建反例揭示方法在奇异度和等式检测上的局限性。

Result: 1) SDD-部分FR恢复的终端面由固定为0/1的变量和强制相等的变量组描述，且具有{0,1}基矩阵和不相交列支撑，可自然获得保持稀疏性的简化形式。2) 多面体内逼近中，每步暴露的面由外层松弛的活跃不等式表征。3) 存在奇异度为2的问题族，使得DD-和SDD-部分FR需要最大可能的步数。4) 因子宽度的限制可能导致无法检测稠密的有效仿射等式。

Conclusion: 可处理的部分面约化在计算代价和结构恢复之间提供了有效的折中方案，但存在固有局限：当问题奇异度较高或有效等式具有稠密结构时，基于窄因子宽度的内逼近无法有效恢复全部约束信息。

Abstract: Semidefinite relaxations of binary optimization problems often fail Slater's condition. Full facial reduction restores strict feasibility but may require auxiliary problems as costly as the original relaxation. We analyze partial facial reduction based on tractable inner approximations of the positive semidefinite cone: the nonnegative diagonal, diagonally dominant (DD), and scaled diagonally dominant (SDD) cones.
  For this class of SDP relaxations, we prove that every terminal face identified by SDD-partial FR is described by variables fixed to zero or one and groups of variables forced to be equal. The face admits a full-column-rank \(\{0,1\}\) basis matrix with disjoint column supports, yielding a natural reduced formulation that can preserve sparsity. For polyhedral inner approximations, we characterize the face exposed at each step through the inequalities active on the associated outer relaxation.
  We also establish sharp limitations. We construct a family with singularity degree two for which DD- and SDD-partial FR require the maximum possible number of steps. We further show that factor-width restrictions can prevent detection of dense valid affine equalities. These results characterize both the structure recovered by tractable partial FR and the cost of restricting the exposing-matrix search.

</details>


### [115] [Analysis and Optimal Design of Equilibria in the Vlasov-Poisson System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25698)
*Alfio Borzì,Gennaro Infante,Giovanni Mascali,Riccardo Scida*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在Vlasov-Poisson系统稳态Maxwellian平衡框架下研究外电场产生的平衡粒子分布最优设计问题，分析了归一化Poisson-Boltzmann方程，通过Schauder不动点论证和变分方法建立了自洽平衡势的存在唯一性，提出了统一的最优设计框架，涵盖谷势、二次跟踪和Kullback-Leibler设计准则，导出了一阶最优性条件，并提出了投影非线性共轭梯度算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在外电场作用下，如何最优地设计平衡粒子分布是一个基础性的物理与数学问题。作者旨在建立自洽平衡势的存在性与唯一性理论，并在此基础上构建一个统一的最优设计框架，以处理不同类型的设计准则。

Method: 首先分析归一化Poisson-Boltzmann方程：采用Schauder不动点论证（基于非线性源的均匀估计）和变分方法（基于严格凸自由能泛函）两种互补途径证明平衡势的存在唯一性。然后建立统一最优设计框架，针对谷势、二次跟踪和KL散度三种准则推导一阶最优性条件，最后提出投影非线性共轭梯度算法进行数值求解。

Result: 建立了自洽平衡势的存在唯一性理论，构建了涵盖多种设计准则的统一优化框架，导出了相应的一阶最优性条件，并给出了可行的数值算法。

Conclusion: 本文在Vlasov-Poisson平衡理论的框架下，为外电场最优设计问题提供了严谨的数学基础与统一的优化方法论，为后续相关物理系统的控制与设计奠定了理论依据。

Abstract: The optimal design of equilibrium particle distributions generated by external electric fields is investigated in the framework of stationary Maxwellian equilibria of the Vlasov-Poisson system. The resulting normalized Poisson-Boltzmann equation is analyzed, and existence and uniqueness of the self-consistent equilibrium potential are established by two complementary approaches. A Schauder fixed-point argument based on uniform estimates for the normalized nonlinear source and a variational approach based on a strictly convex free-energy functional are developed. Building upon these analytical results, a unified optimal design framework is formulated for equilibrium densities. Valley-potential, quadratic tracking, and Kullback-Leibler design criteria are treated within the same optimization framework. First-order optimality conditions are derived, and a projected nonlinear conjugate-gradient algorithm is proposed for the numerical solution of the resulting optimization problems.

</details>


### [116] [Vertex Loci of Max-Indicator Polytopes and Vertex Retrieval in Ball Polyhedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25752)
*Marius Costandin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在球心位于球面且半径相等、仅有一个顶点满足1^T·x=0的条件下，提出了一种利用最大指标多面体的顶点轨迹线（过原点与Q的顶点）以及对称扰动策略，通过线性规划高效求解该顶点的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在球交区域Q中寻找距目标点C_S最远的点是一个重要几何优化问题。此前工作引入最大指标多面体族来刻画该问题的极值点，但如何高效检索满足特定条件的顶点仍待解决。

Method: (1) 分析最大指标多面体顶点随参数变化的轨迹（顶点轨迹线）——这些线过原点与Q的顶点；(2) 沿1_{n×1}方向上下扰动目标点C_S，证明对称扰动的两个最大指标多面体的交集严格包含于未扰动多面体，且唯一公共边界点位于过原点与满足1^T·x=0的Q顶点的连线上；(3) 利用该性质，通过线性规划检索该顶点。

Result: 在给定假设（球心在球面上、半径相等、仅有一个顶点满足1^T·x=0）下，证明了对称扰动的最大指标多面体交集与未扰动多面体仅在该特定顶点处有公共边界点，从而可通过线性规划高效唯一确定该顶点。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个在特定几何条件下高效检索球交区域中特定顶点的算法，核心贡献在于揭示了最大指标多面体顶点的轨迹线结构及对称扰动下的包含关系，将问题简化为线性规划求解。

Abstract: The so called "max-indicator polytopes" were introduced in \cite{mainB} as an intersection of half-spaces related to the problem of finding the farthest in an intersection of balls $\mathcal{Q}$, from a given target point $C_S$. It was shown that if the target point belongs to the convex hull of the balls centers, then a specific intersection of half-spaces forms a single real variable parameterized polytope family which is used to characterized the extreme points.
  This paper, under the assumptions that the intersecting balls centers lie on a sphere and all have equal radii with only one vertex $x$, of $\mathcal{Q}$, meeting $1_{n \times 1}\cdot x = 0$, we provide an efficient algorithm to retrieve that vertex.
  The main idea behind the result is that the max-indicator polytopes vertices, as the family parameter varies, slide on some lines, that we call vertex locii. These lines pass through the space origin and the vertices of $\mathcal{Q}$. Perturbing the target point $C_S$ up and down along the direction $1_{n\times 1}$ we are able to show that the intersection of two simetrically perturbed max-indicator polytopes is strictly included in the unperturbed polytope, with the only boundary common points being on the line connecting the origin with the vertex of $\mathcal{Q}$ meeting $1_{n\times 1}^T\cdot x = 0$. As such, under the said assumptions, the only such vertex can be retrieved using linear programming.

</details>


### [117] [Variance-Reduced Conditional Gradient Methods under Markovian Sampling for Nonconvex Composite Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25785)
*Zhaojun Peng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对随机复合非凸优化问题，提出了MC-ALFCG算法，该算法结合动量条件梯度法与耦合截断的多层蒙特卡洛估计，在马尔可夫链单轨迹采样下实现了投影自由优化，并给出了混合时间相关的样本复杂度分析。在正中心噪声下达到$\widetilde{O}((\tau_{\mathrm{mix}}^2G_\sigma+\tau_{\mathrm{mix}}^{5/2}G_\sigma^2)\varepsilon^{-3}+\tau_{\mathrm{mix}}^5\varepsilon^{-2})$的期望样本复杂度，无噪声特例达到混合时间无关的$\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单轨迹方差缩减理论局限于光滑无约束目标函数，而实际应用中常出现带紧凸集约束的随机复合非凸优化问题，且梯度样本来自固定遍历马尔可夫链的单条轨迹。需要填补该场景下的理论空白，提供投影自由的优化方法并建立混合时间相关的收敛保证。

Method: 提出MC-ALFCG算法：(1) 采用动量条件梯度法；(2) 结合耦合截断的多层蒙特卡洛估计（Capped Multilevel Monte Carlo），最深层嵌套平均使用同一轨迹的连续状态，引入条件偏差$O(\tau_{\mathrm{mix}}/T)$；(3) 通过耦合机制控制梯度差二阶矩与迭代位移的关系；(4) 使用逐次截断保证路径一致有界；(5) 将马尔可夫递归化简为独立采样情形，参数变换为$\sigma^2\mapsto 2\Lambda G_\sigma^2$和$L^2\mapsto 2\Lambda L^2$，其中$\Lambda=O(\tau_{\mathrm{mix}}\log T)$。

Result: 正中心噪声下期望样本复杂度$\widetilde{O}((\tau_{\mathrm{mix}}^2G_\sigma+\tau_{\mathrm{mix}}^{5/2}G_\sigma^2)\varepsilon^{-3}+\tau_{\mathrm{mix}}^5\varepsilon^{-2})$；精确无噪声特例达到$\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$且常数与混合时间无关；混合时间不可知变体达到$\widetilde{O}(\tau_{\mathrm{mix}}^6\varepsilon^{-3}+\tau_{\mathrm{mix}}^3\varepsilon^{-2})$。所有保证均在固定转移核下的期望意义下成立。

Conclusion: 首次为马尔可夫链单轨迹采样的随机复合非凸优化提供了投影自由的方差缩减理论，通过耦合截断MLMC技术有效处理了马尔可夫依赖性和复合约束，数值实验验证了参数敏感性、非凸复合实例及截断行为。

Abstract: We study stochastic composite nonconvex optimization over a compact convex set when gradient samples arrive along a single trajectory of a fixed ergodic Markov chain. Existing single-trajectory variance-reduction theory covers smooth unconstrained objectives; we address the projection-free composite setting using the generalized Frank-Wolfe gap. We propose MC-ALFCG, which combines a momentum conditional-gradient method with coupled capped multilevel Monte Carlo estimation and per-iteration clipping. The deepest nested average uses consecutive states from the same trajectory, yielding conditional bias $O(τ_{\mathrm{mix}}/T)$ uniformly over the starting state, while coupling controls the gradient-difference second moment through the iterate displacement. Clipping enforces the pathwise bounds needed by the adaptive analysis. We reduce the Markovian recursion to its independent-sampling counterpart under $σ^2\mapsto 2ΛG_σ^2$ and $L^2\mapsto 2ΛL^2$, where $Λ=O(τ_{\mathrm{mix}}\log T)$. For positive centered noise, the tuned method achieves expected sample complexity $\widetilde{O}((τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^2G_σ+τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^{5/2}G_σ^2)\varepsilon^{-3}+τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^5\varepsilon^{-2})$. The exactly noiseless specialization achieves $\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{-2})$ with mixing-time-free constants, while a mixing-time-oblivious variant achieves $\widetilde{O}(τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^6\varepsilon^{-3}+τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^3\varepsilon^{-2})$. All guarantees are in expectation under a fixed transition kernel. Controlled numerical studies examine dependence sensitivity, a nonconvex composite instance, and clipping behavior.

</details>


### [118] [Optimization under Persistent State-Dependent Bias: Gradient-based Method and Complexity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26032)
*Zhaoxian Wu,Quan Xiao,Tayfun Gokmen,Tianyi Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在更新受持久且状态依赖偏差影响的随机梯度下降（SGD）收敛性问题，提出了一种名为Residual Learning的双层优化算法，能有效消除硬件非理想响应函数导致的收敛偏差，并量化了响应函数对收敛复杂度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGD在硬件实现中，更新量常受到持久且状态依赖的偏差（如响应函数对梯度的分量级缩放），导致算法收敛到有偏的惩罚问题最优解而非真正的最优解。本文旨在解决这一收敛失效问题。

Method: 将原始优化问题重新表述为等价的双层优化问题，并提出了基于梯度的Residual Learning算法。理论分析证明了该算法在硬件非理想条件下仍能收敛到原始无偏优化问题的最优解，并通过定义硬件条件数来量化响应函数对收敛复杂度的影响。

Result: Residual Learning能够精确收敛到原始无偏优化问题的解；响应函数通过硬件条件数影响收敛复杂度，且一般情况下多项式依赖是不可避免的（通过构造困难实例证明）。数值仿真验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文从理论和实验两个层面验证了Residual Learning算法能够有效克服硬件更新偏差，实现无偏优化，为硬件受限场景下的SGD应用提供了理论基础和实用方案。

Abstract: This paper studies the convergence of stochastic gradient descent (SGD) when the implemented updates are subject to a persistent and state-dependent bias, in which the desired update is scaled by response functions component-wise. Our first contribution is to demonstrate that SGD in this setting implicitly optimizes a penalized problem whose minimizer does not coincide with the true minimizer. To mitigate this convergence failure, we reformulate the original task as an equivalent bilevel optimization problem and propose a gradient-based algorithm, termed Residual Learning. Theoretical analysis shows that Residual Learning finds a solution to the original, unbiased optimization problem despite the hardware imperfections. Beyond exact convergence, we quantify how the response functions affect convergence complexity via the hardware condition number and show that a polynomial dependence on it is unavoidable in general, via a construction of a hard instance. The theoretical results are supported by numerical simulations that demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次搜索B^0 → K_S^0 τ^+τ^-衰变，利用Belle和Belle II探测器收集的11.6亿Υ(4S)事件数据，未发现信号证据，在90%置信水平设定分支比上限为8.3×10^{-4}，并结合B^+→K^+τ^+τ^-测量给出联合上限5.4×10^{-4}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B→Kτ^+τ^-衰变是探测轻子味普适性（LFU）破缺和寻找新物理的关键模式。τ轻子质量大，潜在新物理贡献可能增强其分支比。此前仅有B^+→K^+τ^+τ^-的测量，B^0→K_S^0τ^+τ^-尚未被搜索过。

Method: 利用Belle和Belle II实验的11.6亿Υ(4S)→B^0\bar{B}^0事件，将非信号侧B完全重建到强子道，信号侧B^0候选者中要求τ轻子衰变到单带电粒子末态，通过多变量分类器组合多个鉴别变量，再对拟合观测量进行统计分析。

Result: 未观察到显著信号，在90%置信水平设定B^0→K_S^0τ^+τ^-分支比上限为8.3×10^{-4}。结合B^+→K^+τ^+τ^-的最新测量，得到B→Kτ^+τ^-分支比联合上限为5.4×10^{-4}。

Conclusion: 该搜索是目前对B^0→K_S^0τ^+τ^-最严格的限制，结合带电道结果显著约束了B→Kτ^+τ^-过程的分支比，为轻子味普适性检验和新物理搜索提供了重要实验依据。

Abstract: We present the first search for $B^0 \to K^0_{\rm S} τ^+τ^-$ decays. We look for signal decays in $B^0\bar B^0$ events produced in asymmetric-energy electron-positron collisions. This work uses samples from the Belle and Belle~II detectors, comprising 1.16 billion $Υ(4S)$ events. In $Υ(4S)\to B^0\bar{B}^0$ decays, the non-signal $\bar{B}^0$ meson is fully reconstructed in a hadronic channel. For the signal $B^0$ meson, $τ$-lepton decays into final states with a single charged particle are selected. A multivariate classifier is used to combine several discriminating inputs into a single fit observable. We observe no evidence for the signal and set an upper limit on the branching fraction $\mathcal{B}(B^0\to K^0_{\rm S} τ^+τ^-) < 8.3 \times 10^{-4}$ at the 90\% confidence level. Combining this with the recent measurement of the isospin-partner decay $B^+\to K^+τ^+τ^-$, we determine an upper limit $\mathcal{B}(B\to Kτ^+τ^-) < 5.4\times10^{-4}$ at the 90\% confidence level.

</details>


### [120] [Measurement of the fragmentation properties of jets containing $Υ$(nS) mesons in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25653)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用质子-质子对撞数据（√s=13 TeV, 积分亮度138 fb⁻¹）测量了包含Υ(nS)介子的喷注碎裂性质，发现现有蒙特卡洛预测与数据描述不匹配，表明夸克偶素在部分子簇射中的建模需要改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Υ(nS)介子在喷注中的碎裂性质，以检验和改善夸克偶素产生在部分子簇射中的理论建模。

Method: 利用138 fb⁻¹的质子-质子对撞数据，重建Υ(nS) → μ⁺μ⁻衰变，分析Υ(nS)动量相对于喷注动量方向的纵向和横向投影分布，并与蒙特卡洛预测进行比较。

Result: 现有的蒙特卡洛预测（包括夸克偶素产生在部分子簇射中的最新发展模型）无法满意地描述实验数据。

Conclusion: 当前夸克偶素在部分子簇射中的建模存在不足，需要进一步改进以更好地描述喷注碎裂过程。

Abstract: A measurement of the fragmentation properties of jets containing $Υ$(nS) mesons using proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$, is presented. The $Υ$(nS) mesons associated with jets are reconstructed via their decays producing pairs of oppositely charged muons. The longitudinal and transverse projections of the momenta of the $Υ$(nS) mesons along the momenta of the corresponding jets are studied. The results are compared with Monte Carlo predictions, including recent developments in the modelling of quarkonia production in parton showers. The description of the data by these Monte Carlo predictions is found to be unsatisfactory, leaving room for improvements in the modelling of such processes.

</details>


### [121] [Observation of the electroweak production of $γγjj$ at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV in $140\ \text{fb}^{-1}$ of $pp$ collision data with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25869)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ATLAS实验利用140 fb⁻¹的13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据，以6.2σ显著性观测到两个孤立光子和两个喷注的弱电产生过程，测量的 fiducial截面与标准模型预测一致，并给出了微分截面、QCD+弱电联合截面以及有效场论中反常四规范玻色子耦合的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验标准模型中弱电产生过程的预言，探索是否存在超出标准模型的新物理信号，特别是通过反常四规范玻色子耦合来约束新物理参数空间。

Method: 利用ATLAS探测器收集的√s=13 TeV、积分亮度140 fb⁻¹的质子-质子对撞数据，通过末态两个孤立光子和两个喷注的拓扑特征，采用基于有效场论框架的截面测量和上限设定方法进行分析。

Result: 弱电产生过程观测显著性6.2σ，fiducial截面σ^{EW}_{obs}=13.8^{+3.0}_{-2.6} fb，与标准模型预言17.1^{+2.4}_{-2.4} fb一致；给出了微分截面和QCD+弱电联合截面，并设定了多个维度-8算符Wilson系数的上限。

Conclusion: 成功观测到双光子双喷注的弱电产生过程，测量结果与标准模型一致，同时利用有效场论对反常四规范耦合施加了实验限制。

Abstract: This Letter presents the observation of the electroweak production of two isolated photons in association with two jets together with measurements of the corresponding fiducial and differential cross sections. The analysis is performed using proton-proton collision data collected by ATLAS at the LHC at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV and with an integrated luminosity of 140 fb$^{-1}$. The electroweak production of two isolated photons in association with two jets is observed with a significance of $6.2 σ$ and with a fiducial cross-section of $σ^{\mathrm{EW}}_{\mathrm{obs}}= 13.8^{+3.0}_{-2.6}\, \mathrm{fb}$, in agreement with the Standard Model prediction of $σ^{\mathrm{EW}}_{\mathrm{obs}}= 17.1^{+2.4}_{-2.4}\, \mathrm{fb}$. Differential cross-sections are measured as a function of various kinematical variables probing photon and jet properties and compared with predictions. In addition, fiducial and differential cross-sections are reported for the combined QCD and electroweak production of two isolated photons and two jets. The measurement is also interpreted in the context of an effective field theory, and limits are set on the Wilson coefficients of several dimension-8 operators parameterising anomalous quartic gauge couplings.

</details>


### [122] [The calibration of large-radius jets using the Run 2 dataset with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25893)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: ATLAS实验基于蒙特卡洛模拟和碰撞数据，开发了一套大半径喷注校准程序，对Run 2期间使用Unified Flow Objects重建并经Soft Drop算法修饰的大半径喷注进行校准，实现了喷注能量和质量标度的残余不确定性在1 TeV以下约为1%、最高2 TeV时为2-3%的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大半径喷注在搜寻超出标准模型粒子和精确测量标准模型 boosted 物体中至关重要，而高精度的喷注能量和质量校准是实现这些物理目标的前提，因此需要开发一套可靠的校准程序。

Method: 基于蒙特卡洛模拟进行修正，并采用原位方法（in situ methods）验证碰撞数据中的喷注能量和质量标度及其分辨率。喷注重建使用Unified Flow Objects，修饰算法为Soft Drop算法。

Result: 喷注能量和质量标度的残余不确定性在横动量1 TeV以下约为1%，最高2 TeV时为2-3%。

Conclusion: 该校准程序达到了高精度，能够支持对超出标准模型粒子的灵敏搜索以及使用boosted物体进行精确的标准模型测量。

Abstract: The ATLAS experiment has developed an extensive procedure based on Monte Carlo simulation and collision data to calibrate the response of large-radius jets. This paper describes the Run 2 calibration of large-radius jets reconstructed from Unified Flow Objects and groomed with the Soft Drop algorithm, using data produced in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV. Results are presented for the Monte Carlo-based correction and for the in situ methods that are used to validate the jet energy and mass scales and their resolutions in collision data. The residual uncertainty on the jet energy and mass scales is close to 1% for transverse momenta up to 1 TeV and is 2-3% up to 2 TeV, enabling sensitive searches to beyond the Standard Model particles and precise Standard Model measurements with boosted objects.

</details>

### [123] [Momentum-Selective Two-Component Excitations in Electron-Doped Mott Insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24936)
*Zeyu Han,Can Cui,Jia-Xin Zhang,Zheng-Yu Weng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过t-t'-J模型的变分蒙特卡洛研究，揭示了电子掺杂铜氧化物中低能单粒子激发的双分量结构：相干准粒子传播与不相干复合组分之间的共振，解释了电子掺杂体系为何同时表现出费米液体行为与强关联特征，并提出了与实验一致的现象学格林函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验发现电子掺杂与空穴掺杂铜氧化物在低能单粒子激发上存在显著不对称性：电子掺杂体系同时表现出费米液体行为（暗示较弱电子关联）和强关联驱动的特征，这种双重性挑战了掺杂Mott绝缘体框架下的统一描述。

Method: 采用t-t'-J模型的变分蒙特卡洛方法，在单空穴水平上分析基态波函数的双分量结构（相干准粒子+不相干复合组分），并构建现象学格林函数推广至有限掺杂。

Result: 对于空穴掺杂(t'<0)，组分间共振在节点区低能区域占主导；对于电子掺杂(t'>0)，相干准粒子传播在反节点区低能区域被选择性增强，导致动量空间分离：反节点区由相干准粒子主导，节点区由不相干复合组分主导。

Conclusion: 电子掺杂体系在动量空间中存在分离：反节点低能谱权重由相干准粒子（费米液体性质）主导，节点区由不相干复合组分（强关联特征）主导，这解释了电子掺杂铜氧化物看似矛盾的双重实验特征。

Abstract: Experimental studies reveal a striking asymmetry in low-energy single-particle excitations between electron- and hole-doped cuprates. Electron-doped cuprates display a nontrivial dichotomy: Fermi-liquid-like behavior (suggesting weaker electronic correlations) coexists with correlation-driven features typical of hole-doped systems. This dual nature challenges a unified description within a doped Mott insulator framework. The present work addresses this issue by establishing that the ground-state wave function of the $t$-$t'$-$J$ model generically possesses a two-component structure, comprising a coherent quasiparticle and an incoherent composite component. The kinetic energy arises from both the intrinsic propagation of the coherent quasiparticle and the resonance between these components. Using variational Monte Carlo at the level of a single hole, we show that for hole doping ($t'<0$), this resonance between components dominates and concentrates in the nodal region at low energies. This emergent propagation induced by resonance can be physically interpreted as originating from the recombination of fractionalized degrees of freedom, which drives various phenomena associated with strong correlations. Conversely, for electron doping ($t'>0$), the coherent quasiparticle propagation, which exhibits conventional properties of a Fermi liquid, is selectively enhanced in the antinodal region at low energies. This produces a separation in momentum space for systems with electron doping: the antinodal spectral weight at low energies is governed by the coherent quasiparticle, fundamentally differing from the nodal region, which remains dominated by the incoherent composite component. Motivated by such a structure and guided by experimental observations, we propose a phenomenological Green's function at finite doping, yielding spectral features consistent with experiments.

</details>


### [124] [Chiral Magnons: Mechanisms and Research Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24963)
*Wanxing Lin,Hanchen Deng,Bao-Tian Wang,Dao-Xin Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了手性磁振子的统一框架，涵盖对称性破缺机制、材料实现、实验表征、输运响应及多体非厄米动力学，并评估了面向室温及器件平台的实现路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性磁振子因其色散关系破缺动量反演对称性（ω(k)≠ω(-k)），导致非互易自旋波传播，为自旋信息传输、热-自旋转换及低耗散非互易微波器件提供了新机遇；近年来交变磁性的提出拓展了其物理起源和研究范式，亟需建立统一理论框架。

Method: 基于对称性分析、模型哈密顿量和自旋波理论，结合第一性原理计算以及非弹性极化中子散射、布里渊光散射和输运测量等实验手段。

Result: 归纳了体带隙和贝里曲率诱导的手性磁振子边缘态、通过手性自旋泵浦和腔-磁振子杂化增强非互易性，以及多粒子阻尼和增益-损耗竞争导致的非厄米特征。

Conclusion: 为阐明手性磁振子的微观机制、推进新材料合成与实验表征以及指导下一代非互易磁振子器件设计提供了全面参考。

Abstract: Chiral magnons are distinctive collective spin excitations in magnetic ordered systems, whose dispersion relations break momentum-inversion symmetry, $ω(\boldsymbol{k}) \neq ω(-\boldsymbol{k})$, resulting in essential non-reciprocal spin-wave propagation. This built-in directionality provides new opportunities for spin information transfer, thermal-spin interconversion, and low-dissipation non-reciprocal microwave devices, which complement but differ from topological magnonics. In recent years, the proposal and rapid development of altermagnetism have broadened the physical origin and research framework of chiral magnons, making them a research frontier in condensed matter physics. This review presents a unified framework for chiral magnons, covering symmetry-breaking mechanisms, material implementation, experimental characterization, transport response, and many-body non-Hermitian dynamics, and evaluates routes toward room-temperature and device-related platforms. The discussion is based on symmetry analysis, model Hamiltonians, and spin-wave theory, combined with first-principles calculations as well as recent spectroscopic (e.g., inelastic and polarized neutron scattering, Brillouin light scattering) and transport measurements. This review further summarizes bulk-gap and Berry-curvature induced chiral magnon edge states, the enhancement of non-reciprocity via chiral spin pumping and cavity-magnon hybrids, as well as non-Hermitian features arising from multiparticle damping and gain-loss competition. This review provides a comprehensive reference for elucidating the underlying mechanisms of chiral magnons, advancing the synthesis and experimental characterization of novel materials, and also guiding the design of next-generation non-reciprocal magnonic devices.

</details>


### [125] [Stacking-dependent anisotropic altermagnetism in V$_{1/3}$NbS$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25213)
*Chris J. Lygouras,Nathan Prouse,Jack H. Drouin,Youzhe Chen,Laura Garcia-Gassull,Zili Feng,Mingxuan Fu,Lü Fang,Alexander I. Kolesnikov,Christina Hoffman,Yiqing Hao,Huibo Cao,Maxime A. Siegler,Robert J. Birgeneau,Roser Valentí,Satoru Nakatsuji,Collin L. Broholm*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: V₁/₃NbS₂的两种多型体具有垂直的易磁化轴和不同的反常霍尔响应，尽管都展现准共线A型交变磁序。堆叠序列深刻影响层状材料宏观磁性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究三角晶格过渡金属离子在范德华材料NbS₂层间的堆叠序列对磁性和输运性质的影响。

Method: 采用单晶X射线和中子衍射、输运及磁化测量表征多型体结构；利用非弹性中子散射数据自洽分析RKKY相互作用。

Result: 发现V₁/₃NbS₂存在两种多型体，具有垂直的易磁化轴和不同的反常霍尔响应；振荡型RKKY相互作用延伸至1 nm，稳定了准共线A型交变磁序。

Conclusion: 层状三维固体的堆叠序列可显著调控宏观反常霍尔响应和磁性，为工程化层状材料体性质提供新路径。

Abstract: We report profound impacts of the stacking sequence of triangular lattices of magnetic transition metal ions intercalated between the layers of the van der Waals material NbS$_2$. Using single crystal x-ray and neutron diffraction, and transport and magnetization measurements, we show there are two distinct polytypes of $\rm V_{1/3}NbS_2$ with disparate easy axes of magnetization and different anomalous Hall responses. Self-consistent analysis of inelastic neutron scattering data provides evidence for oscillatory RKKY interactions that extend to 1 nm and stabilize quasi-collinear A-type altermagnetic orders in both polytypes though with perpendicular easy axes. The detailed stacking sequence of a bulk polytype crystal dramatically impact its macroscopic anomalous Hall response and magnetism, which suggests a new path to engineer the bulk properties of a layered three dimensional solid.

</details>


### [126] [Covariance Geometry of Basis-Resolved Low-Energy Wavefunctions in the Spin-$1/2$ Kitaev--Heisenberg Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25462)
*Sk Saniur Rahaman,S. R. Hassan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了基矢分辨的协方差框架，通过PCA分析低能态的自旋、键关联和磁通表示，揭示了Kitaev-Heisenberg模型中协方差几何的演化：磁有序相呈现一维协方差流形，磁相边界出现竞争模式，Kitaev区域展现内在多维几何；协方差几何由量子态和观测量共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统序参量难以充分刻画阻挫量子多体系统的复杂低能结构，需要发展超越常规序参量的互补统计视角来描述量子多体波函数的组织方式。

Method: 基于基矢分辨的协方差框架，构建低能态在局域自旋、键关联和磁通表示下的协方差矩阵，利用主成分分析(PCA)识别主导集体协方差模式。

Result: 磁有序相呈现本质上一维的协方差流形；磁相边界出现主导协方差模式间的竞争；Kitaev区域发展出内在多维协方差几何；同一波函数在不同算符表示下产生不同协方差几何。

Conclusion: 基矢分辨的协方差几何可作为表征阻挫量子多体系统的互补统计框架，超越传统序参量的描述能力。

Abstract: We develop a basis-resolved covariance framework for investigating the organization of low-energy many-body wavefunctions in the spin-$1/2$ Kitaev--Heisenberg model. By constructing covariance matrices from local-spin, bond-correlation, and plaquette-flux representations of the low-energy states, Principal Component Analysis (PCA) is employed to identify the dominant collective covariance modes. We find a systematic evolution of the covariance geometry across the phase diagram: magnetically ordered phases are described by an essentially one-dimensional covariance manifold, conventional magnetic phase boundaries exhibit competition between leading covariance modes, whereas the Kitaev regimes develop intrinsically multidimensional covariance geometry. Furthermore, the same many-body wavefunction produces distinct covariance geometries in different operator representations, demonstrating that covariance geometry is determined jointly by the quantum state and the physical observables used to probe it. Shannon entropy and the participation ratio provide quantitative measures of this evolution. The present framework establishes basis-resolved covariance geometry as a complementary statistical perspective for characterizing frustrated quantum many-body systems beyond conventional order parameters.

</details>


### [127] [Anisotropic bond-current susceptibilities and real-space current topology in correlated electron systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25483)
*Worapon Phatcharasirinawakun,Hiroyuki Yamase*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文推导了正方、三角和kagome晶格上近邻键电荷相互作用所允许的所有对称性键流形因子，发现键流磁化率的各向异性系统地偏好支持闭合环流态的序波矢，并建立了键流形因子、磁化率各向异性与实空间流拓扑之间的直接联系。

Method: 基于对称性分析，推导了正方、三角和kagome晶格上近邻键电荷相互作用产生的所有对称性允许的键流形因子；通过分析键流磁化率的各向异性，比较不同序波矢的稳定性。

Result: 磁化率各向异性系统地偏好支持闭合环流态的序波矢；在范霍夫填充附近，正方晶格产生交错通量相，三角晶格产生菱形流模式，kagome晶格产生手性和非手性环流态。

Conclusion: 建立了键流形因子、磁化率各向异性与实空间流拓扑之间的直接对应关系，为识别环流序和解释关联电子材料中时间反演对称性破缺信号提供了通用框架。

Abstract: We derive all symmetry-allowed bond-current form factors generated by nearest-neighbor bond-charge interactions on square, triangular, and kagome lattices. We find that the anisotropy of the bond-current susceptibility systematically favors ordering wave vectors that support closed loop-current states, whereas symmetry-related wave vectors with weaker susceptibility generate noncirculating current textures. Near van Hove filling, this correspondence is robust across all lattice geometries considered, producing staggered flux phases on the square lattice, diamond-shaped current patterns on the triangular lattice and both chiral and nonchiral loop-current states on the kagome lattice. Our results establish a direct link between bond-current form factors, susceptibility anisotropy, and real-space current topology, providing a general framework for identifying loop-current orders and interpreting signatures of time-reversal symmetry breaking in correlated electron materials.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 设计了一种三混合并行方案，同时利用MPI进行进程间通信、CPU-openMP进行节点内多线程并行，以及GPU-openMP进行加速器计算，以适配现代异构高性能计算架构。

Result: 提出了一种针对各向异性自旋系统的有限温度Lanczos方法在混合架构上的实现框架。

Conclusion: 该三混合并行方案能够有效利用现代高性能计算架构资源，满足有限温度Lanczos方法在各向异性自旋系统中的计算需求。

Abstract: Lanczos concepts are often realized on supercomputers. In view of modern architectures of high-performance computing triple-hybrid schemes employing MPI, CPU-openMP as well as GPU-openMP should be utilized. The present article sketches how such a scheme could be utilized in order to meet the demands of the finite-temperature Lanczos method for anisotropic spin systems.

</details>


### [129] [Aharonov-Casher phase in twisted bilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25660)

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文研究了扭曲双层石墨烯中电子的Aharonov-Casher效应，发现AC相位与电场强度呈线性关系，并在魔角处出现尖锐峰值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索扭曲双层石墨烯中电子的Aharonov-Casher效应，特别是理解介质（TBG）对AC相位的影响，以及该相位与魔角的关系。

Method: 通过SU(2)规范势构建AC相位因子，对哈密顿量进行规范变换，分析在垂直均匀电场下TBG中电子的AC相位行为。

Result: AC相位与电场强度E呈线性关系，且在魔角处出现尖锐峰值。

Conclusion: 扭曲双层石墨烯中的AC效应具有显著的魔角增强特性，为量子调控提供了新的自由度。

Abstract: The Aharonov-Casher (AC) effect is a quantum mechanical phenomenon in which the wave function of a particle with a magnetic moment moving in a region subject to an electric field develops a phase shift due to spin-orbit interaction, even if no classical force acts on it. This phase also depends on the medium through which the particle moves. Here we focus on the AC phase of an electron moving in twisted bilayer graphene (TBG) lying in the $x$-$y$ plane, subject to a uniform electric field perpendicular to the plane of the graphene, ${\bf E}=E{\hat{\bf z}}$. The AC phase is determined by an $SU(2)$ vector potential ${\bf A}$ from which a phase factor is generated, and used to perform a gauge transformation of the Hamiltonian. We find that the AC phase is linear with $E$ and exhibits sharp peaks at the magic angles.

</details>


### [130] [Kinetically induced order from mobility-constrained excitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25806)
*Aprem P. Joy,Urban F. P. Seifert*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动力学机制：原本受运动限制的激发（如携带非平庸量子数的准粒子）通过与局域序参量场耦合获得迁移能力，序参量凝聚后带来巨大动能收益，从而稳定长程有序相。通过在自旋链（磁化偶极守恒导致二聚化失稳）和Kitaev-Kondo模型（电子与自旋液体局域矩耦合导致巡游磁性）两个例子中展示该机制，并指出非平衡制备或热激活下该机制可逆直觉地通过升温增强序参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联系统中激发携带的非平庸量子数会强烈约束其运动，作者旨在揭示这种受约束激发如何通过动力学机制驱动对称性破缺的长程有序，而非仅被动响应序参量。

Method: 理论分析：约束激发通过耦合到局域序参量场被激活迁移性，序参量凝聚释放动能收益从而稳定有序相；通过两个具体模型（磁化偶极守恒的自旋链与Kitaev-Kondo模型）加以验证。

Result: 在自旋链中实现二聚化失稳，在Kitaev-Kondo模型中实现巡游磁性；发现温度升高可逆直觉地增强热激活激发的动能收益从而促进序参量。

Conclusion: 该动力学机制普遍适用于具有有限密度运动受限激发的系统（无论通过非平衡制备还是热激活），为理解强关联系统中对称性破缺与动力学约束之间的相互作用提供了新视角。

Abstract: A fascinating feature of strongly correlated systems is that excitations may carry nontrivial quantum numbers under emergent symmetries which, in some cases, may strongly constrain their motion. We show that such constrained excitations can play a central role in driving symmetry-breaking long-range order through a kinetic mechanism: coupling to a local order-parameter field activates the mobility of otherwise constrained excitations, and condensation of the order parameter then enables a large gain in kinetic energy, thereby stabilizing a phase with long-range order. We illustrate this mechanism in two settings: (i) a dimerization instability in a spin chain with a magnetization dipole conservation law, and (ii) itinerant magnetism of electrons in the Kitaev-Kondo model, where charge carriers couple to local moments that form a spin liquid. This mechanism becomes operative whenever a finite density of mobility-constrained excitations is present, either through non-equilibrium preparation or thermal activation. In the latter case, we argue that increasing temperature can, counterintuitively, promote order by enhancing the kinetic-energy gain of thermally activated excitations.

</details>


### [131] [Scaling universal Fermi network toward ground states: A diffusion-Monte-Carlo assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25872)
*Yu-Sheng Li,Saskia Poldmaa,Tzen Ong,Ahmed Abouelkomsan,Tay-Rong Chang,Hsin Lin,Liang Fu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文展示了Fermi Sets——一种通用神经网络费米子波函数架构——可以通过变分蒙特卡洛框架系统扩展以求解相互作用基态。结合固定相位扩散蒙特卡洛方法，随着网络规模增大，变分能量系统性地降低，而DMC的能量改进单调趋于零，表明收敛到基态。在凝胶模型和高磁场量子点中验证了该方法的扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经网络波函数方法虽然取得了进展，但难以系统性地扩展到更大规模的费米子相互作用系统并可靠地逼近基态。作者旨在证明Fermi Sets架构能够通过增加网络规模实现系统性的精度提升，并通过DMC评估确认其收敛到真实基态的能力。

Method: 采用Fermi Sets神经网络波函数架构，在变分蒙特卡洛框架中进行能量最小化优化；随后对优化后的波函数执行固定相位扩散蒙特卡洛（DMC）评估，通过观察变分能量随网络规模增大而系统降低、DMC能量改进单调趋于零的行为来判断基态收敛性。在凝胶模型中的相互作用电子和高磁场下的量子点系统中进行验证。

Result: 随着Fermi Sets网络规模增大，变分能量系统性地下降；DMC对较大网络的能量改进幅度单调减小并趋近于零，表明波函数已接近真实基态。在凝胶模型和强磁场量子点两个系统中均观察到这一收敛行为。

Conclusion: Fermi Sets架构具有系统可扩展性，能够通过增大网络规模并结合DMC评估可靠地逼近费米子相互作用系统的基态，为神经网络量子蒙特卡洛方法提供了收敛性保证的实证基础。

Abstract: In this work, we show that Fermi Sets---a provably universal neural network architecture for fermionic wavefunctions---can be systematically scaled up to find interacting ground states through energy minimization in a variational Monte Carlo framework. By further performing fixed-phase diffusion Monte Carlo (DMC) on the optimized neural network wavefunction, we demonstrate that as the network size increases, the variational energy systematically decreases while the energy improvement from DMC collapses monotonically to zero, indicating convergence to the ground state. We illustrate the scaling of Fermi Sets accompanied by the DMC assessment for interacting electrons in jellium and in a quantum dot under high magnetic fields.

</details>


### [132] [Charge-6e superconductivity from doping SU(3) spin liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25909)
*Yan-Qi Wang,Boran Zhou,Hui Yang,Zhi-Qiang Gao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出掺杂SU(3)对称自旋液体作为实现电荷-6e超导的途径，通过研究双层三角晶格Hubbard模型，分析了掺杂Z₃量子自旋液体和手性自旋液体等多种相，发现了多种电荷-6e超导态以及相关拓扑相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将掺杂SU(4)对称相构建电荷-4e超导的思想推广到SU(3)情形，探索掺杂SU(3)对称自旋液体能否产生更高阶的电荷-6e超导电性，并研究其中对称性、分数化和拓扑的协同作用。

Method: 采用互补的部分子构造方法，研究具有SU(3)自旋对称性和层间反铁磁交换的双层三角晶格Hubbard模型，系统分析掺杂Z₃量子自旋液体、Abelian SU(3)₁和SU(6)₁手性自旋液体、以及non-Abelian SU(3)₂手性自旋液体等不同自旋液体相。

Result: 发现了多种电荷-6e超导相：掺杂Z₃量子自旋液体产生时间反演对称的电荷-6e超导体；掺杂Abelian手性自旋液体产生有/无残留Abelian拓扑序的手性电荷-6e超导体；掺杂non-Abelian SU(3)₂手性自旋液体产生与SO(3)₋₃拓扑序交织的非Abelian手性电荷-6e超导体，支持非Abelian h/(6e)超导涡旋。还识别了Z₃正交金属、量子反常霍尔相、电荷-2e超导体、复合费米液体等多种相。

Conclusion: 掺杂SU(3)自旋液体是产生电荷-6e超导电性的自然平台，其中对称性、分数化和拓扑的协同作用共同实现了高阶超导相，为探索新型超导机制和拓扑物质态提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We propose doping $SU(3)$-symmetric spin liquids as a route toward charge-$6e$ superconductivity. This generalizes the idea of constructing charge-$4e$ superconductivity from doped $SU(4)$-symmetric phases. As a concrete platform, we study a bilayer triangular-lattice Hubbard model with $SU(3)$ spin symmetry and interlayer antiferromagnetic exchange. Using complementary parton constructions, we analyze doped $\mathbb{Z}_3$ quantum spin liquid and $SU(3)$-related chiral spin liquids. Doping a $\mathbb{Z}_3$ quantum spin liquid can produce an orthogonal metal with a gauge invariant fermi surface of charge-$3e$ fermionic trions. Pairing these trions gives a time-reversal-symmetric charge-$6e$ superconductor. Doping Abelian $SU(3)_1$ and $SU(6)_1$ chiral spin liquids yields chiral charge-$6e$ superconductors with and without residual Abelian topological order, respectively. Doping a non-Abelian $SU(3)_2$ chiral spin liquid leads to a non-Abelian chiral charge-$6e$ superconductor intertwined with $SO(3)_{-3}$ topological order and supporting non-Abelian $h/(6e)$ superconducting vortices. We also identify several other phases, including $\mathbb{Z}_3$ orthogonal metal, quantum anomalous Hall (crystal) phases enriched by $\mathbb{Z}_3$ or $\mathbb{Z}_2$ topological order, $SU(3)$-breaking charge-$2e$ superconductors, composite fermi liquid coupled to non-Abelian gauge field, and descendant chiral spin liquids. Our results identify doped $SU(3)$ spin liquids as a natural setting where symmetry, fractionalization, and topology cooperate to produce charge-$6e$ superconductivity.

</details>


### [133] [Superfluidity without charge order in the attractive Hubbard model on the kagome lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25983)
*Xiaodong Jin,Yingping Mou,Rubem Mondaini*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文运用辅助场量子蒙特卡洛方法研究了kagome晶格上零温吸引Hubbard模型的超导特性，发现2/3填充时存在属于手征XY普适类的超流相变，且超流态不伴随电荷序，即使在强耦合区域也是如此；1/3填充时电荷序仅在远大于费米子模型映射对应的相互作用强度下出现；对于存在van Hove奇异点的填充，任何吸引相互作用都会导致超流，且同样无电荷序，与平均场理论结论相反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究kagome晶格上吸引Hubbard模型在不同电子填充下的超导与电荷序行为，特别关注Dirac点、van Hove奇异点等能带特征对超流相变的影响，并检验平均场理论的可靠性。

Method: 采用辅助场量子蒙特卡洛方法对kagome晶格上的零温吸引Hubbard模型进行数值模拟分析。

Result: (1) 2/3填充时超流相变临界吸引相互作用U_c/t=-4.58(3)，属于手征XY普适类；超流态不伴随电荷序。(2) 1/3填充时电荷序仅在远大于映射对应的相互作用强度下出现。(3) van Hove奇异点填充下任何吸引相互作用均产生超流，且无电荷序。

Conclusion: Kagome晶格吸引Hubbard模型的超流相变具有丰富的填充依赖性，且超流态普遍不伴随电荷序，纠正了平均场理论关于电荷序的预测。

Abstract: Using auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo simulations, we study the zero-temperature attractive Hubbard model on the kagome lattice at various relevant electronic fillings. The low-energy physics at $2/3$-density is influenced by the Dirac point at the Fermi energy, wherein we unveil a superfluid transition at a critical attractive interaction $U_c/t=-4.58(3)$, which belongs to the chiral-XY universality class. This U(1) symmetry-breaking is not accompanied by charge order, even for substantially large interaction strengths, in a regime where a description in terms of hardcore bosons becomes increasingly suitable. An investigation of the latter shows that charge ordering at $1/3$-filling only occurs at interaction strengths much larger than those corresponding to the mapping to the original fermionic model. Additionally, for densities exhibiting van Hove singularities in the non-interacting density of states, our results show that any attractive interaction gives rise to superfluidity, yet again without charge ordering, contrary to recent studies employing mean-field theory.

</details>
